Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) have transformed multi-modal understanding, excelling in tasks like image captioning and visual question answering by integrating visual and textual inputs. However, their robustness against adversarial attacks, particularly those exploiting both modalities, remains underexplored, posing risks to critical applications like autonomous driving and content moderation. Existing attacks focus on single modalities or require impractical white-box access, limiting their real-world relevance. In this paper, we introduce Multi-Modal Adversarial Synergy, a groundbreaking framework that crafts universal, black-box multi-modal attacks against LVLMs. MMAS simultaneously generates a texture scale-constrained universal adversarial perturbation for images and a learnable prompt perturbation for text, optimized jointly using only model queries. The image perturbation leverages wavelet-based texture constraints to ensure imperceptibility and robustness across diverse visual inputs. The text perturbation, constrained by an L-norm in the embedding space, maintains semantic coherence while steering outputs toward a target. A novel cross-modal regularization term aligns the perturbations' gradient directions, enhancing their synergistic impact and transferability across tasks and models. Extensive experiments show the strong universal adversarial capabilities of our proposed attack with prevalent LVLMs.

</details>


### [38] [Re-M3Dr: Rebalanced MultiModal Mean Deviation Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26513)
*Haojie Yin,Chengcheng Feng,Tianyi Liu,Tianqi Zhang,Kaizhu Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的多模态回归框架Re-M3Dr，用于改善结合OCT和眼底摄影预测视野缺损的性能，通过自适应对比学习和锐度感知梯度调制解决了数据分布与模态学习冲突导致的训练不稳定问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于OCT和眼底摄影提供互补信息，结合二者应能提升平均偏差（MD）预测性能，但现有方法在多模态融合中表现不如单模态模型，因此需要探究其根本原因并提出改进方案。

Method: 提出了Re-M3Dr框架：1）采用基于自适应边界的监督对比学习增强单模态表征；2）引入锐度感知梯度调制来稳定联合优化过程。

Result: 在公共和私有临床数据集上的实验表明，相比当前最先进的多模态学习方法，Re-M3Dr平均MSE降低了29%。

Conclusion: Re-M3Dr有效解决了多模态医学图像融合中的优化失衡问题，在预测视觉场损失方面显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Mean Deviation (MD) is a critical metric for assessing visual field loss in ophthalmology. While previous work has focused solely on predicting MD from Optical Coherence Tomography (OCT), it is intuitive to assume that combining OCT with another imaging of fundus photography (FP) could improve performance, as two ophthalmic medical imaging provide complementary information. This is particularly expected when sophisticated multi-objective optimization is applied, as documented in common multimodal classification. Surprisingly, our investigations reveal that multimodal fusion in this medical imaging scenario performs worse than unimodal model. Through detailed analysis, we identify the root cause as a coupled imbalance between data distribution and modality learning conflict. This imbalance distorts the optimization landscape, leading to unstable training. To address this challenge, we propose the method of Rebalanced MultiModal Mean Deviation Regression (Re-M3Dr), a novel multimodal regression framework. We enhance unimodal representation through adaptive margin based supervised contrastive learning. Then, our framework stabilizes the joint optimization with the sharpness-aware gradient modulation. Experimental results on both public and private clinical datasets show average 29\% reduction in MSE compared to SOTA multimodal learning methods, demonstrating the superiority of Re-M3Dr. The code is available in the supplementary materials.

</details>


### [39] [CSV-ViT: A Vision Transformer with the Variable-sized Cortical Supervertices for Detection of Alzheimer's Disease Pathologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26514)
*Geonwoo Baek,Ikbeom Jang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于皮层表面分块的深度学习方法CSV-ViT，用于在PET或脑脊液检查前通过MRI预测阿尔茨海默病相关状态，具有更高的分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于脑皮层表面的深度学习模型在处理非欧几里得数据时存在边界重复、区域不敏感等问题，且依赖昂贵的PET检测，因此需要一种更精确、高效的MRI预筛方法。

Method: 提出皮层超级顶点（CSV）进行ROI保持、基于顶点、可变大小的分块划分，并设计了支持可变大小patch的CSV-ViT模型，采用掩码感知的嵌入机制。

Result: 在AD诊断、淀粉样蛋白阳性、tau蛋白阳性三类任务中，CSV-ViT均优于近期表面模型，表现出更高的分类性能。

Conclusion: CSV-ViT有望作为PET或CSF确认前的MRI辅助工具，提升阿尔茨海默病相关状态的预测能力。

Abstract: Confirming Alzheimer's disease (AD) typically relies on positron emission tomography (PET), which remains costly and invasive, motivating the use of structural MRI-based prescreening. Deep learning on non-Euclidean manifolds, particularly brain cortical surfaces, faces significant challenges due to the data's spherical topology. Recent surface models have enabled learning from cortical surface data; however, imposing face-based uniform patches often causes duplicate vertices at patch boundaries. In general, many surface-based models are limited in their awareness of the region of interest (ROI), which can result in non-cortical regions, such as the medial wall, being included. We propose a cortical surface tokenization that performs ROI-preserving, vertex-based, variable-sized patch partitioning. We refer to these cortical surface patches as cortical supervertices (CSVs). Building on this representation, we design the CSV Vision Transformer (CSV-ViT), a variable-size patch-tolerant Vision Transformer that uses padding and a mask-aware patch embedding. We used T1-weighted MRI and evaluated our framework by classifying AD-related status into three categories: AD diagnosis, amyloid positivity, and tau positivity. Across the experiments, CSV-ViT achieved higher classification performance than recent surface-based models. The results suggest that the proposed CSV-ViT may support MRI-based prediction of AD-related status prior to PET or CSF confirmation.

</details>


### [40] [$R^3$: 3D Reconstruction via Relative Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26519)
*Congrong Xu,Huachen Gao,Xingyu Chen,Yuliang Xiu,Jun Gao,Anpei Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为 $R^3$ 的相对回归方法，通过置信度加权的相对约束来解决现有前馈几何模型在长序列和流式重建中因全局坐标系假设导致的性能瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的前馈几何基础模型依赖于全局坐标系，这在处理长时间序列或流式数据时会导致无法控制的平移增长和对时间原点的过度依赖，限制了其扩展性和实用性。

Method: 提出相对回归策略 $R^3$，使用轻量级MLP预测置信度加权的相对约束；该置信度在训练时用于加权损失，在推理时指导姿态聚合，支持全上下文离线重建与因果性、有限内存的流式重建。

Result: $R^3$ 在离线和流式两种设置下的实验评估表明，其相对机制能有效提升长序列和流式场景中的深度与位姿恢复性能。

Conclusion: $R^3$ 通过摒弃全局坐标系依赖，引入基于置信度的相对回归框架，显著增强了模型在长期上下文和流式输入下的泛化能力与实用性。

Abstract: Recent feed-forward geometry foundation models have demonstrated impressive generalization by recovering depth and poses in a single forward pass. However, these models are typically constrained by a global coordinate frame assumption. This dependency becomes a significant bottleneck for long-context and streaming reconstruction, as it forces the network to maintain an arbitrary temporal origin and handle translation magnitudes that grow unbounded over time. Our solution, which we call $R^3$, employs relative regression. We employ a lightweight MLP to predict confidence-weighted relative constraints. These confidences serve as a unified anchor: weighting losses during training and guiding pose aggregation during inference. $R^3$ supports both full-context offline reconstruction and causal, bounded-memory streaming. Our evaluation in both offline and streaming settings validates the effectiveness of our relative mechanism. Project page: https://kevinxu02.github.io/r3-site

</details>


### [41] [InterSketch: An Interleaved Reasoning Model with Self-correcting Visual Sketch and Stepwise Reward](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26520)
*Zhiwei Ning,Wenwen Tong,Xiangli Kong,Shengnan Ma,Ziyi Shang,Jingcheng Ni,Tao Hu,Yong Xien Chng,Jixuan Ying,Zehuan Wu,Hanming Deng,Jie Yang,Yuanjie Zheng,Wei Liu,Lewei Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: InterSketch是一种增强视觉-语言模型中交错视觉-文本思维链（VT-CoT）能力的模型，通过自修正和逐步奖励机制实现长视野视觉推理，在多个基准上表现优于现有模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言模型的推理过程较浅且以文本为中心，难以应对复杂的视觉任务；而人类推理是视觉与文本交错的长程过程，因此需要更接近人类思维的模型。

Method: 提出InterSketch模型，动态生成中间视觉草图并与文本推理交错进行；首先构建高质量的交错VT-CoT数据集并引入反思机制实现自修正；随后在强化学习阶段设计逐步奖励机制，缓解长程推理中奖励信号稀疏的问题。

Result: 在多个视觉推理基准上的实验表明，InterSketch显著优于基线模型，甚至超过Gemini-3-Pro等专有模型。

Conclusion: InterSketch通过引入交错的视觉-文本推理、自修正机制和逐步奖励，有效提升了模型在复杂视觉任务中的长视野推理能力，推动VLM向更类人化的认知方式迈进。

Abstract: While vision-language models (VLMs) have exhibited multi-turn visual reasoning capabilities, their reasoning trajectories remain relatively shallow and are dominated by a text-centric paradigm, limiting their applicability to complex visual challenges. In contrast, human-like thought typically involves long-horizon reasoning with an interleaved visual-textual chain-of-thought (VT-CoT). To bridge this gap, we introduce InterSketch, an interleaved reasoning model to enhance the VT-CoT capability via self-correcting and stepwise reward mechanisms. InterSketch dynamically generates intermediate visual sketches using external tools and interleaves them with textual reasoning, enabling effective perception and logical reasoning over long-horizon visual understanding tasks. Specifically, in the first cold-start stage, we propose a synthesized high-quality interleaved VT-CoT dataset and include a reflection mechanism to enable the model's capability in multi-turn interleaved reasoning and self-correction. In the subsequent reinforcement learning (RL) stage, we design a stepwise reward mechanism to mitigate the sparsity of reward signals inherent in end-only supervision over long-horizon reasoning. Extensive experiments on visual reasoning benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of InterSketch, even outperforming proprietary models such as Gemini-3-Pro.

</details>


### [42] [CmIVTP: Cross-modal Interaction-based Vessel Trajectory Prediction for Maritime Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26524)
*Yuxu Lu,Dong Yang,Xiaoyu Li,Mengwei Bao,Congcong Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于跨模态交互的船舶轨迹预测框架CmIVTP，结合AIS和CCTV数据，提升复杂水域环境下轨迹预测的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单一数据源（如AIS或CCTV）在船舶轨迹预测中存在局限性，难以全面捕捉船舶动态与环境交互，需融合多模态数据以提高预测精度与鲁棒性。

Method: 设计了目标感知场景编码器提取环境语义特征，提出跨模态交互Transformer融合AIS运动特征、CCTV环境特征与场景表示，并利用跨模态注意力机制建模模态内语义与模态间交互；构建历史轨迹聚类的船舶群体轨迹库用于候选轨迹生成。

Result: 在自建的大规模多模态数据集Maritime-MmD+上验证了方法有效性，实验表明CmIVTP在多模态轨迹预测基准上性能优于现有方法。

Conclusion: CmIVTP通过有效融合AIS与CCTV多模态信息，实现了更准确、环境一致且动态合理的船舶轨迹预测，推动了海上智能交通系统的应用发展。

Abstract: Maritime intelligent transportation systems (MITS) are essential for ensuring navigation safety and efficiency in busy waterways. However, accurate vessel trajectory prediction remains challenging due to the limitations of single-source data. Automatic identification system (AIS) data is often sparse or unavailable for small vessels, while closed-circuit television (CCTV) data alone cannot fully capture dynamic vessel behavior. To mitigate these challenges, we propose a cross-modal interaction-based vessel trajectory prediction (named CmIVTP) framework to model the intricate interactions between vessel dynamics and environmental constraints. Specifically, we introduce a target-aware scene encoder to extract scene semantic features, effectively capturing vessel-environment interactions and enhancing trajectory prediction accuracy. In addition, we propose a cross-modal interaction transformer, which integrates AIS-derived motion features, CCTV-based environmental features, and scene representations. It leverages cross-modal attention mechanisms to simultaneously capture intra-modal semantics and inter-modal interactions, ensuring dynamically consistent and environmentally feasible predictions. Furthermore, we construct a vessel group trajectory bank by clustering historical AIS trajectories into representative motion patterns, providing an efficient and scalable approach for candidate trajectory generation. Additionally, we introduce the maritime multimodal dataset plus (named Maritime-MmD$^+$), a large-scale dataset that synchronizes AIS data and CCTV video data, providing robust support for multimodal trajectory prediction research. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CmIVTP achieves better performance on multimodal-driven vessel trajectory prediction benchmarks. The code resources for this work can be available at https://github.com/LouisYxLu/CmIVTP.

</details>


### [43] [ReCA: Multi-Shot Long Video Extrapolation via Recursive Context Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26525)
*Akide Liu,Jinbo Xing,Chaojie Mao,Ye Li,Zeyu Zhang,Yefei He,Weijie Wang,Zihan Wang,Yu Liu,Gholamreza Haffari,Bohan Zhuang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了多镜头视频外推（MSVE）任务，旨在生成分钟级、具有电影结构的视频序列，同时保持初始状态并推进叙事意图。为此，作者提出了一种递归上下文分配（ReCA）框架，在推理时分层分配上下文以解决全局规划、镜头提示和时间链中的瓶颈问题，并构建了适用于3到5分钟长视频评估的MSVE-Bench和NB-Q协议。实验表明，ReCA在一致性和评分上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视频生成方法要么局限于单镜头外推而缺乏叙事结构，要么进行多镜头故事生成却无法保持视觉一致性；同时，长视频生成失败的根本原因被归结为上下文分配不当而非单纯的上下文长度限制。因此需要一种既能保持锚定状态又能构建电影化结构的新范式。

Method: 提出Recursive Context Allocation（ReCA）框架：通过递归方式将MSVE分解为上下文受限的子问题，在叶节点调用冻结的生成器，并跨时间传播结构化状态更新，实现层次化的上下文分配。同时构建MSVE-Bench与NB-Q评估协议用于3-5分钟长视频生成评测。

Result: ReCA相比最强竞争方法平均标准化得分提升8%–16%，多镜头一致性指标提高28%–43%；验证了上下文分配比单纯扩展上下文长度更关键。

Conclusion: 长视频生成的关键挑战在于上下文的有效分配而非仅扩展长度；ReCA通过分层递归结构实现了在有限生成预算下的高质量、连贯性分钟级视频生成，为未来研究提供了新方向与基准。

Abstract: Minute-scale cinematic video generation is a central challenge for generative video models. Existing paradigms address only fragments of this challenge: single-shot extrapolation preserves an anchor but lacks cinematic structure, while multi-shot storytelling imposes structure yet remains free to invent its visual states rather than continue an observed one. We define Multi-Shot Video Extrapolation (MSVE), a task that extends an observed frame or clip into a sequence of cinematically structured shots while preserving anchor state and advancing narrative intent. This setting operates under the finite per-call generation budget of short-video models. We identify three coupled bottlenecks: (1) global planners over-specify unsupported details from full screenplays; (2) shot-level prompts dilute task-relevant state when carrying the complete story; and (3) temporal chaining turns generated frames into a lossy memory in which identity, scene, object, and action state decay. MSVE reveals that long-video failure is not merely a limitation of context length, but a failure of context allocation. We propose Recursive Context Allocation (ReCA), an inference-time framework that allocates context hierarchically across planning and generation. ReCA recursively decomposes MSVE into context-bounded subproblems, invokes frozen generators at leaf nodes, and propagates structured state updates across time. To evaluate this setting, we further propose MSVE-Bench and NB-Q, a source-grounded protocol with prompts purpose-built for 3 to 5 minute long-video generation, a regime not addressed by existing short-clip benchmarks. Compared to previous methods, ReCA improves average normalized score by 8 to 16 percent over the strongest competing controller and improves multi-shot consistency metrics by 28 to 43 percent. View the project page at https://reca.vmv.re.

</details>


### [44] [Scheduled Style Injection: Expanding the Style-Content Pareto Frontier in Training-Free Diffusion-based Style Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26538)
*Amey Sunil Kulkarni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过调度机制优化预训练扩散模型中风格注入的方法，发现减少型调度（在浅层和早期时间步注入更强的结构信号）能显著提升风格质量与内容保持之间的权衡，结合ControlNet条件控制可进一步扩展帕累托前沿，且所有改进无需训练或新增参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法如StyleID使用全局固定的风格注入强度，导致风格质量与内容保持之间的权衡过于僵化，缺乏灵活性，本文旨在探索更精细的控制维度以突破这一限制。

Method: 系统研究了四个控制维度：解码器各层、去噪时间步的风格注入强度变化，以及ControlNet几何条件在两轴上的调度策略；比较了不同调度形状（如余弦、平方根、线性）的影响，并分析其独立性与组合效果。

Result: 实验表明减少型调度在所有设置下均优于反向调度，余弦和平方根时间表优于线性；gamma调度与ControlNet条件几乎相互独立，组合后显著扩展帕累托前沿；最佳配置将ArtFID从28.801降至27.036，相对提升6.1%，结果在超过28,000张图像和四种指标上验证有效，并跨SD主干模型一致泛化。

Conclusion: 通过细粒度调度风格注入强度，可在不增加参数或训练成本的前提下显著改善风格迁移效果，揭示了调度设计在生成模型中的关键作用。

Abstract: Style transfer with pre-trained diffusion models has advanced rapidly, but a core question remains underexplored: where in the model should style injection be strongest? StyleID, the leading training-free method, uses a single global parameter (gamma) uniformly across all layers and timesteps, which forces a fixed tradeoff between style quality and content preservation. We show this tradeoff is unnecessarily rigid. We systematically explore four dimensions of control: varying style injection strength across decoder layers, across denoising timesteps, and scheduling ControlNet geometric conditioning along both axes. The pattern is consistent everywhere: decreasing schedules, with stronger structural signal injection in shallower layers and earlier timesteps, reliably outperform the reverse. Beyond direction, schedule shape matters: cosine and square-root timestep schedules outperform linear. Most importantly, we find that gamma scheduling and ControlNet conditioning are nearly independent. The resulting combined configurations expand the Pareto frontier, offering superior tradeoffs between style fidelity and content preservation compared to any single baseline setting. Our best balanced configuration achieves ArtFID of 27.036 versus StyleID's 28.801 - a 6.1% relative improvement, with consistent gains across the full style-content tradeoff frontier. Results are validated across 35 configurations totaling over 28,000 stylized images using four complementary metrics. These findings generalize across SD backbones with identical rank ordering. All modifications are training-free, parameter-free, and require only a few lines of scheduling code; code is available at https://github.com/ameyskulkarni/scheduled_style_injection.

</details>


### [45] [TrackRef3D: Multi-View Consistent Track-then-Label for Open-World Referring Segmentation in 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26576)
*Yuyang Tan,Renhe Zhang,Hang Zhang,Ao Li,Xin Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TrackRef3D，一种无需人工标注、实现多视角一致的开放世界3D指代表达分割方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖昂贵的手动标注和伪掩码生成，存在多视图不一致和泛化能力差的问题。

Method: 引入多视图一致的‘先跟踪后标注’范式，通过轨迹感知语义共识模块（TSCM）进行同义聚类和轨迹感知投票，并采用可见性感知描述生成与混合训练策略（HTS）联合优化粗粒度类别与细粒度指代线索。

Result: 在多个基准上实现了最先进的性能，有效提升了多视图一致性与对不同查询粒度的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: TrackRef3D实现了全自动、开放世界的3DGS中指代表达分割，解决了标注成本高、多视图不一致和泛化性差等关键问题。

Abstract: Referring 3D Gaussian Splatting (R3DGS), which utilizes natural language for 3D object segmentation, has emerged as a crucial capability for embodied AI. However, existing methods typically rely on expensive per-scene manual annotation and per-view pseudo mask generation, which suffer from multi-view inconsistency and poor generalization to varying query specificities. To address this, we present TrackRef3D, a fully automatic pipeline that achieves open-world referring segmentation in 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) without manual annotation by introducing a multi-view consistent track-then-label paradigm that fundamentally decouples object discovery from semantic grounding. Specifically, we propose a Trajectory-Aware Semantic Consensus Module (TSCM) which aggregates cross-view predictions via synonymous clustering and trajectory-aware voting to establish a canonical semantic identity, thereby ensuring multi-view consistency. Furthermore, we employ a visibility-aware description generation strategy to mitigate ambiguity and propose a Hybrid Training Strategy (HTS) that jointly optimizes coarse category semantics and fine-grained referential cues to ensure robustness under varying query specificities using a multi-positive contrastive objective. Extensive experiments on benchmarks demonstrate that TrackRef3D achieves state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [46] [O-MARC: Omni Memory-Augmented Compression Distillation for Efficient Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26584)
*Peiran Wu,Yunze Liu,Chi-Hao Wu,Chen Chen,Junxiao Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了UGC-AVQA基准和OMAC压缩方法，用于降低多模态大模型在用户生成视频上的推理成本，并通过O-MARC框架提升压缩输入下模型的鲁棒性，实验证明其在保持性能的同时显著提升了效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态基准未能充分评估音频视觉关联能力，且长序列联合推理导致计算开销大，尤其在用户生成内容（UGC）视频中更为突出。因此需要一个更有效的基准和推理压缩方法。

Method: 提出UGC-AVQA：包含1000个视频和4816个问答对的新基准，通过音频移除测试确保问题需结合音视频信息回答；设计OMAC：一种无需训练的推理压缩方法，保留关键视觉记忆和时序对齐的音频锚点；引入O-MARC：一种压缩蒸馏框架，使小型模型能在压缩的多模态上下文中有效学习。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-Omni-3B上，O-MARC在四个基准上的平均得分达到45.8，优于全token推理（44.1）和OmniZip（41.0）；OMAC将延迟降低34.6%（提速1.53倍），内存减少34.7%。

Conclusion: 所提方法在不牺牲性能的前提下显著提升多模态模型的推理效率，为处理长序列用户生成视频内容提供了高效可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Omnimodal large language models enable unified audio video understanding, but long joint token sequences make inference costly, and existing benchmarks do not fully isolate audio visual association in noisy user generated videos. We introduce UGC-AVQA, a public UGC benchmark with 1,000 videos and 4,816 QA pairs, where an audio removal test ensures that benchmark questions require both acoustic and visual evidence. To reduce inference cost, we propose OMAC, a training free plug in compression method that preserves salient visual memory and temporally grounded audio anchors. To further make compact models robust to compressed inputs, we introduce O-MARC, a compression distillation framework for learning with memory compressed multimodal contexts. On Qwen2.5-Omni-3B, O-MARC improves the average score across four benchmarks to 45.8, outperforming full token inference at 44.1 and OmniZip at 41.0. OMAC also keeps inference efficient, reducing latency by 34.6\% (1.53$\times$ speedup) and memory by 34.7\% compared with full token inference.

</details>


### [47] [FTibSuite: A Comprehensive Resource Suite for Tibetan Vision-Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26601)
*Guixian Xu,Yide Liang,Zeli Su,Xuexian Song,Ziyin Zhang,Yushuang Dong,Ting Zhang,Xu Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了FTibSuite，一个针对藏语视觉-语言研究的综合资源套件，包括训练数据、基准测试和基于Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct的可复现基线模型FTibVLM，显著提升了藏语多模态任务性能，同时保持了原有的中文能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 藏语作为一种低资源语言，在视觉-语言模型研究中长期被忽视，缺乏可复现的训练和评估基础设施，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 构建了FTibSuite，包含三部分：FTibData（涵盖持续预训练、图文对齐和指令调优的人工验证多模态训练语料）、FTibBench（五个主流多模态基准的藏语版本，采用分层质量控制减少翻译噪声）以及FTibVLM（基于Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct的三阶段适配基线模型）。

Result: 在FTibBench上的实验表明，FTibVLM在所有任务上均取得显著性能提升，例如MMBench准确率从42.97提升至67.78，POPE-random准确率从47.53提升至80.56，同时对原始模型的中文能力影响极小。

Conclusion: FTibSuite为藏语多模态研究提供了首个标准化基础，推动了低资源语言在视觉-语言领域的技术发展。

Abstract: Vision-language models have progressed rapidly, but Tibetan remains a severely underserved low-resource language due to the lack of reproducible training and evaluation infrastructure. To fill this gap, we introduce FTibSuite, a comprehensive resource suite for Tibetan vision-language research, consisting of FTibData (human-verified multimodal training corpora spanning continual pretraining, image-text alignment, and instruction tuning data), FTibBench (Tibetan adaptations of five mainstream multimodal benchmarks with a hierarchical quality-control workflow to reduce translation noise), and FTibVLM, a reproducible baseline built on Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct via a three-stage adaptation pipeline. Experiments on FTibBench show FTibVLM delivers consistent performance gains across all tasks, such as improving MMBench accuracy from 42.97 to 67.78 and POPE-random accuracy from 47.53 to 80.56, while retaining the backbone's original Chinese capabilities with minimal degradation, providing the first standardized foundation for Tibetan multimodal research.

</details>


### [48] [Gaussian-Voxel Duet: A Dual-Scaffolding Hybrid Representation for Fast and Accurate Monocular Surface Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26616)
*Zhenhua Du,Zhen Tan,Haoyu Zhang,Dewen Hu,Shuaifeng Zhi,Peidong Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合高斯点阵与稀疏体素骨架的混合表示方法（VoxelGS），通过将高斯元锚定在SDF引导的体素表面附近，显著提升了3D重建的几何精度和优化效率，在多个真实室内场景数据集上实现了最先进的表面重建与新视角合成效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D高斯点阵方法在追求高质量新视角合成时面临几何精度与优化效率之间的权衡：仅基于图像渲染的方法几何结构不佳，而引入神经SDF的方法则训练成本过高。

Method: 提出一种混合高斯-体素表示法，将高斯元锚定到联合优化的稀疏体素骨架上，并通过隐式表面牵引损失使高斯元靠近体素化SDF定义的表面，实现相互正则化。

Result: 在ScanNet++, ScanNetv2和DeepBlending数据集上实验表明，该方法在保持快速训练收敛和实时渲染的同时，取得了优于现有基线方法的表面重建质量和新视角合成效果。

Conclusion: VoxelGS有效平衡了高斯点阵在几何保真度与训练效率之间的矛盾，为高效高质量的3D场景重建提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: While 3D Gaussian Splatting has achieved remarkable success in photorealistic novel view synthesis, its pursuit of fast and high-fidelity 3D reconstruction has long been constrained by a trade-off between geometric accuracy and optimization efficiency. Methods specialized in image rendering converge quickly at the cost of imperfect geometry caused by superfluous primitives overfitting training views, while methods integrating neural signed-distance field (SDF) for better geometry incur prohibitive training costs. In this paper, we attempt to strike a better trade-off by tethering scaffold-anchored Gaussians to a jointly optimized sparse voxel scaffold. This hybrid Gaussian-Voxel representation explicitly confines anchored Gaussians to a narrow band around surfaces defined by voxelized SDFs, which effectively improves representation efficiency and condenses floating Gaussians without sacrificing geometry quality. An implicit surface tethering loss further pulls individual Gaussian primitives closer to SDF-induced surfaces in a mutually regularized manner for improved reconstruction accuracy. Extensive experiments on diverse real-world indoor scenes from ScanNet++, ScanNetv2, and DeepBlending datasets demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art surface reconstruction quality as well as superior novel view synthesis against leading baselines, while maintaining fast training convergence and real-time rendering. Code will be available at https://github.com/duzh11/VoxelGS.

</details>


### [49] [MedVol-R1: Reward-Driven Evidence Grounding for Volumetric Reasoning Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26621)
*Zichun Wang,Hairong Shi,Bingzheng Wei,Yan Xu,Zihua Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于强化学习的VRS框架MedVol-R1，通过将临床推理与三维分割解耦，利用2D证据锚点提升可解释性和泛化能力，并在多个医学数据集上实现了最先进的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖专用分割标记，导致决策过程不透明，限制了对多样化临床描述的泛化和解释性。因此需要一种更透明、可验证的分割机制。

Method: 提出MedVol-R1框架，使用LVLM定位关键轴向切片和2D边界框作为可验证的证据锚点，再由冻结的MedSAM2模块将其扩展为3D掩码；采用冷启动监督微调加GRPO强化学习训练，结合多组分奖励函数优化证据选择、2D定位和跨切片一致性。

Result: 在CT-ORG、AbdomenCT-1K和KiTS23数据集上均优于强基线模型，达到SOTA水平，且强化学习显著优于纯监督微调。

Conclusion: MedVol-R1通过解耦证据推理与体积分割，提升了模型的可解释性与泛化能力，为医学图像中自由形式语言查询的三维分割提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Volumetric Reasoning Segmentation (VRS) aims to segment a target region in a 3D medical scan from a free-form clinical query, where the referent is often implicit and requires both medical knowledge and volume-grounded reasoning. Existing methods typically rely on specialized segmentation tokens to connect language with mask decoding, but this coupling collapses the decision process into opaque latent representations, limiting interpretability and generalization to diverse narrative expressions. In this paper, we present MedVol-R1, a reinforcement learning-based framework for VRS that explicitly decouples evidence grounding from volumetric delineation: the LVLM grounds clinical reasoning to a verifiable 2D evidence anchor (key axial slice and 2D bounding boxes), which is then propagated into a coherent 3D mask by a frozen MedSAM2 module. We train MedVol-R1 with cold-start supervised fine-tuning followed by GRPO, guided by a multi-component reward that encourages informative evidence selection, accurate 2D spatial grounding, and cross-slice volumetric coherence, without requiring costly chain-of-thought annotations. Experiments on CT-ORG, AbdomenCT-1K, and KiTS23 from the M3D-Seg benchmark demonstrate that MedVol-R1 consistently outperforms strong baselines and achieves state-of-the-art performance, with reinforcement learning providing clear gains over pure supervised fine-tuning.

</details>


### [50] [DelowlightSplat: Feed-Forward Gaussian Splatting for Lowlight 3D Scene Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26629)
*Fuzhen Jiang,Zengtian Xie,Zhuoran Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对低光条件下的新颖视图合成与3D重建框架DelowlightSplat，通过构建可控的多视角低光基准和引入轻量级低光适配器，显著提升了在低光环境下的性能表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于噪声、颜色偏移和不可靠的对应关系，现有的前馈式3D高斯重建方法在低光条件下表现不佳，因此需要一种能够应对低光挑战的新方法。

Method: 提出DelowlightSplat框架，构建仅退化上下文视图而保持目标视图清晰的可控多视角低光基准，引入轻量级低光适配器进行残差增强以提高匹配性，并结合基于代价体的多视角推理直接预测干净的3D高斯分布。

Result: 实验表明，DelowlightSplat在低光条件下显著优于之前的前馈方法和两阶段流水线方法。

Conclusion: DelowlightSplat是一种有效的低光感知前馈高斯点阵化框架，能够在低光条件下实现高质量的新颖视图渲染和3D重建。

Abstract: Novel-view synthesis and 3D reconstruction from sparse posed images are central to robotics and AR/VR. Yet, feed-forward 3D Gaussian reconstruction fails under lowlight due to noise, color shifts, and unreliable correspondence. We propose DelowlightSplat, a lowlight-aware feed-forward Gaussian splatting framework for clean novel-view rendering. We build a controllable multi-view lowlight benchmark by degrading only context views while keeping target views clean. We introduce a lightweight Lowlight Adapter for residual enhancement to improve matchability, and couple it with cost-volume-based multi-view inference to directly predict clean 3D Gaussians. Experiments show that DelowlightSplat significantly outperforms previous feed-forward method and two-stage pipeline under lowlight conditions.

</details>


### [51] [JetViT: Efficient High-Resolution Vision Transformer with Post-Training Attention Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26636)
*Dongyun Zou,Zhuoyang Zhang,Junyu Chen,Wenkun He,Qinhe Peng,Hanrong Ye,Yao Lu,Hongxu Yin,Yu Wang,Song Han,Han Cai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: JetViT是一种新型混合架构视觉Transformer，通过后训练注意力搜索技术，在不损失精度的情况下显著提升高分辨率图像的推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在保持先进全注意力视觉模型精度的同时，提高其在高分辨率图像上的推理效率。

Method: 提出Post-Training Attention Search框架，将预训练的全注意力ViT转换为高效的混合注意力变体，通过优化线性注意力设计、组合窗口与线性注意力块，并保留关键的全注意力块来实现加速。

Result: 在NVIDIA H100 GPU上，JetViT相比DINOv3和DepthAnythingV2等模型，最高实现1.79倍吞吐量提升和44.81%的延迟降低，且无精度损失。

Conclusion: JetViT能够在不牺牲准确率的前提下，显著提升高分辨率场景下视觉Transformer的推理效率，具备良好的应用前景。

Abstract: We introduce JetViT, a novel family of hybrid-architecture Vision Transformer (ViT) models that match the accuracy of state-of-the-art full-attention vision foundation models while achieving substantially higher inference efficiency on high-resolution images. At the core of our approach is Post-Training Attention Search, a post-training acceleration framework that converts pre-trained full-attention ViTs into efficient hybrid-attention variants by identifying and replacing redundant full-attention blocks with linear or window-attention blocks. By inheriting the MLP and attention weights from the base model, Post-Training Attention Search efficiently explores the architectural design space through three key steps: (1) optimizing the linear-attention block design; (2) finding the best combination of linear-attention and window-attention blocks; and (3) identifying and preserving critical full-attention blocks. We evaluate JetViT on two representative high-resolution vision foundation models, DINOv3 and DepthAnythingV2. On the NVIDIA H100 GPU, JetViT achieves up to 1.79x higher throughput and up to 44.81% lower latency without sacrificing accuracy. We will release our code and accelerated ViT models soon.

</details>


### [52] [OmniRetriever: Any-to-Any Audio-Video-Text Retrieval via Fusion-as-Teacher Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26641)
*Yunze Liu,Chi-Hao Wu,Enmin Zhou,Junxiao Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为OmniRetriever-7B的多模态检索模型，通过“融合为教师”蒸馏方法和Tuple-InfoNCE损失函数，充分利用音频-视频-文本三模态联合嵌入信号，显著提升了零样本检索性能，在多个基准上超越了Gemini Embedding 2和现有开源方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态编码器在训练时未能有效利用三模态（音频、视频、文本）同时存在的联合嵌入信号，仅依赖成对的对比学习目标（如InfoNCE），限制了跨模态表示的学习效果。

Method: 提出“融合为教师”蒸馏方法：将停止梯度的融合嵌入作为单模态嵌入的教师信号；同时引入Tuple-InfoNCE项直接监督融合嵌入。基于此构建OmniRetriever-7B模型，并发布新的12向AVT检索基准OmniRetriever-Bench用于评估。

Result: 在六个零样本检索基准上，OmniRetriever-7B在Clotho和SoundDescs上比Gemini Embedding 2高13.3-18.0 R@1；在MSR-VTT和MSVD上达到当前最先进的开放视频-文本编码器水平；在新提出的OmniRetriever-Bench上，平均性能（AVG-all）达34.84，分别超越Gemini Embedding 2和此前最佳开源方法1.72和8.03。

Conclusion: 该研究有效利用三模态联合嵌入信号，通过新型蒸馏与损失函数设计，显著提升了统一多模态嵌入空间的质量，推动了开放音频-视频-文本检索系统的发展。

Abstract: Unified multimodal embedding spaces have become the standard interface for cross-modal retrieval and multimodal RAG, and recent audio-video-text (AVT) encoders extend this setting to three modalities. Such encoders can produce a joint (T,V,A) embedding whenever all three modalities are available, but standard pairwise InfoNCE objectives leave this signal unused during training. We close this gap with fusion-as-teacher distillation, which treats a stop-gradient copy of the fused embedding as a teacher signal for the single-modal embeddings, paired with a Tuple-InfoNCE term that supervises the fused embedding directly. We instantiate this objective as OmniRetriever-7B. Across six zero-shot retrieval benchmarks, OmniRetriever-7B surpasses the closed-source Gemini Embedding 2 by 13.3-18.0 R@1 on Clotho and SoundDescs, and reaches the contemporary zero-shot specialist band of open video-text encoders on MSR-VTT and MSVD. To stress-test joint representations, we further release OmniRetriever-Bench, a 12-direction AVT retrieval benchmark totaling 3782 triples; on it OmniRetriever-7B attains AVG-all 34.84, improving over Gemini Embedding 2 by 1.72 and over the best prior open-source AVT method by 8.03.

</details>


### [53] [DV-SFT: Direct Vision Supervision for Fine-Grained Visual Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26656)
*Jianfei Zhao,Feng Zhang,Xin Sun,Chong Feng,Bing Wang,Zhixing Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为DV-SFT的直接视觉监督微调方法，通过在OCR相关场景中构建视觉令牌的显式、逐令牌监督信号，提升多模态大语言模型的细粒度视觉理解与多模态对齐效率，且无需修改模型结构或增加计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态大语言模型通常仅对文本标记施加监督信号，视觉标记只能被间接优化，导致视觉理解粗糙；此前尝试引入视觉监督的方法依赖额外组件，实用性受限。

Method: 利用OCR场景中图像区域与文本的直接对应关系，为每个视觉标记自动标注其对应图像块中的词语，并通过与文本相同的下一词预测目标进行训练，实现对视觉标记的直接监督。该方法将MLLM视为黑箱，无需修改架构或额外前向传播。

Result: 在三个领域内和四个领域外基准测试中，DV-SFT consistently 优于标准SFT；分析表明该方法提升了细粒度视觉理解能力和多模态对齐效率。

Conclusion: 直接对视觉标记施加监督信号是有效且高效的，DV-SFT为多模态大语言模型的视觉理解提供了简单、通用且高性能的微调范式。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models are typically trained end-to-end to predict ground-truth answers, yet supervision signals are applied exclusively to text tokens. Visual tokens, the core carriers of visual information, are optimized only implicitly as part of the context, leading to coarse-grained visual understanding. Prior works attempt to supervise visual inputs but inevitably rely on auxiliary components such as additional decoders or forward passes, because visual tokens lack readily interpretable labels. This limits their practical applicability. In this work, we propose \textbf{D}irect \textbf{V}ision \textbf{S}upervised \textbf{F}ine-\textbf{T}uning (DV-SFT), which constructs explicit, token-level supervision for visual tokens and trains them through the same next-token prediction objective used for text. Specifically, we exploit the direct vision--text correspondence in OCR-related scenarios and automatically label each visual token with the word in its corresponding image patch. DV-SFT treats the MLLM as a black box, requiring no architectural modifications or additional forward passes. Extensive experiments demonstrate the superiority of direct vision supervision. DV-SFT consistently outperforms standard SFT across three in-domain and four out-of-domain benchmarks. Further analyses show that vision supervision effectively enhances fine-grained visual understanding and achieves higher multimodal alignment efficiency.

</details>


### [54] [Respecting Modality Gap in Post-hoc Out-of-distribution Detection with Pre-trained Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26661)
*Yuanwei Hu,Bo Peng,Yadan Luo,Zhen Fang,Ling Chen,Jie Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的在线伪监督框架，用于零样本分布外检测，通过在视觉特征空间中学习类别原型来缩小文本与视觉模态之间的固有差距，并在多种OOD检测场景下实现了最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于预训练视觉-语言模型的零样本分布外检测方法通常将类别名称的文本嵌入作为类别原型，但这种文本即原型范式存在模态间不匹配的问题，限制了检测性能。

Method: 提出一种在线伪监督框架，利用测试阶段无标签数据流和预训练VLM的软预测，在视觉特征空间中直接学习类别原型，并提供在线优化过程的收敛性理论保证。

Result: 在多个OOD检测设置上进行了广泛实验，结果表明该方法显著优于现有方法，达到了新的最先进水平。

Conclusion: 文本嵌入作为原型存在内在局限性，通过在视觉空间中动态学习原型可有效缩小模态差距，提升零样本分布外检测性能。

Abstract: Out-of-distribution (OOD) detection has emerged as a popular technique to enhance the reliability of machine learning models by identifying unexpected inputs from unknown classes. Recent progress in pre-trained vision-language models (VLMs) has enabled zero-shot OOD detection without access to in-distribution (ID) training data; in this setting, existing methods commonly treat text embeddings of class names as class prototypes. In this paper, we challenge the widely adopted text-as-prototype paradigm by theoretically showing that off-the-shelf textual prototypes are generally misaligned with the optimal visual prototypes, yielding an intrinsic modality gap that cannot be eliminated by prompt engineering alone. To mitigate this gap under the post-hoc constraint, this paper presents an online pseudo-supervised framework that directly learns class prototypes in the visual feature space using unlabeled test-time data streams and soft predictions from the pre-trained VLMs. We provide theoretical guarantees for the convergence of the online optimization procedure. Extensive experiments empirically demonstrate that our method achieves a new state of the art across a variety of OOD detection setups.

</details>


### [55] [DynFrame: Adaptive Reasoning-Driven Multimodal Framework with Dynamic Frame Augmentation for Complex Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26680)
*Peng Zhang,Guanghao Zhang,Wanggui He,Longxiang Zhang,Mushui Liu,Yan Xia,Zhenhao Peng,Weilong Dai,Jinlong Liu,Haobing Tang,Le Zhang,Hao Jiang,Pipei Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DynFrame框架，通过可学习的时间窗口和采样密度选择，在单次自回归过程中实现多粒度视频证据检索，并结合SD-GRPO方法进行角色解耦的强化学习优化，在多个视频理解任务上取得领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频多模态大模型在推理时存在两个问题：一是采样密度不可学习且固定，导致需要多次检索以获取细粒度信息；二是检索与回答生成共享相同的奖励信号，无法区分‘看哪里’和‘如何答’的贡献。

Method: 提出DynFrame框架，将时间窗口和采样密度作为可学习的token嵌入到自回归过程中，实现一次检索获取多粒度证据；并设计Segment-Decoupled GRPO（SD-GRPO），在检索边界处分割轨迹，为不同角色的token分配独立的优势函数。

Result: DynFrame-4B在六个基准测试（NExT-GQA、Charades-STA、ActivityNet-MR、Video-MME、MLVU、LVBench）上表现优于7B-8B的强基线模型，DynFrame-8B在多数指标上达到新的SOTA。

Conclusion: DynFrame通过可学习的时空检索机制和解耦的强化学习策略，有效提升了视频多模态大模型的推理效率与准确性，为‘thinking-with-video’系统提供了新范式。

Abstract: Recent video multimodal large language models (MLLMs) increasingly couple step-by-step reasoning with on-demand visual evidence retrieval, allowing models to revisit relevant video segments during inference. However, two structural gaps remain in existing thinking-with-video systems. (i) Sampling density is not a learnable decision: existing methods may let the model decide where to look, but the per-window frame rate is largely fixed. As a result, fine-grained evidence is often recovered through repeated retrieval calls, which increases inference context length and training difficulty. (ii) Retrieval and answer generation are usually optimized with a single trajectory-level advantage, so the "where to look" tokens and the "how to answer" tokens receive the same credit even when one is correct and the other is not. To address these gaps, we present DynFrame, a framework that emits the temporal window and the sampling density as native tokens within a single autoregressive pass. This learnable span-density retrieval enables acquiring multi-granularity evidence with a single retrieval step. Based on the above tokenized retrieval interface, we further introduce Segment-Decoupled GRPO (SD-GRPO), which splits each rollout at the retrieval boundary and assigns role-specific token-level advantages, separately crediting the sampling decision and the answer. Trained on the curated DM-CoT-74k and DM-RL-45k, DynFrame-4B is competitive with strong 7B-8B baselines across six benchmarks (NExT-GQA, Charades-STA, ActivityNet-MR, Video-MME, MLVU, LVBench), and DynFrame-8B sets new state-of-the-art on most metrics. Code is available at https://github.com/zhangguanghao523/DynFrame.

</details>


### [56] [PinPoint: Prompting with Informative Interior Points](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26689)
*Pouya Sadeghi,Shawn He,Pedro Pablo Guerrero Vela,C. Thomas,Alex Wong,Sirisha Rambhatla*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为PinPoint的无训练、确定性的点选择方法，通过融合多种视觉线索来生成更优的内部提示点，显著提升了基于VLM和SAM的指代表像分割性能，在无需任务特定训练的情况下匹配了有监督和强化学习调优方法的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有指代表像分割方法中，使用VLM生成边界框后接SAM进行分割的效果受限，主要瓶颈在于提示信息的歧义性，尤其是内部点采样不理想导致性能下降。作者旨在不依赖额外训练的前提下，解决由随机采样引起的点质量差的问题。

Method: 提出PinPoint方法，融合四种视觉线索（如边缘、显著性等）构建共识图，选择远离边界、紧凑且空间分布多样的内部点，并利用冻结的VLM对每个点打标签，仅用两次VLM调用即可完成选择，整个过程无需训练。

Result: 在RefCOCO/+/g数据集上，相较于朴素采样方式，使用PinPoint在相同五点预算下cIoU提升12-18个点；固定所有模型组件时，仅用两个VLM调用即达到甚至超过需训练或强化学习调优的方法性能。

Conclusion: 提示歧义是当前无训练指代表像分割性能瓶颈的主因，而高质量的内部点选择可有效缓解该问题；PinPoint证明了无需专门训练也能实现高性能，为构建高效、轻量化的视觉语言系统提供了新思路。

Abstract: Modern referring image segmentation pipelines couple a vision-language model (VLM) for grounding with a promptable segmenter such as the Segment Anything Model (SAM) for mask generation. Prior training-free instances of this recipe consistently trail fine-tuned and reinforcement-learning (RL)-tuned specialists, and it has been unclear whether the gap comes from the VLM's grounding, SAM's capacity, or the prompt. We show that the gap is dominated by prompt ambiguity: a VLM-proposed bounding box (bbox) leaves SAM to guess which pixels inside the bbox belong to the object the expression denotes. Interior points are the natural disambiguator, but where they fall matters; prior work relies on naively sampled points that land on boundaries, distractors, and background clutter, and can even hurt performance compared to the bbox alone. Supervised and RL-tuned methods close this gap by training a VLM to predict better points; we show that this training is unnecessary. At a matched budget of five interior points, replacing naive sampling with stable, informative point selection improves cumulative Intersection-over-Union (cIoU) by 12-18 points across RefCOCO/+/g, with every model fixed. We turn this observation into PinPoint, a deterministic, training-free point selector that fuses four visual cues into a consensus map, selects compact, spatially diverse points away from boundaries, and uses the frozen VLM to label each point. Without any task-specific training, PinPoint matches supervised and RL-tuned specialists on the same stack while issuing only two VLM calls per query.

</details>


### [57] [Rotation-Invariant Spherical Watermarking via Third-Order SO(3) Representation Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26702)
*Pengzhen Chen,Yanwei Liu,Xiaoyan Gu,Antonios Argyriou,Wu Liu,Weiping Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于球谐系数高阶不变量构造的全景图像水印方法，利用SO(3)表示理论构建严格旋转不变的双谱特征，在保持高视觉质量的同时实现对任意3D旋转的鲁棒水印提取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于全景图在球面上定义并受SO(3)群作用，传统的平面表示和数据增强策略难以保证水印对任意3D旋转的可靠性，因此需要一种具有理论保障的旋转不变水印方法。

Method: 将全景图建模为球面信号，利用SO(3)群表示理论，通过张量积耦合高阶不可约表示并投影到平凡表示，构建出保留相位信息的三阶不变双谱（spherical invariant bispectrum），并将水印嵌入高阶球谐系数中，通过不变双谱标量进行恢复。

Result: 所提方法在理论上证明了SO(3)不变性，并在实验中展现出对连续3D旋转的近乎完美的鲁棒性，同时保持高视觉保真度。

Conclusion: 该工作为全景图像水印提供了首个具有严格理论保证的旋转不变解决方案，通过高阶不变量设计有效平衡了嵌入容量与变换鲁棒性之间的矛盾。

Abstract: Reliable watermarking of panoramic imagery is fundamentally challenged by arbitrary 3D rotations. As panoramas are defined on the sphere, they naturally transform under the action of $SO(3)$, rendering conventional planar representations and augmentation-based robustness strategies inadequate and devoid of theoretical guarantees. To address this, we formulate panoramas as spherical signals and leverage $SO(3)$ representation theory to derive provably rotation-invariant descriptors. While spherical harmonic coefficients transform equivariantly under rotations, the natural invariant constructions are typically limited to zeroth-order statistics which eliminate directional information and severely constrain embedding capacity. In this work, we introduce a principled third-order invariant construction by coupling higher-order $SO(3)$ irreducible representations via tensor products and projecting onto the trivial representation. This yields a spherical invariant bispectrum that preserves phase information while remaining strictly rotation-invariant. Leveraging this property, we embed watermarks into higher-order spherical harmonic coefficients and recover them from invariant bispectral scalars, enabling reliable extraction under arbitrary 3D rotations. We provide a theoretical proof of $SO(3)$ invariance for it and demonstrate experimentally its near-perfect robustness to continuous rotations while maintaining high visual fidelity.

</details>


### [58] [METATR: A Multilingual, Evolving Benchmark for Automatic Text Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26712)
*Mélodie Boillet,Solène Tarride,Christopher Kermorvant*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了METATR（v1.0），一个用于评估自动文本识别（ATR）模型的多语言、动态演进基准，旨在提升模型在真实世界多样化文档上的可比性与适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ATR系统多在现代英文印刷文本上评估，缺乏对多语言、多布局和手写文本的覆盖，难以反映实际应用场景需求。

Method: 构建包含29种语言、多种文字和版式的真实文档数据集，整合公共资源；提出标准化提示与归一化方法，并建立动态可扩展的评估框架。

Result: 评估了多个开源与闭源先进模型，发现专有模型整体表现更稳定，但在不同文字和版式上仍存在显著性能差异，且框架能有效衡量模型在语言、鲁棒性和计算效率等方面的多维表现。

Conclusion: METATR提供了一个面向实践者、多维度、可演进的评估体系，有助于在真实条件下评估多语言ATR模型并推动领域发展。

Abstract: Benchmarks that reflect the diversity and complexity of real-world documents are essential for accurately evaluating Automatic Text Recognition (ATR) systems, especially Vision-Large Language Models (vLLMs). Although recent models demonstrate impressive performance, they are often evaluated on datasets containing modern, printed texts mostly written in English, which limits their relevance to many practical applications. Therefore, selecting a model for a specific use case requires evaluating it on data that matches the target documents. This highlights the importance of representative benchmarks for real-world applications. In this paper, we introduce METATR (v1.0), a multilingual, evolving benchmark designed to evaluate ATR models across a wide range of documents, facilitating meaningful model comparison and selection. The benchmark was designed to maximize diversity by including documents from various public collections. These documents cover 29 languages and include texts with multiple scripts and layouts. Beyond the dataset itself, METATR defines a standardized prompting and normalization methodology and establishes a dynamic evaluation framework. This approach is intended to produce reproducible results while remaining extensible over time. We evaluated a wide range of state-of-the-art systems, including open-source models and closed-source models. Results are reported across various dimensions, including performance at the dataset and language levels, robustness to handwritten documents, and computational efficiency. Our findings show that, although proprietary models achieve the most consistent performance, substantial variability persists across scripts and layouts. Overall, METATR provides a multidimensional, practitioner-oriented framework for assessing multilingual ATR in real-world conditions and tracking progress as the field evolves.

</details>


### [59] [Joint 2D-3D Segmentation and Association in Street-level Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26725)
*Amir Melnikov,Masayuki Tanaka,Yusuke Monno,Masatoshi Okutomi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合2D语义与3D几何推理的统一框架，用于街景图像的联合2D-3D分割与关联，显著提升了城市级空间数字孪生环境中的对象覆盖与身份保持能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法依赖时序帧进行跟踪，在宽基线视角和变化成像条件下难以稳定关联对象，限制了大规模城市映射与空间数字孪生构建的精度与覆盖范围。

Method: 采用零样本检测与分割结合运动恢复结构（SfM）重建，通过3D驱动的关联机制利用几何一致性实现跨视图的身份保持，融合2D纹理线索与全局3D上下文信息。

Result: 相比最先进的纯2D跟踪方法，在挑战性城市场景中实现了22%的性能提升，显著增强了真值序列的覆盖范围和身份保留的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该框架有效克服了传统2D跟踪在复杂城市环境中的局限性，为大规模、多类型对象的街景解析提供了可扩展且稳健的解决方案。

Abstract: Accurate interpretation of street-level imagery is essential for large-scale urban mapping and the creation of Spatial Digital Twin (SDT) environments. This work presents a unified framework for joint 2D-3D segmentation and association that integrates visual semantics with multi-view geometric reasoning. Unlike conventional approaches that rely heavily on sequential frames for temporal tracking, our method leverages zero-shot detection and segmentation together with structure-from-motion reconstruction to establish stable cross-view correspondences. A 3D-driven association mechanism replaces traditional 2D multi-object tracking, using geometric consistency to guide identity preservation across wide-baseline viewpoints and varying imaging conditions. By combining 2D texture cues with global 3D context, the proposed pipeline is well-suited for scalable street-level processing and can be used for a variety of object types. Experiments demonstrate substantially improved coverage of ground-truth sequences and more robust identity retention compared to state-of-the-art 2D-only tracking methods, achieving a 22% performance gain in challenging urban scenarios.

</details>


### [60] [CIRCLED: A Multi-turn CIR Dataset with Consistent Dialogues across Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26734)
*Tomohisa Takeda,Yu-Chieh Lin,Yuji Nozawa,Youyang Ng,Osamu Torii,Yusuke Matsui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了CIRCLED数据集，通过扩展现有数据集并引入多轮对话一致性，解决了多轮组合图像检索（MTCIR）中对话历史不一致和局限于时尚领域的问题，构建了更大规模、更具通用性的基准数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MTCIR数据集缺乏对话历史一致性且局限于时尚领域，限制了多轮组合图像检索的研究发展。

Method: 基于CIReVL检索管道生成数据，并通过检索成功率、回合长度、一致性和信息冗余等多个过滤器对FashionIQ、CIRR和CIRCO进行扩展和筛选，构建CIRCLED数据集。

Result: 共收集22,608个多轮会话，涵盖九个子集，在规模和通用性上显著超过Multi-turn FashionIQ（11,505个会话）。

Conclusion: CIRCLED为多轮组合图像检索提供了高质量、实用的基准，有助于推动该领域的未来研究。

Abstract: Existing Multi-Turn Composed Image Retrieval (MTCIR) datasets lack dialogue-history consistency and are restricted to the fashion domain. To address these limitations, we construct CIRCLED by extending FashionIQ, CIRR, and CIRCO. In CIRCLED, the query at each turn progressively approaches the target image. Data are generated via a CIReVL-based retrieval pipeline and curated with multiple filters on retrieval success, turn length, consistency, and information redundancy to ensure quality. In total, we collect 22,608 multi-turn sessions across nine subsets, substantially exceeding Multi-turn FashionIQ (11,505 sessions) in both scale and generality. We further apply multiple baseline methods and quantitatively assess retrieval accuracy on CIRCLED. Our work provides a practical, high-quality benchmark to facilitate future research on multi-turn CIR. The dataset and code are publicly available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/tk1441/CIRCLED and https://github.com/mti-lab/circled.

</details>


### [61] [Once-For-All: A Train-Once and Select-Anytime Framework for Multimodal Instruction Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26761)
*Mingkang Dong,Hongyi Cai,Xiwen Lei,Jie Li,Tao Zhang,Muxin Pu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OFA提出了一种可重用的数据选择框架，通过在冻结的CLIP空间中聚类多模态指令并训练轻量级选择器，实现跨数据集和模型规模的高效迁移，显著提升视觉语言模型训练效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态指令数据选择方法依赖特定模型或数据集，每次变更都需要重新计算选择标准，成本高昂且缺乏泛化能力。

Method: OFA在冻结的CLIP空间中对多模态指令进行聚类，利用聚类结构生成伪标签，并仅用少量epoch训练一个轻量级选择器；选择器最不确定的样本被视为最具信息量。该选择器只需训练一次，即可跨数据集和模型直接应用。

Result: 在LLaVA-665K上训练的选择器应用于自身和未见过的Vision-Flan-186K数据集，仅选15%数据即达到全数据98.3%性能；在Vision-Flan-186K上甚至超越全数据训练10.6%。相同子集在Qwen2.5-VL-3B和LLaVA-v1.5-7B模型上均有效，无需针对目标模型重新计算。

Conclusion: 单一、可迁移的选择器能有效解耦数据选择与目标模型，为高效的多模态指令调优提供可复用的解决方案。

Abstract: Multimodal instruction tuning is the de facto recipe for adapting vision language models (VLMs), yet instruction data are highly redundant, making data selection critical for training efficiency. Existing methods derive selection signals from a specific model or dataset, so whenever the target model or candidate pool changes, the criteria must be recomputed from scratch at substantial cost. To address this, we propose OFA, a data selection framework that trains a reusable selector once and applies it to any dataset or model without recomputation. OFA clusters multimodal instructions in a frozen CLIP space, derives pseudo labels from the cluster structure, and trains a lightweight selector for only a few epochs; samples on which this selector is least confident are selected as the most informative. Once trained, the frozen selector transfers directly across datasets and model scales. The selector is trained once on LLaVA-665K and applied both to LLaVA-665K itself and, without any retraining, to the unseen Vision-Flan-186K. Selecting only 15% of the data, OFA achieves 98.3% of full data performance across 10 downstream benchmarks; on the smaller Vision-Flan-186K, the transferred selector surpasses full data training by 10.6%, confirming that the learned signal generalizes to datasets never seen during selector training. The same selected subsets benefit VLMs at both Qwen2.5-VL-3B and LLaVA-v1.5-7B without per model recomputation, decoupling selection from the target model. These results demonstrate that a single, transferable selector provides an effective and reusable solution for efficient multimodal instruction tuning.

</details>


### [62] [Receipt Replay OOD: A Small Benchmark for Screen Replay Detection Under Domain Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26855)
*Alexander Vinogradov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个名为Receipt Replay OOD的小型跨域基准数据集，用于屏幕重放攻击检测，评估了文档重放检测模型在域偏移下的泛化性能，并强调了现有方法在现实场景中鲁棒性的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的身份文件防伪检测研究多依赖于包含敏感信息的数据集，且对跨域鲁棒性的评估不足，尤其在真实域偏移场景下缺乏充分验证。

Method: 构建了一个新的跨域基准数据集Receipt Replay OOD，利用收据图像模拟身份文件的视觉特征（如平面结构、圆角、磨损痕迹等），同时避免个人身份信息问题，并在该数据集上评估现有文档重放检测模型的跨域性能。

Result: 实验表明，当前模型在跨域条件下性能显著下降，说明域偏移对泛化能力有重大影响，凸显出现实场景中OoD鲁棒性的重要性。

Conclusion: Receipt Replay OOD为研究文档防伪技术的跨域鲁棒性提供了一个实用且合规的新基准，推动更稳健的屏幕重放攻击检测方法的发展。

Abstract: Public datasets such as DLC-2021, SynID, and KID34K have significantly contributed to research on presentation attack detection for identity documents, including screen replay attacks. However, evaluation of out-of-domain (OOD) robustness remains insufficiently explored, especially under realistic domain shifts. In this work, we introduce Receipt Replay OOD, a small out-of-domain benchmark for screen replay detection. Receipts share several characteristics with identity documents, including planar geometry, curved corners, wear-and-tear artifacts, and text or logo patterns, while avoiding personally identifiable information constraints commonly associated with identity documents. We evaluate document replay detection models under cross-domain conditions and demonstrate the impact of domain shift on generalization performance. The dataset is publicly available.

</details>


### [63] [Natural Human Motion Recovery by Aligning High-Order Temporal Dynamics from Monocular Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26879)
*Dingkun Wei,Zehong Shen,Yan Xia,Georgios Pavlakos,Yujun Shen,Xiaowei Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出HTD-Refine，一种利用显式高阶时序动力学（速度和加速度）优化单目视频人体运动恢复的后处理框架，显著提升了运动的自然性和轨迹精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单目视频人体运动恢复方法常因缺乏可靠的速度和加速度等高阶时序信息，导致结果过于平滑或动态不一致，影响真实感。

Method: 提出PVA-Net，一种时序Transformer模型，直接从单目视频中预测每关节的2D位置、3D速度和3D加速度，并将这些动力学信息作为软约束融入全局优化过程，以优化世界空间中的运动轨迹。

Result: 在多个具有挑战性的野外基准上实验表明，HTD-Refine能持续提升当前最先进的人体运动恢复方法，显著减少抖动和过平滑现象，恢复出更符合物理规律且更自然的运动动态。

Conclusion: 高阶时序建模对提升单目人体运动恢复的质量至关重要，HTD-Refine通过引入显式动力学约束为现有方法提供了有效的后处理增强方案。

Abstract: Human motion recovered from monocular videos often appears overly smooth or dynamically inconsistent, even when joint positions are numerically accurate. We observe that this limitation stems from the absence of reliable high-order temporal cues -- velocity and acceleration -- which are essential for reconstructing motion that exhibits realistic momentum, timing, and high-frequency detail. We introduce HTD-Refine, a post-processing framework that augments existing Human Motion Recovery (HMR) pipelines using explicitly estimated high-order temporal dynamics. At the core of our system is PVA-Net, a temporal transformer that infers per-joint 2D positions, 3D velocities, and 3D accelerations directly from a monocular video. These predicted dynamics serve as soft yet informative constraints in a global optimization procedure that refines world-space trajectories, significantly reducing jitter, suppressing over-smoothing, and restoring physically plausible motion. Extensive experiments on challenging in-the-wild benchmarks show that HTD-Refine consistently improves state-of-the-art HMR methods, yielding more accurate global trajectories and substantially more natural motion dynamics. Our results highlight the critical role of high-order temporal modeling in advancing monocular human motion recovery.

</details>


### [64] [Small Object Detection in Industrial Recycling: A New Dataset and YOLO Performance Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26884)
*Oussama Messai,Abbass Zein-Eddine,Abdelouahid Bentamou,Mickael Picq,Nicolas Duquesne,Stéphane Puydarrieux,Yann Gavet*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文综述了基于深度学习的监督方法在小尺寸、密集且重叠物体检测中的应用，提出一个包含10000多张图像和12万个实例的新数据集，并对现有系统在工业回收场景下的性能、精度和计算效率进行了比较分析。研究还探讨了数据增强与合成图像的优势，提出了未来可能的研究方向，并开发了对分辨率和缩放变化鲁棒的异常检测策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小、密且重叠的物体检测是计算机视觉中的难题，尤其在工业回收过程中具有重要应用价值。现有方法在该类复杂场景下表现有限，亟需系统性评估与改进。

Method: 采用深度学习监督方法，构建大规模新数据集（>10k图像，120k实例），对多种目标检测系统进行对比实验；结合数据增强与合成图像技术提升模型性能；设计适用于回收场景的目标检测、长度测量与异常检测流程。

Result: 识别出当前最可靠的检测系统，验证了数据增强与合成图像的有效性；提出的异常检测方法在不同分辨率和缩放水平下均表现出良好鲁棒性；开源了数据集、方法与评估代码。

Conclusion: 本研究为小、密且重叠物体检测提供了基准评估与实用解决方案，推动了其在工业回收中的应用，并指明了未来优化方向，如进一步利用合成数据与自适应检测机制。

Abstract: In this paper, we address the problem of detecting small, dense, and overlapping objects, a major challenge in computer vision. Our focus is on reviewing proposed methods based on deep learning supervised approaches. We provide a detailed comparison of these systems on a new dataset of more than 10k images and 120k instances, highlighting their performance, accuracy, and computational efficiency in the industrial recycling process use case. Through this comparative analysis, we identify the most reliable systems currently available and the specific challenges they are designed to tackle. Furthermore, we explore the benefits of data augmentation and synthetic images. Based on our analysis, we also propose potential future directions and innovative solutions that could enhance the effectiveness of small, dense and overlapped object detection systems. The scope of our investigations encompasses object detection, length measurement, and anomaly detection within the context of the recycling process. The anomaly detection strategy is robust against variations in image resolution and zoom levels, ensuring reliable performance in industrial applications. The repository of the proposed dataset, methods and evaluation codes can be found at: https://github.com/o-messai/SDOOD

</details>


### [65] [SIMPC: Learning Self-Induced Mirror-Point Consistency for Unsupervised Point Cloud Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26894)
*Chengwei Zhang,Xueyi Zhang,Tao Jiang,Xinhao Xu,Wenjie Li,Fubo Zhang,Longyong Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为Self-Induced Mirror-Point Consistency (SIMPC)的方法，用于在无监督情况下学习点云中噪声点与潜在表面之间的确定性对应关系，通过生成镜像点并保持去噪目标的一致性来精确定位潜在表面对应位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 点云中的噪声会干扰点的坐标，使得构建一对一对应关系比图像更困难，现有方法因依赖统计映射而存在对应模糊的问题。

Method: 对于每个噪声点，SIMPC利用几何先验在潜在表面的另一侧生成一个镜像点，并在去噪过程中鼓励原始点与其镜像点的去噪目标之间保持一致性，从而学习确定性对应关系。

Result: 在合成和真实世界数据集上的大量实验表明，SIMPC显著优于现有的无监督方法，并超过多个强监督方法。

Conclusion: SIMPC通过自诱导的镜像点一致性有效解决了点云去噪中的对应模糊问题，实现了高精度的潜在表面对应定位。

Abstract: In point clouds, noise directly perturbs point coordinates that encode both spatial location and geometry, making one-to-one correspondence construction more challenging than in images. Existing methods impose statistical mappings across noisy variants via noise or optimal transport, but suffer from correspondence ambiguity. In this work, we propose Self-Induced Mirror-Point Consistency (SIMPC) to learn deterministic correspondences between points and the underlying surface in an unsupervised manner. For each noisy point, SIMPC generates a mirror-point on the opposite side of the underlying surface, guided by geometric priors during the denoising process. By encouraging consistency between the denoising targets of the original point and its mirror counterpart, SIMPC effectively localizes the position of underlying surface. Extensive experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that SIMPC significantly outperforms state-of-the-art unsupervised methods and surpasses several strong supervised counterparts.

</details>


### [66] [I2PRef: Image-Driven Point Completion with Iterative Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26914)
*Azhar Hussian,Marina Ritthaler,André Kaup,Vasileios Belagiannis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于图像的点云补全方法，通过Image-to-Point（I2P）模块直接从单张RGB图像重建完整点云，并结合Transformer-based的Point-to-Point（P2P）模块进行细节优化，在无需3D输入的情况下实现最先进的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于多模态的方法通常依赖辅助损失或融合模块，难以充分利用图像中的几何信息；本文旨在将图像作为主要几何来源而非辅助引导，提升图像在点云补全中的作用。

Method: 设计了一个Image-to-Point（I2P）模块，直接从单张RGB图像生成完整点云；引入基于Transformer的Point-to-Point（P2P）细化模块，通过点标记与图像特征之间的自注意力和交叉注意力机制迭代优化结果。

Result: 在ShapeNet-ViPC数据集上进行了大量实验，相较于先前方法，Chamfer Distance相对改善了12.3%，实现了最先进的点云补全性能。

Conclusion: 所提方法通过显式的I2P任务为图像提供了强几何感知先验，有效提升了图像在点云补全中的主导作用，无需3D输入即可实现高质量补全。

Abstract: We present an image-conditioned point cloud completion approach that treats images as the primary geometric source rather than a secondary guide. To this end, we introduce an Image-to-Point (I2P) module that can reconstruct complete point clouds directly from a single RGB image, with no need for 3D inputs. Additionally, we introduce a transformer-based Point-to-Point (P2P) refinement module that uses self- and cross-attention between point tokens and image features to iteratively refine the coarse I2P output. The I2P module enables the image encoder to learn rich geometric representations, while the P2P module progressively recovers fine-grained details. Unlike existing multimodal methods that rely on auxiliary losses or fusion modules, our explicit I2P task provides a strong, geometry-aware prior based on images alone. Extensive experiments on ShapeNet-ViPC demonstrate state-of-the-art completion performance with a 12.3% relative Chamfer Distance improvement over prior methods. Code is available at: https://github.com/AzharSindhi/I2PRef.git

</details>


### [67] [Revealing the core dimensions underlying representations in brains, behavior and AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26921)
*Florian P. Mahner,Ka Chun Lam,Francisco Pereira,Martin N. Hebart*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为相似性基础表示分解（SRF）的通用计算方法，可从各种数据的相似性矩阵中恢复低维、非负且可解释的嵌入表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在揭示塑造表示的潜在维度方面能力有限，且可解释性不足，因此需要一种更强大且通用的方法来解析表示的内在结构。

Method: 提出相似性基础表示分解（SRF），通过分析来自测量数据的相似性矩阵，恢复低维、非负、可解释的嵌入表示，并在模拟和多种真实数据集上验证其有效性。

Result: SRF能够在神经、行为和计算等多种数据集中恢复出可解释的维度，结果与任务特定模型一致，能预测独立的行为特征，提升探索性分析能力，并在假设检验中提供更高统计效能。

Conclusion: SRF是一种具有广泛应用前景的通用方法，可用于揭示、理解并利用表示背后的潜在维度。

Abstract: The study of representations is widespread across fields, including neuroscience, psychology, and artificial intelligence. While representations are often studied and compared through similarities between stimuli, current methods provide only limited access to the dimensions that shape these representations and are often limited in interpretability. To overcome these challenges, here we introduce Similarity-Based Representation Factorization (SRF), a general computational method for recovering low-dimensional, non-negative, interpretable embeddings from similarity matrices derived from measured data. Across simulations and many neural, behavioral, and computational datasets, SRF recovers interpretable dimensions from diverse forms of representational data, even for very sparsely sampled, incomplete data. The dimensions derived from these datasets match those obtained by task-specific models, predict independent behavioral properties, improve exploratory analysis, and offer higher power for confirmatory hypothesis testing than comparing similarity matrices. Together, these results establish SRF as a general-purpose method with broad applications for uncovering, understanding, and leveraging the dimensions underlying representations.

</details>


### [68] [Leveraging Text-to-Image Diffusion Models for Unsupervised Visual Object Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26933)
*Zhengbo Zhang,Zhigang Tu,Junsong Yuan,De Wen Soh,Bo Du*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种利用预训练文本到图像扩散模型进行无监督视觉目标跟踪的新方法，通过交叉注意力机制实现对视频中目标的精确定位与持续跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无监督跟踪器在需要细粒度理解语义和视觉结构信息的场景中表现不佳，而文本到图像扩散模型具有强大的视觉语义和结构理解能力，因此探索将其用于目标跟踪任务。

Method: 将扩散模型重新解释为连接文本与图像模态的桥梁，利用其跨模态注意力机制：通过学习代表目标对象的提示词（prompt），激活交叉注意力图中的对应区域，从而定位目标；方法包括初始提示学习器和在线提示更新器两个组件，分别用于首帧目标捕捉和基于运动信息的提示优化。

Result: 在六个具有挑战性的跟踪数据集上进行了评估，实验结果验证了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 利用预训练扩散模型的语义理解能力可有效提升无监督目标跟踪性能，为无监督跟踪提供了一个新的可行方向。

Abstract: Unsupervised visual object tracking is a challenging task that requires following arbitrary targets in videos without training on ground-truth annotations. Despite considerable progress, existing state-of-the-art unsupervised trackers often struggle in scenarios that demand fine-grained understanding of semantic and visual structural information within video frames. Text-to-image diffusion models are well known for their ability to generate images that accurately reflect the semantics and structures described in the input prompt, demonstrating a strong grasp of visual semantics and structures. Building on this capability, we approach the unsupervised tracking from a new perspective by exploiting the rich semantic knowledge encoded in pretrained text-to-image diffusion models. To adapt the diffusion models, which are originally developed for image generation, to the tracking task, we reinterpret the models as a bridge between text and image modalities. This connection is realized through the cross-attention mechanism: when both text and an image are input into the models, they highlight the regions of the image that are semantically aligned with the text in the cross-attention maps. We therefore learn a prompt that represents the tracking target and activates its corresponding region in the cross-attention map for each frame, which enables object tracking with the diffusion model. Specifically, our method Diff-Tracking is composed of two main components: an initial prompt learner and an online prompt updater. The initial prompt learner generates a prompt that captures the target object in the first frame, allowing the diffusion model to identify the target. The online prompt updater refines the prompt based on motion information, enabling consistent tracking across video frames. We evaluate our approach on six challenging tracking datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach.

</details>


### [69] [CodecCap: High-Fidelity Codec-Inspired Residual Modeling for Dense Video Captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26967)
*Zihan Lin,Songhe Deng,Shuwei He,Danxiang Zhu,Dan Zhang,Yishu Lei,Xianlong Luo,Shikun Feng,Rui Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CodecCap框架，通过关键帧和残差字幕提升视频描述的视觉保真度并减少冗余，并构建VidCapQA基准和CodecVDC-100K数据集验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频字幕方法在视觉保真度和冗余之间难以平衡，整体字幕丢失细节，分段字幕则冗余严重。

Method: 受视频编解码器启发，CodecCap使用关键帧字幕编码稳定视觉上下文，残差字幕捕捉局部动态变化，实现高保真密集视频描述。

Result: 在VidCapQA基准上，强VLM直接生成的字幕仍遗漏大量视觉细节；CodecCap显著优于直接字幕生成方法。

Conclusion: 关键帧-残差字幕机制可有效提升视频语言监督的保真度，为高质量视频理解提供新路径。

Abstract: Existing video captioning methods struggle to balance visual fidelity and redundancy: holistic captions are compact but lose fine-grained evidence, whereas segment-wise captions improve coverage but introduce heavy redundancy. We propose CodecCap, a codec-inspired framework for high-fidelity dense video captioning. Analogous to video codecs, CodecCap represents videos using keyframe and residual captions. Keyframe captions exhaustively encode stable visual context, while residual captions capture temporally only localized actions, motions and changes. This effectively preserves fine-grained visual evidence while reducing redundant descriptions. To quantify the fidelity of captions, we introduce VidCapQA, a caption-then-QA benchmark with 1,000 questions across 14 capability dimensions. Results on VidCapQA show that captions directly generated by strong VLMs still miss many visual details, highlighting caption representation as a critical bottleneck. Experiments show that CodecCap significantly surpasses direct captioning with the same underlying VLMs, suggesting keyframe-residual captioning a way for high-fidelity video-language supervision. We further use CodecCap to construct CodecVDC-100K, a large-scale dense captioning dataset with anchor, residual, scene-level, and video-level supervision.

</details>


### [70] [On the Robustness of Machine Unlearning for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26992)
*Yujie Lin,Kaidi Jia,Jiayao Ma,Chengyi Yang,Jinsong Su*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次系统性地调查和分析了视觉-语言模型（VLM）的遗忘学习，提出了一个全面的分类体系，并通过多种提示设置进行统一评估。研究还提出三种攻击范式，揭示当前方法往往只是隐藏而非真正删除目标知识。实验表明许多现有方法在攻击下仍存在漏洞，强调需要更可靠的多模态遗忘策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言模型可能从训练数据中记忆不期望的信息，因此需要研究机器遗忘学习以确保模型的安全性和隐私性。

Method: 提出一个全面的VLM遗忘学习方法分类体系，进行统一评估，并设计三种攻击范式来测试被遗忘的多模态知识是否可通过上下文提示或下游再训练被重新激活。

Result: 大量实验显示，许多现有的VLM遗忘方法在所提出的攻击下仍然脆弱，说明这些方法通常只是隐藏而非彻底清除目标知识。

Conclusion: 当前的VLM遗忘方法在鲁棒性方面存在显著局限，亟需发展更可靠、真正能实现知识移除的多模态遗忘策略。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) may memorize undesirable information from training data, motivating growing interest in machine unlearning. In this work, we present the first systematic survey and robustness analysis of VLM unlearning. We provide a comprehensive taxonomy and review of existing VLM unlearning methods, together with unified evaluations under multiple prompt settings. We then propose three attack paradigms to examine whether forgotten multimodal knowledge can be reactivated through contextual prompting or downstream retraining. Extensive experiments show that many existing methods remain vulnerable under these attacks, indicating that current approaches often hide rather than fully remove target knowledge. Our study provides new insights into the robustness and limitations of current VLM unlearning methods and highlights the need for more reliable multimodal unlearning strategies. Code is available at https://github.com/XMUDeepLIT/VLM-UnL-Attack.

</details>


### [71] [Timestep-Aware SVDQuant-GPTQ for W4A4 Quantization of Wan2.2-I2V](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27003)
*Junhao Wu,Dezhong Yao,Hai Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种针对MoE视频DiT的W4A4后训练量化方法，结合低秩异常补偿、残差权重量化和分时分专家的激活剪裁策略，在大幅降低显存占用的同时保持生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的W4A4量化方法在处理具有稀疏大值激活异常和时步依赖性强的大型视频扩散Transformer时表现不佳，尤其是双专家MoE结构因不同专家对量化敏感性不同而难以用统一策略校准。

Method: 结合基于SVDQuant的低秩异常补偿、基于GPTQ的重建感知残差权重量化，以及按时间步分箱并为每个专家独立进行的逐层激活剪裁比搜索，实现精细化的后训练量化。

Result: 在OpenS2V-Eval基准上，相比BF16基线峰值GPU显存降低59.3%，VBench平均分仅下降0.9%，成像质量下降2.3%。

Conclusion: 实验表明，针对专家和时步的细粒度校准对于实现高保真W4A4推理至关重要，尤其适用于MoE架构的视频扩散Transformer。

Abstract: W4A4 quantization of large video diffusion Transformers offers substantial memory savings but is hindered by two main challenges: sparse large-magnitude activation outliers, and strongly timestep-dependent activation distributions across the multi-step denoising trajectory. These difficulties are compounded by Wan2.2-I2V's two-expert Mixture-of-Experts DiT design, whose high-noise and low-noise experts exhibit distinct quantization sensitivities that a single global calibration policy cannot capture. We propose a post-training quantization framework combining SVDQuant-based low-rank outlier compensation, GPTQ-based reconstruction-aware residual weight quantization, and timestep-bin-wise per-layer activation clipping-ratio search conducted independently for each expert. On the OpenS2V-Eval benchmark, our method reduces peak GPU memory by 59.3\% relative to the BF16 baseline while incurring only a 0.9\% drop in VBench average score and a 2.3\% drop in Imaging Quality, demonstrating that expert- and timestep-aware calibration is essential for high-fidelity W4A4 inference on MoE video DiTs.

</details>


### [72] [Black-box Membership Inference Attacks on the Pre-training Data of Image-generation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27020)
*Tao Qi,Huili Wang,Yuanhong Huang,Wendan Wang,Lianchao Zhao,Jinrui Wang,Zichen Qin,Shangguang Wang,Yongfeng Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为SD-MIA的黑盒成员推断攻击框架，利用跨模态数据扰动机制检测扩散模型中的预训练数据成员关系，在无需访问模型内部特征的情况下显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的成员推断攻击在检测扩散模型是否使用了特定训练数据时，依赖去噪能力或内部特征，但对记忆较弱的数据效果差且难以应用于闭源模型。本文旨在设计一种适用于黑盒场景、更具实用性的成员推断方法。

Method: 提出SD-MIA框架，通过分析黑盒扩散模型对目标图像及其对应扰动文本指令的去噪行为，提取跨模态扰动下的响应差异作为成员性线索，实现无需内部特征访问的成员推断。

Result: 在公开基准数据集和新构建的数据集上进行了广泛实验，结果表明SD-MIA在性能上优于包括可访问内部特征的基线在内的现有方法。

Conclusion: SD-MIA通过跨模态扰动机制有效提升了黑盒场景下对扩散模型训练数据成员关系的推断能力，具有较强的实用性和优越的检测性能。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of diffusion-based image generation models has raised serious concerns regarding potential copyright and privacy infringements involving human-created data. Membership inference attacks (MIAs) have emerged as a promising tool for identifying unauthorized data usage during model training. Existing methods typically assess the ability of model to denoise perturbed suspect images as an indicator of membership status. However, the discriminative power of such features is highly dependent on the degree of model memorization and deteriorates significantly when applied to less exposed data (e.g., pre-training data). Although several methods attempt to enhance detection by leveraging internal model features, these features are generally inaccessible in mainstream closed-source image generation platforms, limiting their practicality. In this paper, we demonstrate that analyzing how a black-box diffusion model denoises a target image and corresponding perturbed textual instructions can reveal more distinctive membership cues. Based on this insight, we propose a black-box membership inference attack framework (named SD-MIA) that leverages a cross-modal data perturbation mechanism to detect pre-training data in diffusion models. We conduct extensive experiments on both a public benchmark dataset and a newly constructed dataset, each comprising pre-training membership and non-membership samples with identical distributions. Experimental results demonstrate that SD-MIA achieves superior performance compared to existing baselines, including those with the unfair advantage of accessing internal model features.

</details>


### [73] [IPIBench: Evaluating Interactive Proactive Intelligence of MLLMs under Continuous Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27074)
*Jinzhao Li,Yinuo Chen,Wenxuan Song,Yijia Lei,Yichi Zhang,Honglei Yan,Panwang Pan,Miao Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了IPIBench，首个用于评估多模态大语言模型在流式视频中交互式主动智能的基准，并设计了无需训练的IPI-Agent框架来提升模型在主动触发和多轮交互协调上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态大语言模型主要在静态、单轮问答任务上表现良好，但在真实场景中需要对连续视觉输入进行主动推理。当前缺乏能够评估模型在动态、多轮、混合主动与被动交互下的性能基准。

Method: 提出IPIBench基准，涵盖主动监控、主动任务管理和交错的主被动请求；同时提出IPI-Agent框架，包含交互控制策略和时间门控机制，以稳定主动触发并协调多轮互动。

Result: 在代表性多模态大语言模型上的评测显示，现有模型存在主动触发不稳定和主被动行为协调能力弱的问题；IPI-Agent在所有设置下均显著提升了模型表现。

Conclusion: IPIBench填补了动态多轮主被动交互评估的空白，IPI-Agent为提升多模态模型在流式场景中的交互智能提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Recent multimodal large language models (MLLMs) achieve strong performance on reactive question answering, but real-world streaming assistants require proactive reasoning over continuous visual inputs. Existing benchmarks mainly study reactive or proactive interactions in isolated single-turn settings, overlooking dynamic multi-turn scenarios where users may add, modify, or cancel proactive requests alongside interleaved reactive queries. To address this gap, we introduce IPIBench, the first benchmark for evaluating Interactive Proactive Intelligence of MLLMs under streaming video settings. IPIBench covers proactive monitoring, proactive task management, and interleaved reactive-proactive requests. Evaluations on representative MLLMs reveal two major limitations: unstable proactive triggering and weak coordination between reactive and proactive behaviors. We further propose IPI-Agent, a training-free agentic framework with an interaction-control policy and a temporal-gating mechanism for stabilizing proactive triggering and coordinating multi-turn interactions. Experiments show that IPI-Agent consistently improves existing MLLMs across all benchmark settings.

</details>


### [74] [SoftCap: Soft-Budget Control for Diffusion Transformer Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27075)
*Yuhang Zhang,Junxiang Qiu,Huixia Ben,Zhenhua Tang,Shuo Wang,Yanbin Hao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为SoftCap的训练免费控制层，用于基于缓存的Diffusion Transformer推理，通过轨迹漂移观测器和软预算PI控制器动态调整全步触发阈值，从而在保持计算量相近的情况下显著提升图像生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的训练免费加速方法通常依赖固定的调度或手动调参来决定何时执行全步操作，缺乏灵活性和自适应性，导致性能受限。

Method: SoftCap结合了轻量级隐藏状态统计的轨迹漂移观测器（估计局部缓存风险）和软预算PI控制器（根据实际计算量相对于固定参考配置动态调整全步触发阈值），实现对缓存风险的感知与自适应控制。

Result: 在FLUX.1-dev模型上，SoftCap在相似中等计算量下优于SpeCa方法：ImageReward从0.967提升至0.981，LPIPS-Full从0.518降低至0.498，同时验证了软预算机制的有效性。

Conclusion: SoftCap提供了一种灵活且无需训练的DiT推理加速控制机制，通过软预算控制实现了性能提升与计算效率的良好平衡。

Abstract: Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) achieve strong visual quality, but their iterative denoising process requires many costly Transformer evaluations. Training-free acceleration methods reduce this cost by caching, forecasting, or verifying intermediate features, yet the runtime decision of when to execute a Full step is often driven by fixed schedules or hand-tuned thresholds. We propose \textbf{SoftCap}, a training-free control layer for cache-based DiT inference. SoftCap couples a Trajectory Drift Observer, which estimates local cache risk from lightweight hidden-state statistics, with a Soft-Budget PI Controller, which adjusts the Full-triggering threshold from realized compute relative to a fixed reference profile. The budget is a soft ceiling: it shapes the threshold but does not require a run to spend a prescribed number of Full evaluations. On FLUX.1-dev, SoftCap improves over SpeCa at a comparable middle-compute operating point, raising ImageReward from 0.967 to 0.981 and reducing LPIPS-Full from 0.518 to 0.498 at nearly identical FLOPs, while target-sweep diagnostics show the intended soft-ceiling behavior as the budget is relaxed.

</details>


### [75] [Semi-Supervised Gaze Estimation via Disentangled Subspace Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27080)
*Qida Tan,Hongyu Yang,Wenchao Du*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种解耦子空间对比学习框架（DSCL），通过半监督学习和雅可比正则化提升基于外观的视线估计泛化能力，仅用少量标注数据即可在域内和跨域场景下取得竞争性性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外观-based视线估计因标注样本有限和数据集多样性不足导致泛化能力差，现有弱监督方法依赖伪标签，仍存在域偏移问题。

Method: 设计了一种半监督学习架构，引入雅可比正则化解耦特征表示到专注于俯仰角和偏航角等特定视线分量的判别性子空间，并利用各子空间内的固有有序关系进行对比学习。

Result: 在多个基准上验证了DSCL的有效性，在仅使用20%、10%甚至5%标注数据的情况下，实现了具有竞争力的性能，且具备即插即用特性。

Conclusion: DSCL通过解耦特征表示与子空间对比学习，有效提升了模型对未标注数据的利用能力和跨域泛化性能，减少了对手工标注的依赖。

Abstract: Appearance-based gaze estimation always suffers from poor generalization due to limited annotated samples and insufficient dataset diversity. Leading approaches adopt weakly supervised learning to generate large-scale pseudo-labeled data from unconstrained real-world scenarios, aiming to mitigate the domain shifts. In this work, we devise a simple yet effective semi-supervised learning architecture that leverages unlabeled data to enhance domain generalization, thereby reducing reliance on labor-intensive manual annotations. Our key insight is to impose Jacobian regularization to disentangle feature representations into discriminative subspaces dedicated to specific gaze components, such as pitch and yaw angles.
