*Param Thakkar,Anushka Yadav,Michael Tiemann,Abhi Mehta,Akshita Bhasin,Shrinivas Khedkar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: BhashaSetu是一个大规模、语言学增强的英-马拉地语平行数据集，旨在解决低资源神经机器翻译中的数据稀缺问题，通过严格的语料去重等预处理显著提升翻译性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 马拉地语使用者超过9500万，但在高质量多领域平行语料库中代表性不足，限制了低资源神经机器翻译的发展。

Method: 构建包含278万句对的英-马拉地语平行数据集，涵盖新闻、政治、医疗等多个领域，并提供词干化和词形还原标注；采用BLEU、spBLEU、chrF++和TER等指标评估多种先进翻译模型，并使用LoRA对NLLB-200-distilled-600M进行参数高效微调。

Result: 实验表明，语料级去重是提升下游翻译质量最关键的预处理步骤，去除该步骤会导致BLEU下降1.17、chrF++下降2.21；发布的数据集支持形态感知分析并促进可复现研究。

Conclusion: 系统化的跨源语料清洗是一种低成本、高效益的干预手段，对低资源且形态丰富的语言机器翻译具有重要意义。

Abstract: We present BhashaSetu, a linguistically enriched English--Marathi parallel dataset addressing persistent data limitations in low-resource neural machine translation (NMT). Marathi, spoken by over 95 million people, remains underrepresented in high-quality parallel corpora across diverse domains. Our dataset comprises 2.78 million sentence pairs from heterogeneous sources including news, politics, healthcare, literature, and culture, with stemmed and lemmatized representations to support morphology-aware analysis. We benchmark multiple state-of-the-art translation models using BLEU, spBLEU, chrF++, and TER metrics, and conduct parameter-efficient fine-tuning of NLLB-200-distilled-600M using LoRA. A key finding from our ablation: corpus-level deduplication is the single largest preprocessing contributor to downstream quality (removing it reduces performance by 1.17 BLEU and 2.21 chrF++), demonstrating that disciplined cross-source corpus hygiene is a low-cost, high-impact intervention for low-resource, morphologically rich languages. The dataset is publicly released to promote reproducible and linguistically informed low-resource NMT research.

</details>


### [154] [FalAR: A Large-scale Speaker-Annotated European Portuguese Speech Corpus of Parliamentary Sessions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27062)
*Francisco Teixeira,Carlos Carvalho,Mariana Julião,Catarina Botelho,Rubén Solera-Ureña,Sérgio Paulo,Thomas Rolland,Ben Peters,Isabel Trancoso,Alberto Abad*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了FalAR，一个大规模的欧洲葡萄牙语议会演讲语音语料库，包含约5800小时的语音数据，其中4850小时具有说话人身份标注。该语料库用于改善欧洲葡萄牙语语音识别系统的性能，实验表明将其用作预训练数据可使基线模型的词错误率相对降低14%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于欧洲葡萄牙语在现有大规模语音数据资源中代表性不足，导致其语音识别系统性能较差，因此需要构建专门的大规模标注语料库以弥补这一差距。

Method: 通过使用先进的欧洲葡萄牙语CAMÕES ASR模型进行转录-参考对齐，收集并构建了涵盖约20年时间跨度的欧洲葡萄牙语议会会议语音数据，并对部分数据进行了说话人身份标注及元数据记录。

Result: FalAR语料库包含5800小时语音数据，其中4850小时具有说话人身份信息，共涉及1180名说话人；实验结果显示，将FalAR作为预训练数据可在ASR任务中带来最高达14%的相对词错误率（WER）下降。

Conclusion: FalAR是一个高质量、大规模的欧洲葡萄牙语语音语料库，能够有效提升低资源语言变体的语音识别性能，为未来相关研究和应用提供了重要资源。

Abstract: State-of-the-art performance for Automatic Speech Recognition (ASR) largely depends on the availability of large-scale labeled corpora. This creates a demand for increased data collection efforts, particularly for under-represented languages and dialectal varieties. Due to having considerably fewer speakers (around 11 million), European Portuguese (EP) is overshadowed by Brazilian Portuguese (BP) (around 200 million speakers) in currently available large-scale speech data resources, resulting in under-performing speech-based systems for EP users. To address this gap, and following similar data collection efforts for other languages, we present FalAR, a large-scale, speaker-annotated speech corpus of European Portuguese parliamentary sessions. Spanning approximately 20 years, FalAR comprises 5,800 hours of speech data. In addition, 4,850 hours have speaker identity annotations, for a total of 1,180 speakers with associated metadata including age, gender, political affiliation, and parliamentary role. The corpus was built using a state-of-the-art EP CAMÕES ASR model for transcription-reference alignment. In this paper, we describe the data collection process, together with the main characteristics of the FalAR corpus. Furthermore, we evaluate the trade-off between data quantity and alignment accuracy on ASR performance, with our experiments demonstrating that incorporating FalAR as pre-training data yields up to 14% relative WER improvement over baseline models.

</details>


### [155] [Large Language Model-Powered Query-Driven Event Timeline Summarization in Industrial Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27066)
*Mingyue Wang,Xingyu Xie,Hang Yang,Li Gao,Lixin Su,Ge Chen,Dawei Yin,Daiting Shi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: QDET是百度搜索中部署的查询驱动事件时间线摘要系统，通过领域专用优化实现高质量、低成本的时间线生成，并在真实场景中显著提升用户交互指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统主题中心方法难以满足用户对特定查询事件的时间线需求，且大规模模型计算成本高，需要更高效、聚焦的事件时间线生成方案。

Method: 提出QDET系统，采用多任务监督微调（包含时间排序、因果判断和时间线补全）提升小模型性能，并结合强化学习实现长度受限的事件摘要生成。

Result: 7B参数模型在时间线摘要F1上达到76.2%，略超671B零样本模型，长度合规率达88.2%，在线测试显示CTR提升5.5%，停留时间增加4.6%，探索深度提高4.4%。

Conclusion: 领域特定优化可使小规模模型在专业任务上媲美超大规模模型，QDET实现了生产可用的高效事件时间线生成，具备良好的实际效果与泛化能力。

Abstract: Understanding how events evolve over time is essential for search engines handling queries about trending news. We present QDET (Query-Driven Event Timeline Summarization), a production system deployed on Baidu Search that constructs focused event timelines to explain specific query events. Unlike traditional topic-centric approaches that aim for comprehensive coverage, QDET identifies and organizes sub-events closely relevant to the query from noisy candidate sets formed by millions of documents retrieved daily. QDET incorporates two key innovations: (1) multi-task supervised fine-tuning with three auxiliary tasks-temporal ordering, causal judgment, and timeline completion-that enable compact models to match the performance of much larger general-purpose models in specialized domains; (2) reinforcement learning-based event concise summarization that enforces strict length constraints while maintaining semantic quality, achieving 88.2% length compliance and outperforming 671B-scale models by 7.7 points in constraint satisfaction. Our fine-tuned 7B parameter model achieves 76.2% F1 score on timeline summarization, slightly surpassing the zero-shot performance of DeepSeek-R1-671B (76.1% F1) while using only 1% of its parameters-demonstrating that domain-specific optimization enables production-ready models with comparable quality at drastically reduced computational costs. Online A/B tests on Baidu Search validate real-world effectiveness, showing 5.5% CTR improvement, 4.6% longer dwell time, and 4.4% deeper exploration compared to single-task baselines. We further demonstrate that timeline understanding transfers to heat prediction, confirming effective knowledge transfer to downstream tasks.

</details>


### [156] [QUACK: Questioning, Understanding, and Auditing Communicated Knowledge in Multimodal Social Deduction Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27068)
*Ye Yuan,Rui Song,Weien Li,Zeyu Li,Haochen Liu,Xiangyu Kong,Changjiang Han,Yonghan Yang,Zichen Zhao,Zixuan Dong,Fuyuan Lyu,Bowei He,Haolun Wu,Jikun Kang,Xue Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: QUACK是一个开源环境和评估框架，用于审计多模态社会推理中智能体语言的 grounded 程度，通过游戏结果、行为轨迹和语句一致性三个层面进行评估，并揭示当前VLM智能体在空间陈述和指控方面存在严重幻觉和缺乏证据支持的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的社交推理游戏测试环境大多仅依赖游戏胜负作为评分标准，且局限于文本交互，难以判断智能体的语言是否真正基于其感知和行为，也无法识别其行为背后的失败模式。

Method: 提出QUACK框架，包含一个核心的语句验证管道，从引擎日志重构智能体的真实行为轨迹，并逐条核对讨论中的声明，自动标记空间幻觉、无依据指控、欺骗崩溃和语言-行为不一致等问题。在同构和跨模型对抗设置下评估了三种前沿视觉语言模型（VLM）。

Result: 发现即使是最强的智能体也有15.1%的空间性可验证声明出现幻觉，超过一半的指控缺乏事实依据。

Conclusion: QUACK能够有效揭示LLM/VLM智能体在多模态社交推理任务中的语言与行为脱节问题，为改进智能体的 grounded 推理能力提供了可量化的评估工具和数据支持。

Abstract: Social deduction games have become a popular testbed for probing reasoning, deception, coordination, and belief modeling in Large Language Model (LLM) agents. However, most environments are scored only by game outcomes such as win rates and largely remain to text-only interaction, making it difficult to tell whether an agent's language is actually grounded in what it perceived and did, or to identify the failure modes underlying its behavior. To address this gap, we introduce QUACK, an open-source environment and evaluation framework for auditing the grounding of agent language in multimodal social reasoning. QUACK evaluates agents at three levels: game outcomes, behavioral trajectories, and utterance-level consistency. Its core Statement Verification Pipeline reconstructs each agent's ground-truth trajectory from engine logs and checks every discussion claim against it, automatically flagging spatial hallucination, unsupported accusation, deception collapse, and language-action inconsistency. Evaluating three frontier VLMs in both homogeneous and cross-model adversarial settings, we find that even the strongest agent hallucinates 15.1% of its verifiable spatial claims and makes over half of its accusations without grounded evidence. We release the full engine, evaluation framework, toolkit, and logs at https://github.com/AAAAA-Academia-Attractions/QUACK.

</details>


### [157] [E3: Issue-Level Backtesting for Automated Research Critique](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27072)
*Yashwardhan Chaudhuri,Sanyam Jain,Paridhi Mundra*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: E3是一个自动化评审助手，能够识别研究论文中的技术问题，并报告其性质、位置及解决方法，在ICLR 2026论文评估中表现优于人类评审和基于大模型的基线系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升论文评审效率与质量，自动发现人类评审可能遗漏的技术问题，减少评审偏差。

Method: 提出E3系统，通过分析论文内容识别关键技术问题（如未支持的主张、缺失消融实验等），并采用问题级回溯测试协议进行评估，使用元裁判标注问题-来源对。

Result: 在100篇ICLR 2026论文和4598个问题条目上测试，E3的包含部分匹配的召回率达90.2%，严格召回到65.8%，均高于GPT、Claude和人类评审；E3恢复了人类提出问题的89.6%，并新增1635个有效问题条目。

Conclusion: E3在识别论文技术问题方面显著优于现有方法和人类评审，具备作为评审辅助工具的潜力，且相关数据与代码已公开。

Abstract: We present E3, an automated review assistant that augments reviewers and engineering teams by identifying decision-relevant technical concerns in research papers. For each concern, E3 reports its nature, its location, its bearing on the contribution, and the analysis or evidence that would resolve it, covering unsupported claims, missing ablations, weak baselines, hidden assumptions, threats to validity, and leakage risks. To evaluate E3 without contamination confounds we adopt an issue-level backtesting protocol: the corpus is restricted to papers postdating the training cutoff of every automated source, and for each paper a meta-judge that observes only anonymised reviews labels every issue-source pair as Caught, Partial, or Missed. Applied to 100 ICLR 2026 papers and 4598 judged issue rows, comparing E3 against the ICLR human reviews and two prompt-matched LLM baselines built on gpt-5.4 from OpenAI and claude-opus-4-6 from Anthropic, with meta-judge gpt-5.5, E3 attains the highest recall on every aggregate metric. Partial-inclusive recall reaches 90.2 percent, which is 15.5 points over GPT, 17.1 points over Claude, and 29.2 points over the human reviews, and strict recall preserves the ordering at 65.8 percent. On concerns raised by the human reviewers, E3 recovers 89.6 percent; on concerns the human reviewers missed it surfaces 1635 additional rows admitted into the judged union, 406 above the next-best source. Corpus, baseline prompts, judge prompt template, and evaluation code are released.

</details>


### [158] [On the Hidden Costs of Counterfactual Knowledge Training in LLM Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27083)
*Xiaotian Ye,Xiaohan Wang,Mengqi Zhang,Shu Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了大语言模型中的反事实调优（CFT）在遗忘学习中的表现，发现了知识冲突和幻觉溢出两个问题，并提出了RWKU+基准来系统诊断这些问题，旨在为更严谨的LLM遗忘学习研究提供指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管CFT在LLM遗忘学习中展现出潜力，但在某些方面仍表现不佳，需要深入分析其潜在缺陷。

Method: 引入了一个扩展的基准RWKU+，包含新的权衡指标和梯度级诊断工具，以系统地识别和分析CFT中的问题。

Result: 发现了CFT中存在的知识冲突和幻觉溢出问题，这些问题会影响模型的优化和泛化能力。

Conclusion: CFT虽然有前景，但存在显著缺陷，需进一步改进；RWKU+提供了有效的诊断手段，有助于推动更严格的LLM遗忘学习研究。

Abstract: Counterfactual tuning (CFT) has emerged as a promising paradigm for Large Language Model (LLM) unlearning by training models to generate alternative fictitious knowledge in place of undesired content. However, in this work, we find that this paradigm still underperforms other paradigms in some aspects, and identify two previously overlooked pitfalls underlying this gap: (1) knowledge conflict, where mutual inconsistencies within counterfactual corpora induce conflicting gradients that disrupt parameter optimization, and (2) hallucination spillover, where fitting false targets instills a persistent fabrication bias, inflating hallucination rates on unrelated domains. To systematically diagnose these issues, we introduce RWKU+, an extended benchmark equipped with novel trade-off metrics and gradient-level diagnostic tools. Our work further discusses the limitations and overhead of the paradigm, aiming to provide insights and actionable guidance for more rigorous LLM unlearning research.

</details>


### [159] [LLMs Are Already Good Tutors: Training-Free Prompt Optimization for Pedagogical Math Tutoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27088)
*Unggi Lee,Minchul Shin,Yeil Jeong,Sookbun Lee,Jeongsu Moon,Kyungtae Joo,Eunjoo Lee,Hoilym Kwon*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了无需训练的提示优化作为数学辅导大模型对齐的替代方法，提出并评估了多种教育专用方法，在多个基准上超越了基于强化学习的基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索无需多GPU和强化学习训练的大模型对齐方法，以降低数学辅导模型的开发门槛。

Method: 通过API调用进化系统提示（prompt），适配7种已有方法并提出5种教育专用方法，在5种条件下于2个OOD基准套件上进行评估。

Result: 所有最优方法均超过最强RL基线（R_total=0.633），其中ParetoGrad在后测解题率、信息泄露控制和帮助性之间达到最佳帕累托平衡；行为分析显示训练-free方法更依赖教学知识模式，但在意图级支架上有所下降，且发现跨范式的任务相关推理模式效应。

Conclusion: 仅通过提示优化即可高效开发具有良好教学对齐性的大模型辅导系统，显著降低计算需求。

Abstract: Aligning LLMs for math tutoring typically requires RL-based training with multi-GPU infrastructure. We investigate whether training-free prompt optimization-evolving only the system prompt via API calls-can serve as a practical alternative. We adapt 7 published methods and propose 5 education-specialized methods, evaluating these 12 methods under 5 conditions on 2 OOD benchmark suites. All 12 best-per-method configurations surpass the strongest RL-trained baseline (R_total = 0.633), and our ParetoGrad achieves the best Pareto balance across post-test solve rate, leak control, and helpfulness, rather than dominating any single component. Behavioral analysis with an 82-code educational codebook reveals that training-free methods rely on teaching-knowledge patterns at 2-3x the rate of RL-trained models, with a compensating ~10 percentage-point reduction in intent-level scaffolding. We also find a task-dependent reasoning mode effect consistent across training-free and RL-based paradigms. Our approach enables efficient development of pedagogically aligned LLM tutors with prompts alone and minimal compute.

</details>


### [160] [MiRD: Reliable Set-Valued Prediction for Open-Ended Question Answering via Miscoverage Risk Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27091)
*Anqi Hu,Zhiyuan Wang,Zijun Jia,Bo Fu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了MiRD框架，用于在开放性问答中通过两阶段方法实现可靠的集合值预测，有效控制采样失败和条件选择失败带来的误覆盖风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于共形预测的方法依赖于有限采样能产生至少一个可接受候选答案的脆弱假设，否则需丢弃校准样本，限制了实用性。

Method: MiRD分为两个阶段：第一阶段建立有限采样下未产生可接受答案的概率上界；第二阶段在采样成功的条件下，利用与录取相关的非一致性分数对共形选择阈值进行校准。

Result: 在三个开放性问答数据集和八个模型上的实验表明，MiRD能够同时控制采样风险、条件选择风险和总体误覆盖，并比PAC风格方法提供更紧的上界，生成更自适应的预测集。

Conclusion: MiRD通过分解误覆盖来源并保持校准集完整性，提升了开放性问答中集合预测的可靠性与效率。

Abstract: Reliable set-valued prediction provides a principled way to mitigate hallucinations in open-ended question answering (QA), yet existing conformal approaches typically rely on a fragile premise: finite sampling must already produce at least one admissible candidate, or calibration examples violating this condition are discarded. In this paper, we introduce MiRD, a two-stage framework that decomposes overall miscoverage into sampling failure and conditional selection failure. In Stage I, MiRD establishes an expectation-level marginal upper bound on the probability that finite sampling produces no admissible answer under a fixed budget. In Stage II, conditioned on sampling success, MiRD calibrates a conformal selection threshold using admission-correlated nonconformity scores defined over the full calibration set, thereby preserving calibration-set integrity. Across three open-ended QA datasets and eight models, MiRD controls sampling risk, conditional selection risk, and overall miscoverage, while yielding tighter first-stage bounds than PAC-style alternatives and more adaptive prediction sets than successful-only calibration.

</details>


### [161] [LitSeg: Narrative-Aware Document Segmentation for Literary RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27156)
*Ruikang Zhang,Zhanni Chen,Yiqiao Cai,Qi Su*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于叙事理论指导的文档分割框架LitSeg，用于提升检索增强生成（RAG）在文学作品等长尾领域中的性能。通过多阶段提示提取事件、理清叙事线索与结构，并引入轻量级模型LitSeg-Lite以降低计算开销，实验证明该方法显著提升了检索准确性和下游任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的文档分割策略在处理文学作品时缺乏语义理解，忽视复杂的叙事结构，导致情节碎片化和指代不清，影响RAG系统的检索与生成效果。因此需要一种更符合文学文本特性的分割方法。

Method: 提出LitSeg框架，利用多阶段提示技术从文本中提取有效事件、解构叙事脉络、明确叙事结构并定位转折点来指导分割；进一步构建LitSeg-Lite，通过两阶段训练策略在LitSeg生成的数据上进行微调，实现轻量化的单次推理分割。

Result: 实验表明，所提方法在多个数据集上显著提高了检索准确率和上下文相关性，进而改善了问答等下游任务的表现；消融实验验证了叙事理论指导和数据蒸馏的有效性。

Conclusion: 基于叙事理论的分割方法能有效提升文学类文本在RAG系统中的处理效果，LitSeg及其轻量版本为领域特定的文档分割提供了新思路。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) enhances Large Language Models (LLMs) by incorporating external knowledge, particularly for long-tail domains such as literary works. However, the critical step of document segmentation in RAG remains largely underexplored. Existing strategies are typically semantically blind and overlook the complicated narrative structures of literary works, often resulting in fragmented plots and unclear references that severely hinder retrieval and generation performance. To address this, we propose LitSeg, a novel narrative-theory-guided segmentation framework. By employing multi-stage prompting, LitSeg explicitly extracts valid events, untangles narrative threads, clarifies narrative structures, and locates turning points to inform segmentation. To alleviate the computational overhead of multi-stage inference with large-scale models, we further introduce LitSeg-Lite, a lightweight single-pass chunker fine-tuned on LitSeg-generated data via a two-stage training strategy, distilling the complex process into a single inference pass. Extensive experiments demonstrate that with structurally independent text chunks, our methods significantly improve retrieval accuracy and context relevance over baselines, ultimately enhancing downstream QA performance, while ablation studies validate the efficacy of narratological guidance and data distillation.

</details>


### [162] [Formalization of Malagasy conjugation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27161)
*Joro Ny Aina Ranaivoarison,Eric Laporte,Baholisoa Simone Ralalaoherivony*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了基于Unitex平台构建马达加斯加语简单动词的词典式形态分析器的核心语言学工作，通过有限状态转换器形式化动词词干及其屈折词缀的变化，并设计了可读性强、便于语言学家扩展和更新的词典与转换器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了实现对马达加斯加语简单动词的有效形态分析，支持该语言的自然语言处理应用，需要构建一个系统化的电子词典和分析工具。

Method: 使用Unitex平台构建马达加斯加语简单动词的电子词典，基于形态特征编码数据，并利用78个用于生成词干异形体的有限状态转换器和271个用于识别词干与词缀的转换器进行形态分析。

Result: 成功构建了一个可生成词干异形体并能识别屈折形式中词干和词缀的形态分析系统，形成了结构清晰、可编辑的图形式有限状态转换器。

Conclusion: 该形态分析器具有良好的可读性和可维护性，能够被语言学家方便地扩展和更新，为马达加斯加语的语言技术发展提供了基础资源。

Abstract: This paper reports the core linguistic work performed to construct a dictionary-based morphological analyser for Malagasy simple verbs. It uses the Unitex platform and comprised the contruction of an electronic dictionary for Malagasy simple verbs. The data is encoded on the basis of morphological features. The morphological variations of verb stems and their combination with inflectional affixes are formalized in finite-state transducers represented by editable graphs. 78 transducers allow Unitex to generate a dictionary of allomorphs of stems. 271 other transducers are used by the morphological analyser of Unitex to recognize the stem and the affixes in conjugated verbs. The design of the dictionary and transducers prioritizes readability, so that they can be extended and updated by linguists.

</details>


### [163] [Grounding Text Embeddings in Stakeholder Associations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27168)
*Jonathan Rystrøm,Sofie Burgos-Thorsen,Zihao Fu,Johan Irving Søltoft,Kenneth C. Enevoldsen,Chris Russell*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为“利益相关者基础练习”的方法，用于评估文本嵌入模型是否捕捉到了领域专家所重视的语义差异，并在丹麦政策问题和美国联邦AI用例两个案例中验证了嵌入模型与人类专家判断之间存在显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确保文本嵌入表示与人类意图一致对于有效分析至关重要，但目前尚不清楚嵌入是否能准确反映人类专家的语义距离判断。

Method: 提出了“利益相关者基础练习”方法，通过使专家的关联显式化，将嵌入模型结果建立在人类理解基础上，并在两个不同语言和领域的案例中进行验证。

Result: 在丹麦政策问题的主要案例研究中发现，神经文本嵌入的可靠性明显低于人类专家（差距为19-26个百分点），且这种不一致会影响下游聚类性能；在美国联邦AI用例的次要研究中也复现了这一差距（16个百分点）。

Conclusion: 利益相关者基础练习为评估嵌入模型是否捕捉到对领域专家最重要的语义差异提供了一种实用方法，并表明当前嵌入模型在反映人类语义判断方面仍有显著不足。

Abstract: Text embeddings are widely used to analyse large corpora of complex texts. However, it is unclear whether the embeddings capture the same semantic distances as the human experts using them. Ensuring alignment between embedding representations and human intentions is essential for valid analyses. We present the Stakeholder Grounding Exercise, a method for making expert associations explicit and grounding embedding model results in human understanding. In our primary case study on Danish policy issues, we find that neural text embeddings are substantially less reliable than human experts (19-26 pp gap), and that this misalignment propagates to downstream clustering performance (Spearman $ρ=0.9$ between exercise ranking and cluster quality). A secondary study on US Federal AI use cases replicates the gap (16pp) in English, using a digital protocol and a different community of experts -- demonstrating that the gap is not an artefact of a single instrument or domain. The Stakeholder Grounding Exercise offers a practical method for assessing whether embedding models capture the semantic distinctions that matter most to domain experts.

</details>


### [164] [MAIGO: Mitigating Lost-in-Conversation with History-Cleaned On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27186)
*Haoyu Zheng,Yun Zhu,Shu Yuan,Shangming Chen,Qing Wang,Wenqiao Zhang,Jun Xiao,Yueting Zhuang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MAIGO是一种基于自蒸馏的去污染方法，通过清理历史对话中的助手回复来缓解大模型在多轮任务中因自我污染导致的性能下降问题，在不依赖额外奖励或标签的情况下显著提升了多轮任务准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在多轮对话任务中表现下降（即“迷失在对话”LiC问题），部分原因是中间轮次的助手回复污染了后续上下文，导致早期错误被持续传递。本文旨在识别并缓解这种自我污染机制。

Method: 提出MAIGO方法，采用on-policy自蒸馏策略：在中间轮次移除历史中的助手回复但保留用户可见的分片前缀；在回答轮次则基于完整用户对话生成参考答案进行知识蒸馏，并引入可靠性权重以降低与清洁参考不一致样本的影响。整个过程无需验证器奖励、状态标签或推理时辅助结构。

Result: 在LiC配对视图协议下，MAIGO将Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct模型的SHARDED准确率从52.8提升至66.1，SHARDED/FULL比率从66.5%提升至84.1%，同时保持FULL准确率损失在2.3点以内。

Conclusion: 自我污染是导致LiC差距的一个可训练因素，MAIGO通过历史清理和自蒸馏有效缓解了该问题，为提升模型在多轮任务中的一致性提供了新思路。

Abstract: Large language models often solve tasks from a fully specified prompt but degrade when the same requirements unfold over multiple turns, known as the lost-in-conversation (LiC) gap. We trace part of this degradation to self-contamination: intermediate assistant replies enter later context and carry early deviations forward. Motivated by this mechanism, we propose MAIGO, an on-policy self-distillation method that reduces this contamination using history-cleaned references from the model's own policy. For middle turns, MAIGO removes prior assistant replies while preserving the user-visible sharded prefix; for answer turns, it distills from paired full-view references conditioned on the completed user-side dialogue. A reliability weight downweights middle-turn samples that disagree with the clean reference. MAIGO requires no verifier rewards, state labels, or inference-time scaffolding. Under the LiC paired-view protocol with deterministic verifiers, MAIGO improves Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct SHARDED accuracy from 52.8 to 66.1 and the SHARDED/FULL ratio from 66.5% to 84.1%, while keeping FULL accuracy within 2.3 points. These results show that self-contamination is a trainable component of the LiC gap.

</details>


### [165] [Beyond Binary: Speech Representations Across the Cognitive Score Hierarchy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27189)
*Serli Kopar,Roshan Prakash Rane,Christian Mychajliw,Lydia Federmann,Gerhard Eschweiler,Daniela Berg,Sam Gijsen,Paula Andrea Perez-Toro,Kerstin Ritter*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究利用5754个德语神经心理学评估录音，比较手工声学特征与自监督学习（SSL）嵌入在轻度认知障碍（MCI）分层认知评估中的表现，发现任务约束类型影响模型在不同层级的表现趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究语音表征如何反映轻度认知障碍（MCI）的分层认知评估结构，并比较不同类型特征在多层级评分中的有效性。

Method: 使用5,754个德语神经心理评估录音，评估六个认知任务在任务级、领域级和全局级三个评分层次上的表现，对比手工制作的声学特征与自监督学习（SSL）嵌入的效果。

Result: SSL表征在较低层级通常优于手工特征，但在MCI分类上趋势相反；响应自由度高的任务随层级上升性能下降（‘专家型’表征），而结构化任务在高层级表现更好（‘通才型’表征）。

Conclusion: 任务的结构约束类型显著影响语音表征在分层认知评估中的有效性，揭示了自动化临床语音分析中任务设计与评估层级之间的关联。

Abstract: This study examines the relationship between speech representations and the hierarchical structure of cognitive assessment in mild cognitive impairment. Utilizing 5,754 German neuropsychological assessment recordings, we evaluate six cognitive tasks across three score levels: task, domain, and global levels. We compare hand-crafted acoustic features with self-supervised learning (SSL) embeddings. Results show that although SSL representations generally outperform hand-crafted features at lower levels, this trend reverses for MCI classification. Furthermore, task-specific constraints influence performance: tasks with greater response freedom exhibit performance dilution as hierarchical levels increase, suggesting ``specialist'' representations, whereas the performance of highly structured tasks increases toward higher levels, suggesting ``generalist'' representations. These findings show links between task constraints and assessment hierarchy in automated clinical speech analysis.

</details>


### [166] [Learning When to Think While Listening in Large Audio-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27190)
*Zhiyuan Song,Weici Zhao,Yang Xiao,Suhao Yu,Cheng Zhu,Jiatao Gu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可学习的“等待-思考-回答”控制机制，用于大型音频语言模型（LALMs），通过优化推理时机与响应延迟之间的平衡，在多个任务上提升了问答准确性和响应效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在流式语音交互中，推理质量与响应速度紧密耦合：过早回答可能导致错误，过晚则增加用户可见延迟。因此需要一种能根据部分音频证据动态决策何时等待、何时推理、何时回答的机制。

Method: 基于Qwen2.5-Omni-7B构建模型，利用口语推理数据生成对齐的‘等待-思考-回答’轨迹，采用监督微调训练控制器，并进一步应用解耦剪裁与动态采样策略优化（DAPO），结合多种奖励信号联合优化整个推理路径。

Result: 在六任务合成SRQA基准上，DAPO控制器将行加权准确率从67.6%提升至70.3%，并使端点后最终思考长度减少14%；在186项真实录音的Real Audio Bench上，DAPO控制器是唯一最终思考长度低于基线的变体，且表现稳定。

Conclusion: 流式语音语言模型应学会在音频流中适时显式表达中间推理过程，以实现质量和延迟的更好权衡。

Abstract: Recent advances in Large Audio-Language Models (LALMs) have made real-time, streaming spoken interaction increasingly practical. In this setting, reasoning quality and responsiveness are tightly coupled: delaying reasoning until the speech endpoint can improve answer quality but moves deliberation into user-visible response delay, while answering too early risks committing before decisive evidence arrives. We introduce a learnable wait-think-answer control formulation for LALMs. Motivated by the incremental nature of human conversation, the controller decides under partial audio evidence when to wait, when to externalize a compact reasoning update, and when to answer. Using Qwen2.5-Omni-7B as the base model, we construct aligned wait-think-answer traces from spoken reasoning data, train the controller with supervised fine-tuning (SFT), and then apply Decoupled Clip and Dynamic Sampling Policy Optimization (DAPO). The reward combines answer correctness, action validity, update timing, latency synchronization, reasoning quality, and chain consistency, optimizing the complete wait-think-answer trajectory and not the final answer alone. On a six-task synthetic spoken reasoning question answering (SRQA) benchmark, the six-reward DAPO controller improves the row-weighted accuracy from 67.6% to 70.3% while reducing post-endpoint final-think length by 14% under the same Qwen deployment harness. On a 186-item human-recorded Real Audio Bench, a transfer check beyond text-to-speech (TTS)-rendered speech, the controller family remains functional: SFT achieves the strongest accuracy, while the six-reward DAPO controller is the only learned variant whose final-think length falls below the base. These results suggest that a streaming model should learn when to make intermediate reasoning explicit during the audio stream.

</details>


### [167] [Not All Tokens Matter Equally: Dynamic In-context Vector Distillation with Decisive-Token Supervision for Long-form Medical Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27194)
*Ning Wu,Rui Liu,Xinkun Lin,Weixing Chen,Jinxi Xiang,Tao Wei,Lina Yao,Mingjie Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DIVE是一种针对长文本生成任务的轻量级蒸馏框架，通过增强关键token的监督和动态隐藏状态干预，显著提升医学报告生成的质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于token级别蒸馏的多模态方法在长文本生成中表现不佳，因其平等对待所有输出token，而忽略了决定生成质量的关键稀疏token（如病理相关词和EOS），且自回归解码易偏离教师轨迹。

Method: 提出DIVE框架，包含两个机制：1）关键token监督，提升病理相关token和EOS事件的交叉熵权重；2）状态条件动态引导，使用依赖隐藏状态的适配器替代固定残差，实现解码过程中的自适应干预。

Result: 在MIMIC-CXR和CheXpert Plus数据集上，结合两种医学视觉语言模型，DIVE在BLEU-4、ROUGE-L和RadGraph F1指标上均取得最佳表现，并在CheXbert F1上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: DIVE有效解决了长文本生成中监督不平衡和解码漂移问题，为冻结骨干模型下的高质量医学报告生成提供了高效方案。

Abstract: Distilling demonstration effects into hidden-space interventions offers a lightweight alternative to full finetuning. However, existing multimodal variants are mostly evaluated on short-form tasks, where outputs end after a few tokens. Extending these methods to long-form generation exposes a fundamental yet underexamined limitation: token-level distillation implicitly treats all output tokens as equally informative, but long-form outputs are dominated by high-frequency template and grammatical tokens, while the tokens that actually determine output quality are sparsely distributed. In medical report generation (MRG), two such decisive tokens stand out: pathology-related tokens that determine diagnostic content, and the end-of-sequence (EOS) event that determines termination. Both receive insufficient supervision under uniform cross-entropy, and autoregressive decoding further compounds the problem by drifting away from teacher-forced trajectories. We propose DIVE, a frozen-backbone distillation framework that addresses long-form report generation through two complementary mechanisms matched to these failures. Decisive-token supervision restores supervision balance by upweighting the cross-entropy contribution of pathology-related tokens and the EOS event, ensuring that content fidelity and termination are learned during training rather than imposed at decoding time. State-conditioned dynamic steering replaces fixed open-loop residuals with hidden-state-dependent adapters, allowing the injected signal to adapt as decoding drifts. Experiments on MIMIC-CXR and CheXpert Plus with two medical VLM backbones show that DIVE consistently ranks among the strongest methods across lexical and clinical-proxy metrics. Our method achieves the best BLEU-4, ROUGE-L, and RadGraph F1 in all dataset--backbone settings, while remaining competitive on coarse label-level CheXbert F1.

</details>


### [168] [EpiCurveBench: Evaluating VLMs on Epidemic Curve Digitization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27195)
*Thomas Berkane,Maimuna S. Majumder*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了EpiCurveBench和EpiCurveSimilarity（ECS）两个新工具，用于改进图表到数据提取任务的评估。EpiCurveBench包含1000张真实世界流行病曲线图像，而ECS是一种能容忍局部时间偏移并成比例惩罚误差的新评估指标。实验表明，当前最先进模型在ECS上的表现仍有较大提升空间，且ECS相比传统指标更能反映下游任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图表数据提取评估基准（如ChartQA）已接近饱和，前沿视觉-语言模型得分超过89%，同时现有评估指标将提取点视为无序键值对，无法处理时间序列中的时序结构问题，例如小的时间偏移即被判定为严重错误。因此需要更具有挑战性和现实意义的基准与更合理的评估指标，特别是在公共卫生等关键领域。

Method: 构建了一个包含1000张来自多样化公共卫生来源的真实流行病曲线图像的数据集EpiCurveBench，并提出EpiCurveSimilarity（ECS）作为新的评估指标。ECS利用动态规划对齐预测与真实时间序列，允许局部时间偏移和间隙，并按比例施加惩罚。评估了六种方法：三种前沿闭源视觉-语言模型、一种开源视觉-语言模型以及两种专用图表提取系统。同时将ECS与四种下游流行病学统计结果进行关联验证。

Result: 最强模型在ECS上仅达到52.3%的得分；ECS将四个通用视觉-语言模型拉开25分差距，而传统键值指标（RMS、SCRM）仅压缩在5分范围内。ECS与下游任务（总病例数、峰值时间、峰值大小和增长率保真度）误差的相关性比无间隙惩罚的动态时间规整（DTW）高1.5至3.6倍，能更好地区分截断预测与时间忠实预测。

Conclusion: EpiCurveBench和ECS为图表数据提取提供了更具挑战性、现实性和区分度的评估方案，尤其适用于流行病学等高影响力公共健康应用，同时也可推广至任何结构化时间序列图表提取场景。

Abstract: Chart-to-data extraction with vision-language models (VLMs) is increasingly evaluated on benchmarks that show diminishing headroom (frontier VLMs exceed 89% on ChartQA) and with metrics that treat extracted points as unordered key-value pairs, ignoring the temporal structure of time series and penalizing small alignment shifts as catastrophic failures. We address both gaps with EpiCurveBench, a benchmark of 1,000 real-world epidemic curve images curated from diverse public-health sources, and EpiCurveSimilarity (ECS), an evaluation metric that aligns predicted and ground-truth series via dynamic programming, tolerating local temporal shifts and gaps while penalizing them proportionally. Evaluating six methods--three frontier closed VLMs, one open VLM, and two specialized chart-extraction systems--we find the strongest model reaches only 52.3% ECS, and that ECS spreads the four general-purpose VLMs over a 25-point range where key-value metrics (RMS, SCRM) compress them into a 5-point band. We further validate ECS against four downstream epidemiological summary statistics, finding that higher ECS predicts smaller errors in total counts, peak timing, and peak magnitude, and higher growth-rate fidelity; across all four, ECS correlates 1.5--3.6 times more strongly than Dynamic Time Warping, which lacks a gap penalty and therefore cannot distinguish a truncated prediction from a temporally faithful one. EpiCurveBench targets a high-impact public-health application--unlocking decades of outbreak data trapped in published figures--but the benchmark and metric apply directly to any structured time-series chart-extraction setting.

</details>


### [169] [The Coverage Illusion: From Pre-retrieval Routing Failure to Post-retrieval Cascades in a Production RAG System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27220)
*Zafar Hussain,Kristoffer Nielbo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了现代RAG系统中查询增强方法（如HyDE）在真实生产流量中的实际开销与收益，发现在合成数据中表现良好的增强策略在真实场景下存在“覆盖率幻觉”（Coverage Illusion），即仅有27.8%的真实查询需要LLM增强。为此提出一种无需训练、按成本递增顺序执行的后检索级联策略，显著降低延迟和推理成本，同时提升整体效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代RAG系统对所有查询统一应用LLM驱动的查询增强（如HyDE），带来高昂的推理成本和延迟，但其在真实用户查询中的必要性缺乏实证支持，尤其是在合成数据与真实流量存在分布差异的情况下。

Method: 基于丹麦国家百科全书的2万条真实与合成查询-工作流对，评估五种检索流程；提出一种无需训练的后检索级联架构，按‘最便宜优先’原则执行检索步骤，仅在前序步骤无结果时才触发LLM增强。

Result: 发现合成查询显示90%以上需LLM增强，而真实流量中仅27.8%需要，揭示‘覆盖率幻觉’；现有预检索路由无法预测增强需求；所提级联策略相比Always-HyDE提升+0.140综合评分，降低31.8%延迟，并使72.2%真实查询免于LLM调用。

Conclusion: LLM查询增强不应默认应用于所有查询；‘覆盖率幻觉’源于合成与真实查询分布的结构性差异；后检索级联是一种高效、低成本的替代方案，可在不牺牲性能的前提下大幅减少LLM使用。

Abstract: In modern RAG pipelines, query augmentation methods such as HyDE and query expansion are applied to every query, resulting in substantial LLM inference costs and increased end-to-end latency. The empirical justification for this overhead in real production traffic remains largely unexplored. We present a case study of the Danish National Encyclopedia, evaluating five retrieval workflows over 20,000 query-workflow pairs from production traffic and synthetic conditions. In this system, synthetic queries suggest that LLM augmentation is needed for over 90% of queries to achieve high retrieval coverage. However, under our production deferral policy, only 27.8% of real user queries need LLM augmentation. We call this gap the Coverage Illusion and attribute it to a structural mismatch between synthetic and real query distributions. Pre-retrieval routing cannot resolve this gap, as the need for LLM augmentation is only revealed after searching the index, a result confirmed by our evaluation of four machine learning paradigms. The coverage gap, undetectable from the query alone, motivates a post-retrieval cascade that runs workflows in cheapest-first order and escalates to LLM augmentation only when a step returns no documents. Operating entirely without training overhead or secondary serving infrastructure, the cascade improves quality by +0.140 Composite Overall points over Always-HyDE, reduces latency by 31.8%, and serves 72.2% of real user queries without LLM augmentation.

</details>


### [170] [Temporal Simultaneity Predicts Annotation Quality in Sentiment Corpora](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27239)
*Idris Abdulmumin,Mokgadi Penelope Matloga,Tadesse Destaw Belay,Botshelo Kondowe,Letlhogonolo Mohleleng,Hareaipha Nkopo Letsoalo,Shamsuddeen Hassan Muhammad,Vukosi Marivate*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个包含3,565条推文的塞茨瓦纳语情感数据集，研究了长时间标注任务中间标者一致性（IAA）下降的原因，并发现时间同步性是影响一致性的主要因素，同时评估了多种多语言模型在该数据集上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在长期标注任务中维持标注质量具有挑战性，尤其是在标注人员有限的情况下；因此需要探究影响标注一致性的因素并提升非洲语言NLP资源的可复现性。

Method: 通过八个批次由三名母语标注者对推文进行情感标注，计算每批次的Randolph's自由边际Kappa系数，并进行六项针对性分析以探讨IAA变化原因；同时在三类情感分类任务上微调并评估多个开源多语言编码器及专有模型（如GPT-5和Gemini）。

Result: 总体κ=0.76，但各批次间κ值下降超过32点；标签混淆主要集中于负面/中性边界；两名标注者表现出随时间延长而出现的漂移现象；最影响κ的因素是标注时间的接近程度（1分钟内标注的κ=0.98，相隔一天以上则为0.65）；模型微调比预训练基线提升29至43个macro-F1点，GPT-5少样本设置下表现最佳（62.2 macro-F1）。

Conclusion: 标注时间的同步性显著影响标注一致性，建议未来非洲语言NLP资源建设中引入更精细的质量监控机制，并公开数据、时间戳与分析代码以支持可复现审计。

Abstract: Annotation quality is difficult to sustain when campaigns span weeks or months with small annotator pools. We present a Setswana sentiment dataset of 3,565 tweets annotated by three native-speaker annotators across eight batches and examine why inter-annotator agreement (IAA) declines over time. Despite an aggregate Randolph's free-marginal Kappa of $κ= 0.76$, "excellent," per-batch $κ$ falls by more than 32 points across the annotation task. Through six targeted analyses, we find that (i) label confusion concentrates on the negative/neutral boundary, (ii) two annotators show run-length drift consistent with autopilot labeling, and (iii) the dominant predictor of $κ$ is temporal simultaneity: tweets labeled within one minute achieve $κ= 0.98$, while those labeled more than a day apart reach only $κ= 0.65$. Annotation speed and tweet-level linguistic features show no meaningful association with $κ$. We benchmark three open multilingual encoders and proprietary models (GPT-5 and Gemini) on three-class sentiment classification; fine-tuning yields gains of 29 to 43 macro-F1 points over pretrained baselines, with GPT-5 few-shot leading overall (62.2 macro-F1). We release the dataset, per-annotation timestamps, and analysis code to support reproducible quality auditing for future African language NLP resources.

</details>


### [171] [ENPMR-Bench: Benchmarking Proactive Memory Retrieval for Emotional Support Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27240)
*Xing Fu,Yulin Hu,Mengtong Ji,Haozhen Li,Yixin Sun,Weixiang Zhao,Yanyan Zhao,Bing Qin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了ENPMR-Bench，一个用于评估情感需求感知的主动记忆检索（ENPMR）能力的基准，旨在提升语言智能体在情感支持应用中的共情交互能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多将记忆视为事实检索工具，忽视了其在塑造用户情感体验中的作用，特别是在识别和响应用户潜在情感需求方面存在不足。

Method: 基于马斯洛需求层次理论，构建包含1800多个记忆增强对话的数据集，并定义情感需求与支持性记忆类型之间的结构化映射，提出ENPMR任务并设计相应评测基准。

Result: 实验表明当前主流检索方法（包括嵌入式和基于大模型的方法）在共情得分上显著低于理想记忆条件；思维链提示虽有一定改善，但仍存在明显性能差距。

Conclusion: 当前语言智能体在情感需求敏感的记忆检索方面存在关键缺陷，需进一步发展面向个性化情感支持的、需求感知的记忆机制。

Abstract: Memory-augmented language agents are increasingly deployed in affective applications such as emotional support, where understanding and responding to users' latent emotional needs is critical. However, existing research often treats memory as a tool for factual retrieval, overlooking its role in shaping users' emotional experiences. In this work, we introduce ENPMR-Bench, a benchmark for evaluating Emotional Need-aware Proactive Memory Retrieval (ENPMR), a core capability that enables agents to infer users' latent emotional needs and proactively retrieve appropriate memories to support empathetic interaction. Grounded in Maslow's hierarchy of needs, ENPMR-Bench includes over 1,800 memory-augmented dialogues and defines structured mappings between emotional needs and supportive memory types. Experimental results demonstrate that current retrieval paradigms, including both embedding-based and LLM-driven approaches, exhibit substantial deficiencies, with empathy scores significantly lagging behind golden memory conditions. While chain-of-thought prompting improves the alignment between inferred emotional needs and retrieved memories to some extent, a notable performance gap remains. Together, these findings reveal critical limitations in current agents and outline directions for advancing personalized emotional support through need-sensitive memory retrieval.

</details>


### [172] [Pair-In, Pair-Out: Latent Multi-Token Prediction for Efficient LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27255)
*Wenhui Tan,Minghao Li,Xiaoqian Ma,Siqi Fan,Xiusheng Huang,Liujie Zhang,Ruihua Song,Weihang Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了Pair-In, Pair-Out (PIPO)方法，统一了输入侧的潜在压缩与输出侧的多令牌预测（MTP），并通过训练轻量级置信头避免验证开销，在保持可靠性的同时显著提升了解码效率和性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的长链思维推理方法在输入或输出侧分别优化，但两者独立发展且输出侧方法需昂贵的验证步骤来确认不可靠的预测令牌。因此需要一种统一且高效的方法来降低推理成本。

Method: 将潜在压缩器和MTP头视为镜像操作：压缩器将两个输入令牌折叠为一个潜在表示，MTP头将一个隐藏状态展开为额外输出令牌；同时训练一个轻量级置信头判断是否接受草稿令牌，并结合On-Policy Distillation（OPD）进行低成本训练。

Result: 在AIME 2025、GPQA-Diamond、LiveCodeBench v6和LongBench v2等多个基准上，使用Qwen3.5-4B和9B模型实验表明，PIPO相比常规解码最多提升+7.15点pass@4指标，并实现最高2.64倍的首令牌延迟和2.07倍的每令牌延迟加速。

Conclusion: PIPO有效统一了输入与输出侧的优化路径，去除了验证成本同时保证可靠性，显著提升了大语言模型的推理效率与生成质量。

Abstract: Long chain-of-thought reasoning has made autoregressive decoding the dominant inference cost of modern large language models. Existing methods target either the input side (latent compression) or the output side (speculative decoding and multi-token prediction, MTP), but the two lines of work have been pursued independently. Moreover, output-side methods must incur an expensive verifier pass to validate the unreliable draft tokens predicted by MTP. To address these issues, we propose \textbf{Pair-In, Pair-Out (PIPO)}, which unifies both sides by viewing a latent compressor and an MTP head as mirror-image operations: the compressor folds two input tokens into one latent representation, while the MTP head unfolds one hidden state into one additional output token. To remove the verifier cost without sacrificing reliability, PIPO trains a lightweight confidence head that decides whether draft tokens should be accepted. We observe that On-Policy Distillation (OPD) naturally matches the rejection-sampling criterion of speculative decoding, so the confidence head can be trained alongside OPD with negligible extra cost. Experiments on AIME 2025, GPQA-Diamond, LiveCodeBench v6, and LongBench v2 with Qwen3.5-4B and 9B backbones show that PIPO improves pass@4 over regular decoding by up to $+7.15$ points, while delivering up to $2.64\times$ first-token-latency and $2.07\times$ per-token-latency speedups.

</details>


### [173] [Lost in Sampling: Assessing Lexical Reachability in LLMs via the Word Coverage Score (WCS)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27268)
*Samer Awad,Javier Conde,Carlos Arriaga,Tairan Fu,Javier Coronado-Blázquez,Pedro Reviriego*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了Word Coverage Score (WCS) 来量化标准采样方法对人类语言词汇多样性的影响，揭示了解码机制如何抑制低频但有意义的词汇使用，导致生成文本同质化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型拥有庞大的词汇量，但生成的文本常显得重复和单一。本文旨在探究解码机制在其中的作用，特别是标准采样方法是否无意中过滤掉了合理的低频词汇选择。

Method: 提出了一种名为Word Coverage Score (WCS) 的新指标，用于衡量在不同采样参数下，上下文合理但低频的人类词汇被采样过滤器（如Top-p、Top-k、Min-p）剪枝的程度。通过在人类写作语料片段上审计开源模型来评估词汇存活率。

Result: 研究发现，工业界常用的标准采样默认设置会显著降低低频高信息量词汇的出现机会，即使这些词在概率分布中存在且语义合理，也会被解码器排除，从而导致语言表达的多样性和独特性被削弱。

Conclusion: 标准解码方法在追求文本连贯性的同时，可能充当了无意的审查机制，抑制了语言多样性。WCS为平衡生成文本的连贯性与词汇丰富度提供了可量化的诊断工具，有助于在未来优化生成模型的语言表现力。

Abstract: Modern Large Language Models (LLMs) are often criticized for producing repetitive and homogeneous text, despite possessing vast latent vocabularies. While previous research has focused on model knowledge and training data, we investigate the role of decoding mechanics in suppressing linguistic diversity. We introduce the Word Coverage Score (WCS), a metric that quantifies the extent to which contextually appropriate human vocabulary is mathematically pruned by standard sampling filters (e.g., Top-$p$, Top-$k$, and Min-$p$). Rather than assessing static knowledge, the WCS measures the lexical survival rate of low-frequency, high-information human words as a function of sampling parameters. By auditing open-weight models on human-authored corpus fragments, we identify which logical lexical choices are rendered unreachable by the decoder, even when they reside within the probability space. Our results provide quantitative evidence that industry-standard sampling defaults act as unintended censorship mechanisms, smoothing the unique textures of human expression into a homogenized discourse. The WCS offers a rigorous framework for optimizing the trade-off between text coherence and lexical richness, providing a diagnostic tool for preserving the diversity of human language in generative models.

</details>


### [174] [It's Not Always Sycophancy: Measuring LLM Conformity as a Function of Epistemic Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27288)
*Kevin H. Guo,Chao Yan,Avinash Baidya,Katherine Brown,Xiang Gao,Juming Xiong,Zhijun Yin,Bradley A. Malin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了MUSE框架，用于区分大语言模型（LLM）在面对用户反驳时的服从行为背后的两种机制：谄媚性服从和由认知不确定性驱动的服从，并揭示了用户专业性感知和建议合理性对这两种机制的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多将LLM对用户反馈的服从归因于强化学习中习得的谄媚行为，但作者认为推理时的认知不确定性也是重要因素，因此需要一个框架来分离这两种机制。

Method: 提出MUSE两阶段评估框架，通过映射模型在回答问题时的认知不确定性与其在后续轮次中屈服于用户反驳的可能性，来解构导致LLM服从的不同因素。

Result: 发现服从行为不仅源于谄媚，还受到模型自身不确定性的影响；识别出两种共同驱动服从的因素：即使确信初始答案仍迎合用户的‘谄媚性服从’，以及随不确定性增加而增强的‘不确定性驱动服从’；并通过消融实验证明两者均随用户专业性感知和建议合理性的提高而增强。

Conclusion: MUSE框架有助于更精准地区分由训练数据引发的不确定性和对齐过程中产生的谄媚行为，从而为改进模型可靠性提供更有针对性的干预策略。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are known to abandon their initial stance to conform to user pushback. While prior research largely attributes this behavior to sycophancy learned during reinforcement learning from human feedback, we hypothesize that conformity is also driven by a model's epistemic uncertainty at inference time. In this paper, we introduce MUSE, a two-stage evaluation framework to disentangle the mechanisms driving LLM conformity. Specifically, MUSE maps a model's epistemic uncertainty in responding to a query against its likelihood to yield to user pushback in a subsequent turn. We demonstrate that the mechanisms driving conformity extend beyond sycophancy alone. Specifically, we characterize two distinct factors that jointly drive conformity: sycophantic conformity, where a model aligns with user pushback even with absolute certainty in its initial response, and uncertainty-driven conformity, where a model's likelihood for conformity increases alongside its uncertainty. Furthermore, we conduct ablation studies to demonstrate that both sycophantic conformity and uncertainty-driven conformity grow with 1) the LLM's perceived expertise of the user and 2) the plausibility of the user's suggestions. More broadly, MUSE informs more targeted intervention strategies by distinguishing alignment-induced sycophancy and training-corpora-driven uncertainty.

</details>


### [175] [Separating Semantic Competition from Context Length in RAG Reading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27294)
*Vyzantinos Repantis,Ameya Gawde,Harshvardhan Singh,Rohit Alekar,Cien Zhang,Svetlana Karslioglu,Akash Vishwakarma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种匹配对照协议来研究检索增强生成（RAG）系统中读者模型的性能下降问题，发现性能损失主要源于竞争性干扰片段而非上下文长度，并通过实验验证了该效应在F1和答案包含指标上的显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究RAG系统中即使正确段落被检索到，模型仍可能出错的原因，区分是由于上下文长度还是竞争性干扰导致的读者模型失败。

Method: 引入并应用一种匹配对照协议，在保持段落数量和长度不变的情况下，将高竞争性干扰片段替换为低竞争性真实片段，并在SQuAD数据集上对两个紧凑开源模型进行评估；同时绘制保留曲线并使用右删失半衰期总结性能随竞争积累的变化趋势。

Result: 替换竞争性片段后性能部分恢复：Phi-2模型提升+6.0 EM、+7.0 答案包含、+0.057 F1；Qwen2.5-1.5B模型提升+4.5 EM、+9.0 答案包含、+0.068 F1；效果在F1和答案包含指标上更明显，且受片段长度影响。

Conclusion: 所提出的协议成功分离出独立于上下文长度的竞争效应，表明读者模型的性能下降至少部分归因于竞争性干扰片段的存在，尤其是在F1和答案包含率方面表现显著。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems can respond incorrectly even when the correct passage was retrieved. The model must still read the retrieved passages and identify which one contains the answer among others that look relevant. This passage-reading model is called the reader. Does it fail simply because the context is longer or because the other passages genuinely compete with the correct one? We introduce and demonstrate a matched-control protocol for RAG reading: we keep the number and length of passages fixed, but replace hard competitors with less competitive real passages. We apply this control across two compact open models on SQuAD. This replacement partially restores performance, with the strongest effects on F1 and answer inclusion. For Phi-2, this recovers +6.0 EM points, +7.0 answer-inclusion points, and +0.057 F1. For Qwen2.5-1.5B, it recovers +4.5 EM points, +9.0 answer-inclusion points, and +0.068 F1. To track how performance changes as competitors accumulate, we also report retention curves and summarize them with a right-censored half-life when the curves do not cross half-retention. Together, these results show the protocol isolates a competition effect distinct from context length, though the effect is clearer for F1 and answer inclusion than for exact match, and also varies with snippet length.

</details>


### [176] [Probing Cultural Awareness in LLMs: A Case Study of Cross-Culture Aesthetic Stylistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27296)
*Jiashuo Wang,Fenggang Yu,Jian Wang,Chak Tou Leong,Xiaoyu Shen,Chunpu Xu,Jiawen Duan,Wenjie Li,Johan F. Hoorn*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究提出了C4STYLI基准，用于评估大语言模型在识别和生成具有文化风格文本方面的能力，发现模型在理解香港地区特有风格结构方面存在局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨大语言模型在跨文化语境中对美学风格（即通过语言引发文化共鸣）的掌握程度，尤其是在高度风格化的文本中的表现。

Method: 构建了一个包含香港和中国大陆翻译电影标题及广告口号的风格化文本基准C4STYLI，并通过行为识别与生成能力测试评估大语言模型；使用逻辑回归探针进行结构消融分析。

Result: 实验表明，大语言模型在风格识别上与人类不同，且该能力因文本领域而异；风格识别与生成能力不一致；在香港语境下，模型主要依赖表层语言信息而非深层风格结构。

Conclusion: 大语言模型对特定区域（如香港）的风格结构敏感度有限，更多依赖表面特征进行风格识别，尚未真正掌握深层文化风格。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in diverse cultural contexts, yet their ability to master aesthetic stylistics, i.e., the strategic use of language to evoke cultural resonance, remains underexplored. We curate C4STYLI, a benchmark of highly stylized translated movie titles and advertising slogans from Hong Kong and the Chinese Mainland, to evaluate LLMs via the lens of behavioral recognition and productive competence. Extensive evaluations show that LLMs differ from humans in stylistic recognition, and this recognition ability varies across text domains. In addition, stylistic recognition and generation performance in LLMs are not consistently aligned. To further examine whether LLMs genuinely capture stylistic information in stylistic recognition, we conduct structural ablation with logistic regression probes. We find that, in the Hong Kong setting, stylistic recognition in LLMs relies primarily on surface-level linguistic information rather than stylistic structure. This suggests limited sensitivity to Hong Kong-specific stylistic structure.

</details>


### [177] [Self-Ensembling Vision-Language Models for Chart Data Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27298)
*Thomas Berkane,Qianyi Wang,Maimuna S. Majumder*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于视觉语言模型（VLM）自集成的图表到表格提取方法，通过多次采样和单元级聚合提升提取准确性，并引入收敛检测与不确定性估计，显著优于单次输出；同时构建了更复杂的WB-ChartExtract新基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图表数据提取方法在处理数据点多、样式多样的图表时性能有限，且手动数字化耗时易错，需更鲁棒的自动提取方法。

Method: 采用VLM多次采样生成多个候选表格，通过表对齐和数值型单元格取中位数进行聚合，引入收敛检测以停止采样，并提供基于样本离散度的不确定性估计。

Result: 在ChartQA和新构建的WB-ChartExtract基准上均提升了提取精度，在WB-ChartExtract上相对提升最高达23%；新基准图表平均数据点为ChartQA的7倍，更具挑战性。

Conclusion: 该方法有效提高了复杂图表的数据提取准确性和可靠性，推动了图像中锁定的表格数据的再利用。

Abstract: Charts effectively convey quantitative information, but the underlying data are often locked in image form, hindering reuse and analysis. Manually digitizing charts is time-consuming and error-prone, motivating automatic chart-to-table extraction. Recent approaches use specialized vision-language models (VLMs), yet performance still lags on charts with many datapoints or substantial stylistic variation. We propose a VLM self-ensembling method that repeatedly samples multiple tabular outputs from the same VLM for a fixed chart image and aggregates them at the level of individual table cells. We align candidate tables and take per-cell medians over numerical values to produce a more accurate consensus table. Our method also includes convergence detection to stop sampling once the aggregated table stabilizes, and uncertainty estimation based on dispersion across samples to help users assess extraction reliability. Because existing chart extraction benchmarks contain relatively simple plots with limited room for improvement, we introduce WB-ChartExtract, a new benchmark built from World Bank data with more complex and stylistically diverse charts; on average, its charts contain 7 times more datapoints than those in the ChartQA benchmark. Across both ChartQA and WB-ChartExtract, our approach improves extraction accuracy over single-pass VLM outputs, yielding up to 23% relative improvement on WB-ChartExtract after ensembling. More broadly, our method helps unlock tabular data previously siloed in chart images, enabling downstream analysis and reuse.

</details>


### [178] [Chartographer: Counterfactual Chart Generation for Evaluating Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27311)
*Yifan Jiang,Dae Yon Hwang,Jesse C. Cresswell,Freda Shi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于反事实图表的评估方法，用于严格检验视觉推理能力。通过构建可执行代码逆向工程生成图表变体，发现现有视觉语言模型在需要新视觉推理路径时难以泛化，暴露出单图表评估中隐藏的缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的图表问答基准容易让模型依赖捷径或先验知识回答问题，无法真正评估模型的视觉推理能力，因此需要一种更严格的评估方式。

Method: 提出了Chartographer框架，将图表逆向为可执行代码，验证重建保真度，生成可控的反事实图表变体，并基于可执行的问答逻辑推导新答案。

Result: 在现有图表问答数据集上应用该框架后发现，许多视觉语言模型虽然能正确回答原始图表问题，但在反事实变体上表现不佳，尤其是在需要新视觉推理路径的情况下。

Conclusion: 反事实图表能有效揭示视觉语言模型在视觉推理上的泛化缺陷，表明当前模型仍依赖表面线索而非真正的视觉理解。

Abstract: Chart question-answering (QA) benchmarks aim to pose questions that require visual reasoning to correctly answer, but models can often reach solutions through shortcuts or prior familiarity with a chart based on their own background knowledge. To strictly evaluate visual reasoning, we propose counterfactual charts where the chart-question task remains fixed, but underlying chart and the corresponding answer are varied. We introduce Chartographer, a framework to reverse engineer charts into executable code, validate reconstruction fidelity, generate seed-controlled counterfactual variants, and derive new answers from executable QA logic. We apply this framework to existing chart QA datasets and evaluate proprietary and open-source vision-language models (VLMs), measuring variation sensitivity and generalizability. Counterfactual charts reveal failures hidden by single-chart performance: VLMs often fail to generalize after answering the original chart correctly. We find failures are most prevalent when updated charts require novel visual reasoning pathways.

</details>


### [179] [When Does Demographic Information Help? Data and Modeling Regimes for Perspective-Aware Hate Speech Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27313)
*Weibin Cai,Reza Zafarani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文探讨了在仇恨言论检测等主观任务中，人口统计学特征在何种情况下对模型性能有帮助，并提出了一个门控人口统计残差模型以更有效地利用这些特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人口统计学信息在建模标注者视角时常被使用，但其效果不稳定，本文旨在探究其何时真正带来性能增益。

Method: 通过分析数据划分特性（如标注者分歧、训练规模、人口覆盖）和建模框架的影响，提出一种门控人口统计残差模型，将人口特征作为对纯文本预测的有选择性调整。

Result: 发现人口统计增益集中在低训练分歧、高测试分歧、足够训练数据和更大人口重叠等条件下；新模型在MHS和POPQUORN数据集上表现更好，尤其在高分歧或低置信度样本上。

Conclusion: 人口统计特征不应默认视为有用，其价值取决于数据环境和建模方式的共同作用。

Abstract: Demographic information is often used to model annotator perspectives in subjective tasks such as hate speech detection, but its benefit is inconsistent: it improves performance in some settings and behaves as noise in others. This paper asks when demographic features help. We analyze demographic gain as a function of both data split properties and modeling frameworks. For data splits, we measure annotator disagreement, namely how often annotators assign different labels to the same example, along with training size and train-test demographic coverage. We find that demographic gains concentrate in regimes with low training disagreement, high test disagreement, fine-grained ambiguity measurement, sufficient training data, and greater demographic overlap. Motivated by these regimes, we introduce a gated demographic residual model that treats demographics as a selective adjustment to text-only predictions. Experiments on MHS and POPQUORN show that this design is effective, especially on high disagreement or low confidence examples. Overall, our results suggest that demographics should not be assumed useful by default; their value depends jointly on the data regime and the modeling framework.

</details>


### [180] [Real Images, Worse Judgments: Evaluating Vision-Language Models on Concreteness and Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27315)
*Yifan Jiang,Ruoxi Ning,Sheng Yao,Freda Shi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 当前的视觉语言模型在进行词汇判断时，难以正确利用视觉信息，尤其在视觉无关或弱相关的情况下，真实图像上下文反而会降低模型与人类评分的一致性；通过提示模型仅关注文本内容可缓解这一问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验视觉输入是否真正有助于语言理解，特别是在词汇具体性和意象性判断中，视觉信息是否被合理利用。

Method: 使用人类的具体性与意象性评分作为标准，评估视觉语言模型在有无真实图像上下文情况下的表现，并通过探针分析、典型相关分析和归因研究探究模型表征变化。

Result: 真实图像上下文未带来一致提升，反而在视觉无关词上损害了模型与人类评分的对齐；模型表现出对伪视觉线索的敏感性和目标词汇属性恢复能力下降。

Conclusion: 当前基于指令调优的视觉语言模型需要更好校准视觉信息的使用时机，提示其在适当时候忽略视觉上下文以提升语言理解性能。

Abstract: Visual inputs are often assumed to improve language understanding in multimodal models. We examine this assumption by asking whether vision-language models (VLMs) can distinguish useful visual evidence from incidental image context in lexical judgments. We use human concreteness and imagery ratings because they span words with varying expected visual relevance, from abstract and low-imagery words to concrete and high-imagery words. We find that real-image contexts do not yield consistent gains and often hurt alignment with human ratings, most sharply when visual evidence is least relevant. Through probing and canonical correlation analysis, complemented by an attribution case study, we find that real-image contexts are associated with representational shifts and greater sensitivity to spurious visual cues, coinciding with weaker recoverability of the targeted lexical properties. We further show that instructing models to focus solely on textual content at inference time can reduce this degradation, with the clearest gains on these vulnerable subsets. Our findings suggest that current instruction-tuned VLMs need better calibration of when visual context should inform lexical judgments.

</details>


### [181] [Semantic Gradients Interactions in SSD: A Case Study in Racial Identity and Hate Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27322)
*Felix Ostrowicki,Hubert Plisiecki*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为interaction SSD的新方法，扩展了监督语义微分（SSD），用于建模语义意义如何在不同调节变量（如群体、特质或条件）下变化，并使其可检验和可解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的SSD方法无法有效捕捉语义判断中由群体特征等调节变量引起的差异，特别是在涉及敏感内容（如种族仇恨言论）的评判中，不同背景的标注者可能存在系统性判断偏差。因此需要一种能够量化并检验这种调节效应的语义分析工具。

Method: 通过扩展监督语义微分（Supervised Semantic Differential, SSD），引入主语义梯度、交互梯度和条件梯度，使语义结构的变化可建模与解释；利用标准SSD工具进行结果解读。

Result: 在UC Berkeley的仇恨言论语料库上的实验显示显著的调节效应：主梯度体现为非人化敌意与反制言论之间的对比，而交互梯度揭示了不同种族身份标注者在判断仇恨言论时所依赖的细微语义线索差异。

Conclusion: Interaction SSD成功实现了对语义-结果关系中调节效应的统计检验与解释，提升了对社会敏感语言判断中群体差异的理解能力。

Abstract: We introduce interaction SSD, an extension of Supervised Semantic Differential that models how semantic meaning varies across moderators such as groups, traits, or conditions making this variation testable and interpretable. The method estimates a main semantic gradient, an interaction gradient, and conditional gradients, all interpretable through standard SSD tools. We illustrate it on the UC Berkeley Measuring Hate Speech corpus, testing whether annotator racial identity moderates hate-speech judgments of comments targeting people of color. The interaction model detects a significant moderation effect: the shared gradient contrasts dehumanizing hostility with counter-speech, while the interaction gradient reveals smaller group-linked differences in which semantic cues predict hate-speech ratings. Interaction SSD makes moderated meaning-outcome relationships statistically testable and interpretable.

</details>


### [182] [MATCHA: Matching Text via Contrastive Semantic Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27345)
*Siran Li,Ece Sena Etoglu,Carsten Eickhoff,Seyed Ali Bahrainian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的自动评估指标MATCHA，通过结合语义一致性奖励和矛盾性惩罚，有效提升了对大语言模型输出的评估准确性，在多个基准测试中显著优于ROUGE和BERTScore等现有指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于词元重叠和嵌入相似度的评估指标（如ROUGE和BERTScore）常无法识别语义矛盾，导致对大语言模型性能的误判，因此需要一种能同时衡量语义一致性和检测矛盾的新指标。

Method: MATCHA采用双重视角：一是衡量生成文本与参考文本的接近程度，二是衡量其与对抗生成的矛盾文本的距离；通过联合优化这两个目标实现更可靠的自动评估。

Result: 在八个公开基准上，MATCHA在问答、图像描述生成、自然语言推理、摘要和语义文本相似性任务中均优于主流指标；在TruthfulQA数据集上，相比ROUGE-L和BERTScore分别提升18.38%和20.82%，且在23种嵌入模型对比中表现最优。

Conclusion: MATCHA是一种更准确、可靠的自动评估指标，能够有效区分正确与错误陈述，揭示了现有评估方法的根本缺陷，并为未来的大语言模型评估提供了新方向。

Abstract: Reliable evaluation is essential for understanding large language model (LLM) performance, yet today's go-to metrics, namely token-overlap scores (e.g., ROUGE) and embedding-based measures (e.g., BERTScore), often misjudge semantic similarity of documents. Our study shows that both token-overlap metrics and embedding-based metrics routinely assign nearly identical scores to texts that directly contradict each other, thereby potentially masking fundamental errors. We introduce MATCHA, an automatic metric that jointly rewards semantic agreement with a reference and penalizes contradictions. MATCHA employs a dual-view perspective that measures (i) proximity to the gold text and (ii) distance from an adversarially generated counterfactual contradiction. In eight public benchmarks, MATCHA outperforms popular metrics, compared with human annotations on question-answering, image caption generation, natural language inference, summarization, and semantic textual similarity tasks. On the TruthfulQA dataset (i.e., a dataset without a training set, where no embedding-based metrics could locally train on), this improvement in terms of matching texts with a reference reaches 18.38% over ROUGE-L and 20.82% over BERTScore. Both quantitative comparison and qualitative human assessments confirm the efficacy and validity of MATCHA and uncover fundamental weaknesses in pre-existing metrics. Compared with 23 embedding models, including top state-of-the-art ones, used as a metric similar to BERTScore, MATCHA remains the most accurate in distinguishing correct from incorrect statements solely based on a reference. Our code and metric are publicly available (https://github.com/Siran-Li/MATCHA).

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [183] [When Does Adaptive Guidance Help? Belief-Aware Privileged Distillation for Autonomous Driving Under Partial Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26155)
*Mehmet Haklidir*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了Belief-Aware GSAC (BA-GSAC)，通过集成分歧来自适应调节知识蒸馏系数lambda，系统研究了在何种情况下自适应引导真正有效，并揭示了观测盲区现象及其根源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始GSAC使用固定蒸馏系数lambda，未考虑智能体的不确定性，限制了在部分可观环境中的性能提升。因此需要一种能根据不确定性动态调整lambda的方法。

Method: 提出BA-GSAC，利用集成模型的预测分歧来动态调节lambda；在Highway-Env中评估五种策略（固定lambda、自适应、线性衰减、普通SAC），涵盖三种部分可观测难度级别，并分析不同设计对稳定性的影响。

Result: 在轻度和中度部分可观测环境下，自适应引导有一定优势；但在严重遮挡下，自适应系数迅速降至最小值，因集成模型仅基于可见信息预测导致‘可观测性盲区’；当前架构下，简单线性衰减策略表现最优（均值116.5，变异系数8.9%）；预热阶段显示出明显稳定作用（CV=13.3% vs. 29.8%）。

Conclusion: 自适应指导的效果依赖于集成模型的预测目标设计，当前方法因预测部分观测而产生盲区；建议未来工作应关注全状态预测以更好捕捉缺失信息；实践中，确定性调度（如线性衰减）可能比基于不确定性的自适应更有效且稳定。

Abstract: Guided Soft Actor-Critic (GSAC) distills knowledge from a privileged full-state teacher to a partial-observation student for autonomous driving, but uses a fixed distillation coefficient lambda regardless of the agent's uncertainty. We present Belief-Aware GSAC (BA-GSAC), which modulates lambda via ensemble disagreement, and use it as a testbed for a systematic empirical study asking: when does adaptive guidance actually help? Evaluating five strategies (fixed lambda in {0.01, 0.1}, adaptive, linear decay, and vanilla SAC) across three POMDP difficulty levels on Highway-Env, we find that preliminary single-seed runs suggest benefits under mild and moderate partial observability, but under severe occlusion (evaluated with 3 seeds for all methods) the adaptive coefficient collapses to lambda_min within about 3K steps. We trace this to an observability blindness phenomenon: because the ensemble predicts partial observations, it achieves low disagreement even under heavy occlusion, modeling what is visible but unable to detect what is missing. We diagnose the root cause and propose an architectural fix (training the ensemble on full-state predictions using the guiding actor's privileged access); while not validated here, we show that even with current limitations, the warmup phase provides measurable stabilization (CV=13.3% vs. 29.8% for constant lambda=0.01). In fact, a simple deterministic linear decay schedule achieves the best severe-POMDP performance across all metrics (mean 116.5, CV=8.9%), suggesting that the scheduling effect, not the ensemble, drives the stability benefit. These findings provide practical guidance for designing uncertainty-aware teacher-student frameworks and highlight ensemble prediction targets as an important design choice.

</details>


### [184] [PhyPush: One Push is All You Need for Sensorless Physical Property Estimation with Physics-Guided Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26284)
*Koyo Fujii,Luis Figueredo,Praminda Caleb-Solly,Ivan Boschi,Edoardo Ida',Marco Carricato,Aly Magassouba*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PhyPush是一种基于物理引导的Transformer框架，仅利用单次推动的运动学数据估计物体的质量和摩擦系数，无需专用传感器，提升了在未见场景下的泛化性和物理一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖力/扭矩传感器或触觉阵列等专用硬件来估计物体的物理属性，限制了机器人系统的可扩展性和部署能力。因此，需要一种仅使用常见机器人数据（如末端执行器速度）即可准确推断质量与摩擦的方法。

Method: 提出PhyPush框架，采用Transformer结构建模单次推动过程中的动力学关系，并引入基于牛顿第二定律和库仑摩擦模型的物理引导损失函数，以增强物理合理性和泛化能力。

Result: 在多种仿真和真实环境中，PhyPush在跨域条件下均实现了更精确的质量和摩擦估计；仿真中比拥有完整力信息的基线方法误差降低超过10%，在真实实验中优于纯数据驱动损失方法。

Conclusion: 物理引导的学习方法能够在仅使用标准机械臂运动学数据的情况下，实现低成本、高效率的物理属性估计，为实际机器人操作提供了可扩展且鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Accurately estimating object mass and friction is fundamental to achieving reliable and adaptive robotic manipulation. Although interactive perception provides a powerful mechanism for inferring such properties, most existing approaches depend on specialized hardware such as force/torque sensors, tactile arrays, or multi-camera motion-capture systems, limiting scalability and deployment. This paper presents PhyPush, a physics-guided Transformer framework that estimates an object's mass and friction coefficient using only kinematically derived end-effector velocity from a single push. This typically requires data available on standard robotic arms. The model incorporates constraints from Newton's second law and the Coulomb friction model through a physics-guided loss, improving physical consistency and generalization to unseen objects and surfaces. Across diverse simulation and real-world setups, PhyPush consistently achieves more accurate mass and friction estimation in challenging out-of-domain conditions. In simulation, it reduces error by over 10% compared with a baseline that has privileged access to full force information, while in real-world experiments, it outperforms a data-driven loss approach. Overall, the results demonstrate that physics-guided learning can enable low-cost, sensor-efficient estimation of physical properties, relying solely on a single push and readily available kinematic data.

</details>


### [185] [Collaborative Navigation and Exploration with $β$-Sparse Gaussian Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26304)
*Evangelos Psomiadis,Dipankar Maity,Panagiotis Tsiotras*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于异构机器人在未知环境中协同导航的框架，通过β-稀疏高斯过程和任务感知的诱导点选择，在带宽受限下优化地图信息传输与探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决异构机器人在未知环境中因感知、通信和计算限制带来的协同导航难题，提升导航效率并减少通信开销。

Method: 提出了β-稀疏高斯过程模型，实现任务感知的诱导点选择，并设计了平衡任务相关性与环境探索的动作选择策略，以在线联合优化传感器机器人的传输内容与导航行为。

Result: 在火星和地球地图的仿真中，相比无通信情况路径成本降低18%，相比原始数据传输 baseline 减少76%的信息传输量。

Conclusion: 所提框架能有效支持带宽受限下的移动传感器辅助导航，兼顾环境探索与任务需求，显著降低通信负担并提升导航性能。

Abstract: Collaborative navigation of heterogeneous robots in unknown environments poses significant challenges due to sensing, communication, and computational limitations. In this work, a lead robot navigates toward a target while a mobile sensor robot (e.g., a drone) assists by transmitting information about its locally observed environment under bandwidth constraints. We propose a framework that enables the sensor to jointly select its transmitted map points and navigation actions online, while also predicting unexplored regions of the environment. To this end, we present $β$-Sparse Gaussian Processes, a novel and robust variational sparse Gaussian Process model for task-aware inducing point selection. Furthermore, we develop an action-selection strategy that balances task relevance with exploration. Simulations on Mars and Earth maps show that the framework can reduce path cost by 18% relative to no communication and decrease transmitted information by 76% compared to raw-data transmission baselines.

</details>


### [186] [RCSP: Risk-Sensitive Conjectural Scenario Planning for Safe Dynamic Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26348)
*Zhengye Han,Quanyan Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为RCSP的预测性风险规划方法，用于解决移动机器人在动态瓶颈环境中的近失承诺问题，能够在保持安全的同时提升路径质量，但增加了一定延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动机器人在未发生碰撞前也可能因速度选择不当而陷入未来障碍物封闭的困境，现有方法难以有效预测和规避此类风险。

Method: 提出了Risk-Sensitive Conjectural Scenario Planning (RCSP)，通过维护局部运动假设的轻量级信念、采样未来交互场景、惩罚高风险尾部事件，并结合局部安全检查来评估候选指令。

Result: 在MuJoCo模拟中，RCSP实现了无碰撞到达目标，且在安全性和路径质量上优于非自适应预测器；在ROS2/Gazebo中，集成RCSP后减少了动态近失失败；但在DynaBARN/Jackal真实场景迁移中，调优后的DWA和TEB仍表现更优。

Conclusion: RCSP作为一种预测性风险模块，可补充现有导航系统，在动态瓶颈环境中提升安全性，但其性能边界仍受当前基准算法限制。

Abstract: Mobile robots can fail before they collide: a velocity that is safe now may commit the robot to a passage that moving obstacles will soon close. We study this predictive near-miss commitment problem and propose Risk-Sensitive Conjectural Scenario Planning (RCSP), a planning layer that evaluates candidate commands against plausible short-horizon obstacle futures. RCSP maintains a lightweight belief over local motion conjectures, samples future interactions, penalizes high-risk tails, and executes through a local safety check. In controlled MuJoCo bottleneck tasks, the RCSP planner reaches the goal without collisions and yields higher secondary safety and path-quality point estimates than a non-adaptive predictor, with additional latency. In ROS2/Gazebo, adding the local safety layer to a standard Nav2 stack reduces dynamic near-miss failures. On official DynaBARN/Jackal transfer, tuned DWA and TEB remain stronger on strict benchmark success, revealing the boundary of the approach. These simulation results position RCSP as a predictive-risk module that complements existing navigation stacks in dynamic bottleneck regimes.

</details>


### [187] [Closing the Loop in Teleoperation: Episode-Level Data Quality Assessment and Feedback for High-Quality Demonstration Collection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26349)
*Gokul Narayanan,Yash Shahapurkar,Melih Erdogan,Brian Zhu,Eugen Solowjow*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种数据质量评估与反馈（DQAF）框架，通过语义任务进展和机器人遥测数据为远程操作提供即时反馈，提升新手操作员生成高质量机器人演示数据的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着物理AI的发展，工业自动化对大规模真实机器人示范数据的需求增加，但新手操作员常产生次优的演示数据，影响下游任务效果，因此需要有效机制提升数据质量。

Method: 提出DQAF框架，从子任务进展、运动平滑性、停滞、运动学极限等信号中提取信息，生成结构化质量评估和可操作的自然语言反馈，形成闭环反馈系统。

Result: 在诊断验证研究中，系统与人工评审员表现一致；在针对三名新手操作员的初步用户研究中，接受反馈的操作员更快提升了演示质量。

Conclusion: DQAF框架能有效识别次优操作并提供可解释反馈，帮助操作员快速改进，从而提高远程操作数据的整体质量，推动高质量机器人学习数据集的构建。

Abstract: Industrial automation is at a pivotal moment, as Physical AI is driving a transition from rigid, hand-engineered automation systems toward more flexible and adaptive systems. This shift has created a growing demand for large-scale, real-world robot demonstration data, making teleoperation an increasingly important mechanism for data collection. However, high-quality teleoperated demonstrations remain difficult to obtain in practice, as novice operators often produce episodes that are task-successful but suboptimal for downstream use due to inefficient motion, repeated corrections, or operation near robot joint limits. We present a Data Quality Assessment and Feedback (DQAF) framework that closes the loop in teleoperation by providing immediate post-episode feedback grounded in semantic task progress and robot telemetry. The framework extracts quality relevant signals such as sub-task progress, motion smoothness, stalls, kinematic limits and converts them into structured quality assessments and actionable natural-language feedback. Unlike binary success or failure feedback, the proposed system explains why an episode is suboptimal and highlights specific behaviors to correct in the next trial. We evaluate the framework through a diagnostic validation study and a pilot user study. In the validation study, the system is compared with a human reviewer during dataset curation, producing rejection reasons and actionable feedback for improvement. In the pilot study with three novice operators across two manipulation tasks, the operator who received the systems immediate, automated post-episode feedback improved faster than those who did not, producing higher-quality demonstrations sooner.

</details>


### [188] [Multi-Robot Box Transport over Different Surfaces with Decentralized Role-based Proportional Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26430)
*Aditya Bhatt,Himavarshini Yarragangu,Urvish Shah,Venkata Sai Yaswanth Mohan Thota,Souma Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为R2P2的异步去中心化任务与运动规划方法，用于多机器人在不同地形上协作推动物体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决在不同摩擦和坡度表面上协作运输物体时面临的挑战，避免通信、同步和单点故障问题。

Method: 通过基于规则分配角色（如推动、支撑、防转），结合比例控制或规则控制机器人速度，每个机器人根据自身和箱子的位置与航向执行任务。

Result: 在NVIDIA IsaacSim仿真环境中验证了六机器人团队的表现，显示出对不同表面摩擦、坡度和箱子质量的良好泛化能力，并优于传统的虚拟领导跟随法；同时在四台TurtleBot物理实验中成功搬运1.2kg箱子。

Conclusion: R2P2方法具有良好的适应性和鲁棒性，适用于多种环境下的多机器人协作运输任务。

Abstract: Collaborative transport of objects via pushing by multiple robots has many applications, ranging from construction and warehouse environments to post disaster debris clean-up. Achieving collaborative transport over surfaces with different inclination and friction properties however poses unique challenges. To address these challenges, this paper presents an asynchronous decentralized task and motion planning approach for transporting rectangular boxes of varying mass over flat, uphill and downhill terrain. Such a decentralized approach alleviates communication, synchronization and consensus needs and mitigates single point of failure issues. Our approach, called R2P2 or Roles with Rules and Proportional-control Primitive, assigns roles (e.g., push, support and prevent) to robots based on rules cognizant of the mode of manipulation needed (box rotation vs translation); this is followed by either rule-based control or proportional control of robot velocity based on the roles. Each robot is assumed to observe the location and heading of self and the box in executing the role and controls. R2P2 is evaluated with a six-robot team deployed in a simulator built using NVIDIA IsaacSim -- demonstrating generalizability across different surface friction/inclination and box mass scenarios, and better success rate compared to a standard virtual-leader-follower method. R2P2 is also successfully validated with a physical experiment, where it is executed onboard four turtlebots tasked with moving a 1.2 kg box.

</details>


### [189] [Efficient On-policy Visual-RL via Stochastic Decoupled Policy Gradient](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26478)
*Haoxiang You,Yilang Liu,Davis Zong,Qian Wang,Teeratham Vitchutripop,Qi Wang,Daniel Rakita,Ian Abraham*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了随机解耦策略梯度（SDPG），一种轻量级的视觉强化学习方法，可在单个GPU上几小时内端到端训练多样化的视觉运动控制策略，并在视觉MuJoCo基准上显著优于基线方法，同时引入了新的真实感视觉机器人基准以支持未来研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了降低视觉强化学习在计算和内存开销方面的高成本，实现在单一消费级GPU上快速高效地训练复杂的视觉运动策略。

Method: 通过轨迹 rollout 的随机扰动来估计策略梯度，采用随机扰动策略梯度（SDPG）方法，减少批量渲染环境的数量，实现训练过程的解耦与轻量化。

Result: 在视觉MuJoCo基准测试中，SDPG在训练时间、内存使用和奖励表现上均优于基线方法；成功实现了从仿真到真实硬件的有效迁移，并推出了一套涵盖灵巧操作和复杂移动任务的真实感视觉机器人基准。

Conclusion: SDPG是一种高效、低资源消耗的视觉强化学习方法，能够在短时间内训练高性能的视觉运动策略，具备良好的实际应用潜力和推广价值。

Abstract: We present the stochastic decoupled policy gradient (SDPG), a lightweight visual reinforcement learning (RL) method that trains diverse visuomotor control policies end-to-end within a few hours on a single NVIDIA RTX 4080 GPU. SDPG estimates policy gradients via random perturbations of trajectory rollouts, requiring orders of magnitude fewer batch-rendered environments and substantially reducing compute and memory overhead. On visual MuJoCo benchmarks, SDPG consistently outperforms baseline methods in training time, memory usage, and rewards. Finally, to support future research, we introduce a suite of realistic visual robotics benchmarks spanning dexterous manipulation, challenging locomotion, and demonstrate effective sim-to-real transfer on physical hardware.

</details>


### [190] [Provably Safe Motion Planning Under Unknown Disturbances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26625)
*Ibon Gracia,Qi Heng Ho,Luca Laurenti,Morteza Lahijanian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于采样的运动规划算法，利用Wasserstein模糊集管学习系统状态分布的不确定性，并在未知扰动分布下保证安全性，适用于高维复杂环境中的机器人系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在存在未知分布随机扰动的情况下，如何为受状态和控制约束的机器人系统提供安全且高效的运动规划方法，尤其是在复杂环境中满足概率安全性要求。

Method: 通过系统轨迹数据构建Wasserstein模糊集管（ambiguity tube），将学习到的低维模糊集序列用于采样规划树的增长；采用bandit-based有效性检查器提升算法效率，同时保持概率完备性。

Result: 算法能够在高障碍密度环境下、严格的安全阈值下找到有效的路径，相比现有最先进方法表现出更优性能，且降低了保守性并提升了可扩展性。

Conclusion: 该方法在未知扰动分布条件下实现了高效、安全的概率完备运动规划，通过分解高维模糊集显著提升计算效率与实用性。

Abstract: We present a provably safe sampling-based motion planning algorithm for robotic systems affected by random disturbances of unknown distribution. We consider systems with linear or linearizable dynamics evolving in workspace with arbitrary-shaped obstacles subject to state and control constraints. Safety requirements are formulated as chance-constraints. Our approach leverages data from trajectories of the system to learn a Wasserstein ambiguity tube, i.e., a sequence of ambiguity sets, which contains the trajectory of the system's state distribution with high confidence. This ambiguity tube is then used in a probabilistically complete algorithm to grow a sampling-based motion planning tree that respects the constraints of the problem. We show that learning several lower-dimensional ambiguity tubes instead of a single high-dimensional one effectively reduces the conservatism and boosts scalability. Additionally, we design an efficient bandit-based validity checker that remarkably increases the empirical performance of our approach without sacrificing probabilistic completeness. Case studies show our algorithm finds valid plans in cluttered environments under strict safety thresholds, outperforming state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [191] [Enabling Extensible Embodied Capabilities with Tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26637)
*Xueyang Zhou,Zijia Wang,Qianjiang Li,Yibo Hu,Guiyao Tie,Li Wan,Yidan Liu,Pan Zhou,Lichao Sun,Yongchao Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种能力外化方法，通过将异构能力解耦为独立优化的工具，并引入具身工具协议（ETP）和EmbodiedToolBench评测基准，验证了该方法在认知与感知任务中显著提升性能，但在执行类能力上增益有限，同时揭示了工具调用时机、选择与组合仍是现有模型的重大挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身智能方法将感知、推理、规划和控制统一于单一参数化策略中，但由于这些能力本质上是分层且异构的，难以在一个模型内可靠地学习和模块化。

Method: 提出能力外化方法，将异构能力解耦为可独立优化的工具，在推理时动态调用；设计标准化的具身工具协议（ETP）用于工具注册、发现、调用与执行，并构建包含100多个验证工具的工具库及评测平台EmbodiedToolBench，评估工具增强对具身性能的影响以及模型在工具使用方面的能力。
