Result: 实验表明，能力外化在仿真和真实平台上一致提升了具身性能（EB-ALFRED平均提升31%，EB-Navigation提升36%），但提升主要来自认知与感知类工具，执行类工具增益有限；分析发现当前模型在何时、何种类别及如何调用工具方面仍存在显著困难。

Conclusion: 能力外化是提升具身智能的有效路径，尤其适用于认知与感知任务；而提升模型的工具使用判断力与组合能力——即具身工具素养——是未来研究的关键方向。

Abstract: Most existing embodied intelligence methods formulate perception, reasoning, planning, and control within a unified parameterized policy. Yet these capabilities are inherently hierarchical and heterogeneous, making them difficult to reliably learn and modularize within a single model. We propose a capability externalization approach that decouples heterogeneous capabilities into independently optimized tools, dynamically invoked at inference time. To this end, we introduce Embodied Tool Protocol (ETP), a standardized protocol for embodied tool registration, discovery, invocation, and execution, and curate 100+ validated tools spanning perception, cognition, reasoning, and execution as the tool base. Building on this, we construct EmbodiedToolBench to evaluate both whether tool augmentation improves embodied performance and how well current models use tools across tool-necessity recognition, tool selection, tool execution, and tool-chain composition. Experiments across simulation and real-world platforms confirm that capability externalization consistently improves embodied performance (avg. gain 31% on EB-ALFRED and 36% on EB-Navigation), yet reveal a clear boundary: gains are substantial for cognition and perception but are limited for execution-type capabilities. Moreover, our analysis reveals that knowing when, which, and how to invoke tools remains a persistent challenge across all models, thereby highlighting embodied tool competence as a critical direction for future research.

</details>


### [192] [HyperSim: A Holistic Sim-To-Real Framework For Robust Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26638)
*Junyi Dong,Haotian Luo,Ziwei Xu,Shengwei Bian,Heng Zhang,Sitong Mao,Jingyi Guo,Yang Xu,Wenhao Chen,Qiuyu Feng,Yao Mu,Ping Luo,Shunbo Zhou,Xiaodong Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HyperSim是一个从合成数据生成到策略训练和现实世界部署的完整框架，通过高保真环境合成、对抗性轨迹生成和仿真与真实联合训练三大核心模块，有效缩小仿真到现实的领域差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于仿真与现实之间的领域差异，将机器人操作策略从仿真转移到现实世界仍然具有挑战性，而合成数据生成虽然可以扩展数据量和多样性，但如何有效弥合这一领域差距是关键问题。

Method: 提出HyperSim框架，包含三个核心部分：高保真环境合成以提升视觉保真度，对抗性轨迹生成以扩展数据覆盖范围，以及仿真与真实联合训练以学习领域不变表示。

Result: 在400次真实世界任务执行的大规模实证研究中，使用ACT和π₀模型分别达到80%和95%的仿真到现实成功率；在物理扰动下，对抗性轨迹训练的策略完成率高出35%。

Conclusion: HyperSim通过系统化的方法显著提升了仿真到现实的迁移性能和策略鲁棒性，为具身智能的规模化发展提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Scaling data volume and diversity is critical for generalizing embodied intelligence. While synthetic data generation offers a scalable alternative to expensive physical data acquisition, transferring robotic manipulation policies from simulation to the real world (sim-to-real) remains a formidable challenge due to the domain gap. This paper presents HyperSim, a holistic framework spanning from synthetic data generation to policy training and seamless real-world deployment. To systematically bridge the sim-to-real gap, HyperSim is realized through three core pillars: high-fidelity environment synthesis, adversarial trajectory generation, and sim-and-real co-training. Collectively, these modules address domain discrepancies by enhancing visual fidelity, expanding data coverage, and enforcing domain-invariant representations. We rigorously validate HyperSim through a large-scale empirical study involving 400 real-world task executions across two representative manipulation models. Assessed across three fine-grained metrics, our complete pipeline achieves remarkable sim-to-real success rates of 80% and 95% with ACT and π_{0}, respectively. Furthermore, policies trained on our adversarial trajectories exhibit significantly enhanced robustness against dynamic uncertainties, achieving a 35% higher completion rate under physical perturbations.

</details>


### [193] [L-Learning : A Lyapunov-Based Approach Leveraging Lagrangian Mechanics for Efficient and Stable Robot Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26648)
*Quan Quan,Hao Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: L-Learning 是一种结合李雅普诺夫稳定性理论与拉格朗日力学的数据驱动控制框架，用于提升机器人轨迹跟踪性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统控制方法在动态和不确定环境中性能下降，而现有数据驱动方法常受限于高样本复杂性和缺乏严格的稳定性保证。

Method: 通过从数据中显式学习系统的能量函数，将李雅普诺夫稳定性理论与拉格朗日力学相结合，实现闭环稳定性和高性能控制。

Result: L-Learning 具有更高的控制精度、理论稳定性保证和高样本效率。

Conclusion: L-Learning 为实际机器人应用提供了一种有前景的解决方案。

Abstract: This paper presents L-Learning, a novel data-driven control framework for robotics that integrates Lyapunov stability theory with Lagrangian mechanics to enhance trajectory tracking performance. While traditional control methods often suffer from performance degradation in dynamic and uncertain environments, data-driven approaches, while more adaptable, are frequently limited by high sample complexity and a lack of rigorous stability guarantees. L-Learning mitigates these challenges by explicitly learning the system's energy function from data, thereby optimizing performance while ensuring closed-loop stability intrinsically. Characterized by superior control accuracy, theoretical stability guarantees, and high sample efficiency, L-Learning represents a promising solution for practical robotic applications.

</details>


### [194] [On the Generalization Capabilities, Design Choices and Limitations of Keypoint Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26649)
*Thomas Lips,Marco Moletta,Michael C. Welle,Danica Kragic,Francis wyffels*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于视觉基础模型的关键点提取在机器人操作中的模仿学习应用，提出了关键点模仿学习（KIL）方法，并通过大量真实实验验证其在未见物体和场景变化下的泛化能力。结果显示KIL数据效率高，整体性能优于RGB基线，与S2-diffusion相当，但受限于基础模型的关键点提取能力，且未超越其他表示方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RGB-based imitation learning需要大量示范才能泛化到新场景或物体，因此需要更高效的中间表示来提升泛化性能。视觉基础模型支持单次提取关键点，有望作为有效表示，但如何最优整合进模仿学习尚不明确。

Method: 结合已有关键点模仿学习（KIL）方法，系统评估多种设计选择，提出实用指南；使用超过2000次真实世界 rollout 评估KIL在五项任务中对未见物体和场景的泛化能力，并与RGB基线和S2-diffusion等方法对比。

Result: KIL在五项任务中达到75%的整体成功率，显著优于RGB基线（47%），与S2-diffusion（73%）相当；验证了KIL的数据效率和泛化能力，但也揭示其受限于关键点基础模型的提取能力，尤其在多物体实例任务中表现有限。

Conclusion: KIL是一种数据高效的机器人学习方法，能有效利用视觉基础模型提供的关键点表示，但在当前技术下未能超越其他先进表示方法，且性能受基础模型局限性影响，未来需改进关键点提取的鲁棒性和适应性。

Abstract: RGB-based imitation learning requires many demonstrations to generalize to unseen objects or scenes, motivating research into intermediate representations to improve generalization for robotic manipulation. Visual foundation models enable one-shot extraction of keypoints to provide such representation. However, it remains unclear how to integrate them into imitation learning optimally and when they outperform alternative representations. We combine approaches from previous works on keypoint imitation learning (KIL) and investigate several design choices to provide practical guidelines. Using over 2000 real-world rollouts, we also assess the generalization capabilities of KIL to unseen objects and scene variations. KIL achieves a 75% overall success rate across five tasks, significantly outperforming the RGB baseline (47%) and performing on par with S2-diffusion (73%). Finally, we explore the limitations of the foundation models used for keypoint extraction and extend KIL to tasks with multiple object instances. Our results confirm KIL as a data-efficient approach for robot learning, though it does not outperform alternative representations and inherits limitations of the foundation models used for keypoint extraction. All rollout videos, demonstrations, and results are available at https://kil-manipulation.github.io/.

</details>


### [195] [SteelDS: A High-Resolution Video Dataset of E40 Steel Scrap for Object Detection and Instance Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26682)
*Melanie Neubauer,Christian Rauch,Gerald Koinig,Alexia Tischberger-Aldrian,Roland Pomberger,Elmar Rueckert*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该数据集包含高分辨率、带标注的钢和铜废料视频序列，适用于机器学习模型开发与自动分拣算法评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为解决工业中磁选后需人工去除铜杂质的问题，推动自动化分拣技术发展。

Method: 在受控实验室环境中采集24,297个标注帧，涵盖五种不同密度和间距的子集，并提供像素级分割掩码和材料类别标注。

Result: 数据集包含396个钢件和101个铜件，按尺寸分类，可模拟真实工业分拣场景。

Conclusion: 该数据集可作为评估自动化分拣算法识别复杂钢屑流中铜杂质性能的基准。

Abstract: This dataset provides high-resolution, annotated video sequences of shredded E40-grade steel and copper scrap on a conveyor belt. Captured in a controlled laboratory environment, the data reflects the industrial post-magnetic sorting stage, where manual intervention is typically required to remove copper contaminants. The dataset comprises 24,297 labeled frames across five subsets, featuring 396 steel and 101 copper objects categorized by size. It supports the development of machine learning models for material classification, object detection, and instance segmentation. Variations in object spacing and density are included to simulate realistic industrial sorting conditions. Ground truth annotations include pixel-wise segmentation masks and material classes. This dataset serves as a benchmark for evaluating automated sorting algorithms aiming to identify copper impurities within complex, heterogeneous steel scrap streams.

</details>


### [196] [Look Further: Socially-Compliant Navigation System in Residential Buildings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26710)
*Akira Shiba,Marina Obata,Nathan Kau,Zoltan Beck,Rishi Shah,Michael Sudano,Sabrina Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Proactive Lane-Changing (PLC)的运动模式，通过在八米远距离提前避让行人，提升室内配送机器人在走廊环境中的人机交互体验。实验表明，该方法在直道场景中显著改善了机器人运动的安全性、流畅性和礼貌性感知，但在盲区交叉口场景中未表现出明显优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的社交导航方法主要关注近距离（几米内）的人机冲突避免，但忽略了远距离反应对人类感知的影响。本文旨在探索延长机器人反应距离是否能够提升人类对其运动行为的感知质量。

Method: 设计并实现了一种Proactive Lane-Changing (PLC)运动模式，使机器人在距离行人八米时即从走廊中央向侧边移动，以实现更自然的避让。开发了支持该模式的导航系统，并通过包含42名参与者的用户研究，在直道和盲角交叉口两种场景下评估机器人在安全性、流畅性和礼貌性方面的表现。

Result: 在直道场景（Frontal Approach）中，PLC模式在安全性、流畅性和礼貌性方面均显著优于传统的减速、停止和近距避障等行为；而在盲角交叉口（Blind Corner）场景中，不同方法之间无显著差异，参与者偏好呈现多样化。

Conclusion: 延长机器人对行人的反应距离至八米以上，通过PLC模式可有效提升人类对机器人运动的正面感知，尤其适用于直道环境中的服务机器人。然而，在复杂视野受限的交叉口场景中，单一策略难以满足所有用户偏好，需进一步研究个性化或自适应策略。

Abstract: The distance at which a mobile robot reacts to a person strongly impacts various qualities of the human-robot interaction. In this paper, we focus on the navigation of a mobile delivery robot platform in a residential indoor hallway environment. Social navigation methods typically focus on avoiding uncomfortable human-robot interactions, such as when a robot encroaches on someone's personal space. Since personal space has been shown to be in the range of just a few meters, social navigation methods typically focus on deconflicting and resolving these short-range interactions. In this work, however, we demonstrate that by extending the reaction distance to over eight meters, far beyond the typical interaction distance, we can improve the human's perception of the robot's motion. We introduce the Proactive Lane-Changing (PLC) motion pattern and a navigation system that leverages it to react to people at an increased distance. This pattern consists of changing the robot's lateral position as it navigates down the hallway from the center to the side at an eight-meter distance from an oncoming person.
  We conducted a user study with 42 participants to assess their impressions of the delivery robot based on three service objectives: safety, smoothness, and politeness. In the straight hallway scenario (Frontal Approach), results showed significant improvement in each of these three objectives compared to typical motion patterns found in the literature: slowing down, stopping, and reactive collision avoidance in the proximity of a person. In contrast, in the intersection (Blind Corner) scenarios, none of the approaches performed significantly better than any other, with participants having a diverse range of preferences among robot motion patterns.

</details>


### [197] [Manipulating Tangible Virtual Object Dynamics to Promote Learning of Precision Force Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26782)
*Alberto Garzás-Villar,Alba Riera-Cardona,Alexis Derumigny,J. Micah Prendergast,Jane Murray Cramm,Laura Marchal-Crespo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了通过虚拟现实中的非线性弹簧动力学训练精确力控制的效果，发现反称高斯弹簧有助于提高健康受试者的力生成准确性，但长期保持效果无差异，且参与者主要依赖伸长感知而非力感知，提示需优化以减少对本体感觉的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精细力生成是中风康复中的关键但被忽视的方面，现有训练方法不足，因此探索结合虚拟现实和机器人触觉设备的新训练策略具有重要意义。

Method: 在一项包含50名健康参与者的实验中，采用冰壶启发的任务，要求拉伸具有不同力-伸长关系（线性、高斯、反称高斯）的虚拟弹簧以达到目标释放力；通过比较不同弹簧类型下的训练表现、保留效果及个性特征影响进行分析，并设置转移任务评估控制策略。

Result: 反称高斯组在整个训练过程中力准确性始终高于线性组，高斯组仅在训练后期表现出优势；个性特质分析显示‘自由精神’得分高者在高斯条件下表现较差，‘挑战转化’得分高者探索更多；所有组在长期保留测试中无显著差异；转移任务表明参与者主要依赖目标伸长而非目标力进行控制。

Conclusion: 操纵虚拟物体的力学特性可有效提升力控制训练中的即时表现，尤其是使用反称高斯弹簧，但未能改善长期保留效果；当前方法仍依赖于本体感觉线索，需进一步优化后才适用于神经康复患者。

Abstract: Robotic haptic devices combined with virtual reality offer novel opportunities to train fine force generation, an essential yet overlooked component of post-stroke rehabilitation. This study proposes that manipulating the rendered dynamics of tangible virtual objects can be leveraged to train precise force control while engaging the somatosensory system. We conducted an experiment with fifty healthy participants who performed a curling-inspired task in which they had to stretch a virtual spring to generate a target release force to propel the stone to a predefined location on the ice sheet. During training, the spring's force-elongation relationship was modeled as either a linear or non-linear function, i.e., a Gaussian or antisymmetric Gaussian (AS-Gaussian) function with zero derivative at the release target force. Results indicate that the AS-Gaussian group consistently achieved higher force accuracy during training than the linear group, while the Gaussian group only outperformed the linear group toward the end of training. Analysis of personality traits revealed that higher Free Spirit scores were associated with poorer performance and reduced task exploration under Gaussian dynamics, whereas higher Transform-of-Challenge scores correlated with increased exploration. Despite these training effects, no significant differences in long-term retention were found across spring types or personality traits. Participants primarily relied on learned target elongation rather than target force, as evidenced by performance in a transfer task with a different stiffness but the same target force. While promising for somatosensory neurorehabilitation, these methods require refinement to reduce reliance on proprioceptive cues before testing with neurological patients.

</details>


### [198] [Can VLA Models Learn from Real-World Data Continually without Forgetting?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26820)
*Jiarun Zhu,Yijun Hong,Xiaoquan Sun,Zetian Xu,Mingqi Yuan,Zhiyong Wang,Wenjun Zeng,Jiayu Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究首次对现实世界中视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的持续学习进行了实证研究，构建了包含四个连续操作任务的真实世界数据集，并系统评估了经验回放方法在缓解灾难性遗忘中的有效性，为长期运行的机器人策略部署提供了实践指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLA模型在持续学习新技能时容易遗忘旧知识，尤其在真实复杂环境中这一问题尚未充分研究。为此，本文旨在探索现实条件下VLA模型的持续学习能力，并填补模拟环境与现实应用之间的差距。

Method: 构建了一个包含四种顺序操作任务（刚性物体抓放、接触丰富的按压、可变形物体折叠）的真实世界持续学习数据集；基于该数据集，全面实验评估了VLA模型在异构真实演示下的持续学习表现，并系统分析了经验回放策略及其关键实现因素的影响。

Result: 实验发现，VLA模型在持续学习真实世界异构演示时会出现严重的灾难性遗忘现象；经验回放在适当实现下能有效缓解该问题，其成功依赖于存储容量、重放频率和训练策略等关键因素。

Conclusion: 这是首个针对现实世界VLA模型持续学习的实证研究，验证了持续学习在真实机器人场景中的挑战与潜力，为未来长期部署的通用机器人策略提供了实用建议和基准基础。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models provide a promising foundation for general-purpose robotics. However, their successful deployment in real-world scenarios requires the ability to continually acquire new skills while retaining previously learned behaviors. While pioneering research has studied the continual learning of VLA models in narrowly simulated environments, this challenge remains largely unexplored under realistic conditions. To address this limitation, we construct a real-world continual learning dataset comprising four sequential manipulation tasks, spanning rigid-object pick-and-place, contact-rich pressing, and deformable-object folding. Using this dataset, we conduct comprehensive experiments and find that VLA models suffer significant catastrophic forgetting when continually learning from heterogeneous real-world demonstrations. We then systematically evaluate experience replay and uncover key implementation factors that govern its success. In summary, this work provides the first empirical study of real-world continual VLA learning and offers practical guidance for deploying long-lived robot policies.

</details>


### [199] [Learning Compositional Symbolic Task Rules from Demonstrations with Inductive Logic Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26828)
*Oleh Borys,Karla Stepanova*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于归纳逻辑编程（ILP）的分层学习方法，用于从示范中学习机器人任务的高层结构，实现了可解释、可重用的任务表示，并在积木组装场景中验证了其泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了使机器人具备自主性，需要从示范中不仅学习任务执行方式，还要捕捉可检查、可重用且人类可理解的高层任务结构。

Method: 采用归纳逻辑编程（ILP），将复杂任务分解为多个不同抽象层次的简单学习目标，利用示范和先验知识推断符号规则，并在学习高层结构时复用已学规则。

Result: 在合成的积木组装场景中，系统能够从示范中学习到可解释的抽象规则，并成功泛化到包含未见物体的更难任务上。

Conclusion: 分层的ILP方法是任务级示教学习的一种可行途径，支持强泛化和人类可理解的表示。

Abstract: Learning from Demonstration~(LfD) should capture not only how a task is executed, but also its high-level task structure that explains the demonstrated behavior. As robots become more autonomous, such task representations must be inspectable, reusable, and human-interpretable. To address this, we study how to represent and learn robotic tasks with inductive logic programming~(ILP) by decomposing a complex task into a series of simpler learning objectives at different abstraction (ontological) levels. The system infers symbolic rules from demonstrations and prior (domain) knowledge, and reuses learned rules when learning higher-level task structure. We evaluate the approach in a synthetic block-assembly scenario and show that the learned abstractions are interpretable and support strong generalization to harder, held-out tasks with unseen objects. These results provide preliminary evidence that decomposed ILP is a feasible approach to task-level LfD.

</details>


### [200] [A Bioinspired Underwater Robot with a Latch-Mediated Soft Bistable Mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26936)
*Chongze Bi,Wenjie Wu,Zonghao Zuo,Li Wen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究受自然界中闩锁介导的弹簧驱动系统启发，开发了一种集成锁存机制的仿生软双稳态执行器，实现了单电机下的非对称能量输入与释放，显著提升了微型水下机器人的推进效率与机动性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统能源能量密度低限制了微型水下机器人的发展，而自然界的生物如螳螂虾通过闩锁介导的弹簧驱动（LaMSA）实现快速运动，但现有技术难以在紧凑结构中复现此类快速、非对称驱动。因此，亟需一种新型高效驱动机制以突破当前瓶颈。

Method: 设计一种具有集成锁存机制的仿生软双稳态执行器，利用单个电机实现能量的非对称存储与释放，并结合鳍状结构实现水下推进与操控；通过调节鳍的角度实现多种运动模式。

Result: 实验验证了该系统可实现稳定的周期性扑动、精确转向，最大推力达0.528 N，冲量为0.147 Ns，垂直位移达30 mm，并能实现垂直上升、斜向前进和侧向移动等多种运动模式。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一种新颖且节能的微型水下机器人运动控制方法，为未来仿生水下设备在探索、环境监测和检测等领域的应用提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Underwater robotics has advanced significantly over recent decades. however, the development of miniaturized underwater robots remains limited by low energy densities of traditional power sources. Nature offers compelling solutions-organisms like mantis shrimps and fleas utilize latch-mediated spring actuation (LaMSA) systems that achieve rapid movements through a decoupled energy storage and release mechanism. Despite extensive studies of LaMSA, replicating such rapid, asymmetric actuation within simple, compact structures remains challenging. In this work, we introduce a bioinspired, soft bistable actuator with an integrated latch mechanism that enables asymmetric energy input and release using a single motor. Coupled with fin structures, this design facilitates efficient underwater propulsion and maneuverability. Experimental results demonstrate stable periodic flapping, precise steering, and a maximum thrust of 0.528 N, impulse of 0.147 Ns, and vertical displacement of 30 mm. By modulating fin angles, the robot achieves versatile motions, including vertical ascent, diagonal forward movement, and lateral translation. This study presents a novel, energy-efficient approach for controlling motion in compact underwater robots, paving the way for advanced biomimetic designs with potential applications in exploration, environmental monitoring, and inspection.

</details>


### [201] [Trust, Geometry, and Rules: A Credibility-Aware Reinforcement Learning Framework for Safe USV Navigation under Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26974)
*Yuhang Zhang,Shuqi Chai,Yukang Zhang,Liusha Yang,Mingchuan Zhang,Wei Wang,Qingjiang Shi,Quanbo Ge*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合可信度感知学习、几何安全屏蔽和连续规则感知嵌入的框架，以提升无人水面航行器在动态海洋环境中遵守避碰规则的安全自主导航能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于强化学习的方法因状态估计误差导致的不可靠信念状态和离散交通规则带来的学习目标不连续性，在感知系统不确定性校准不良时表现不佳。

Method: 引入可信度加权值学习（CW-VL）调节异方差损失；使用协方差膨胀速度障碍（CI-VO）构建保守几何屏蔽；将二元会遇责任松弛为连续的风险感知COLREGs职责嵌入。

Result: 模拟会遇实验表明，该方法在感知不一致情况下具有更强的训练鲁棒性，并在防撞和遵守COLREGs方面优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 所提框架有效提升了USV在不确定感知条件下的安全性和规则合规性，解决了强化学习中由噪声数据和离散规则引起的训练不稳定问题。

Abstract: Autonomous navigation of Unmanned Surface Vehicles (USVs) that is safe and compliant with the International Regulations for Preventing Collisions at Sea (COLREGs) remains a formidable challenge in dynamic maritime environments, particularly when perception systems exhibit miscalibrated uncertainty. Existing Reinforcement Learning (RL)-based methods often falter because state-estimation errors induce unreliable belief states that mislead the value function, while discrete traffic rules introduce discontinuity in the learning objective. To address these challenges, we propose a framework integrating credibility-aware learning, geometric safety shielding, and continuous rule-aware embedding. First, Credibility-Weighted Value Learning (CW-VL) introduces a dynamic trust factor derived from the discrepancy between filter-estimated covariance and empirical error statistics to modulate the critic's heteroscedastic loss, preventing policy overfitting to noisy samples. Second, the Covariance-Inflated Velocity Obstacle (CI-VO) maps position-estimation uncertainty into set-wise angular margins, forming a conservative geometric shield that overrides hazardous exploratory actions. Third, Risk-Aware COLREGs Duty Embedding relaxes binary encounter duties into continuous rule-aware signals, providing smooth sector-transition information and suppressing oscillation from sparse rule rewards. Simulated encounter studies demonstrate improved training robustness against perceptual inconsistency and superior collision avoidance and COLREGs compliance over baselines.

</details>


### [202] [Towards Shared Embodied Intelligence in Humanoid Robots through Optimization Development and Testing of the Human Aware ergoCub Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26991)
*Carlotta Sartore,Mohamed Elobaid,Lorenzo Rapetti,Giulio Romualdi,Stefano Dafarra,Nicola A. Piga,Ines Sorrentino,Paolo Maria Vicecone,Silvio Traversaro,Ugo Pattacini,Luca Fiorio,Francesco Draicchio,Giovanna Tranfo,Lorenzo Natale,Marco Maggiali,Daniele Pucci*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种融合共享智能与具身认知的架构，用于设计能与人类安全高效协作的人形机器人，通过优化机器人硬件和物理智能参数以适应人体工学指标，并以ergoCub机器人为实例验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了实现人机间的安全有效协作，需要将人类行为中的共享智能与具身认知原理整合到人形机器人设计中。

Method: 提出一种集成共享智能和具身认知的架构，建模人机交互并嵌入人体模型，优化机器人形态及控制参数以符合人类人体工学。

Result: 成功开发出ergoCub人形机器人，其形态和控制经过优化，适用于与人类的协作任务。

Conclusion: 该方法为在硬件和物理智能层面优先考虑人体工学的人形机器人设计提供了可行框架，适用于工业和服务机器人领域。

Abstract: Collaboration is central to human behavior, enabling tasks beyond individual capability. This ability arises from coordinating actions through internal representations of others, a concept known as shared intelligence. Additionally, humans are characterized by physical bodies and cognitive abilities that are optimized in response to their environment, a phenomenon referred to as embodied cognition. Designing humanoid robots that collaborate safely and effectively with people requires unifying these principles. Here we propose an architecture that integrates shared intelligence and embodied cognition to enable robots to physically collaborate with humans, where robot hardware and control are optimized for human metrics, using representations of the human body and motion intelligence. The ultimate goal is to achieve a form of shared embodied intelligence. Specifically, our architecture optimizes robot hardware and physical intelligence parameters with respect to human ergonomic metrics. This is accomplished by modeling human-robot interaction as a function of hardware configurations and embedding human models into the robot's physical intelligence. As a concrete implementation, we present the humanoid robot ergoCub, whose morphology and control have been optimized for collaborative tasks with humans. Our approach provides a framework for designing humanoid robots that prioritize human ergonomics at both the hardware and physical intelligence levels, with applications in industrial and assistive robotics.

</details>


### [203] [TPS-Drive: Task-Guided Representation Purification for VLM-based Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27038)
*Jiaxiang Li,Yumao Liu,Ke Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TPS-Drive是一种面向自动驾驶规划的新型视觉语言模型框架，通过任务引导的表示净化机制，在保持语义推理能力的同时实现精确的3D空间预测，有效缓解了现有方法中的空间幻觉和表示干扰问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型在自动驾驶规划中难以兼顾语义推理与精确3D空间预测：文本对齐方法破坏几何结构导致空间幻觉，密集视觉方法因背景纹理冗余引发表示干扰。

Method: 提出TPS-Drive框架，核心是任务引导的表示净化。采用以智能体为中心的分词器，通过任务引导的向量量化机制（由冻结的3D检测头监督），将码本资源从静态背景重新分配给关键动态智能体，剥离空间冗余；并采用解耦的推理流程，依次进行场景理解、未来预测和动作生成；通过三阶段渐进式训练（含奖励驱动微调）进行优化。

Result: 实验表明，TPS-Drive在开放环路的nuScenes评估中实现了准确的智能体状态预测并降低了碰撞率；在严格的闭环NAVSIMv1和NAVSIMv2基准上均创下新的安全性能记录。

Conclusion: TPS-Drive通过任务引导的表示净化，成功弥合了语义推理与精确空间预测之间的鸿沟，为基于VLM的自动驾驶规划提供了更高效、更安全的解决方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) provide a promising foundation for autonomous driving planning, yet bridging semantic reasoning and precise 3D spatial forecasting remains a critical challenge. Existing representation strategies generally follow two paths: text-aligned methods flatten continuous spatial states into symbols, which compromises geometric structure and induces "spatial hallucinations"; dense visual methods preserve spatial topology but overwhelm standard tokenizers with redundant background textures, leading to "representation interference". To address these limitations, we introduce TPS-Drive, a novel framework centered on Task-Guided Representation Purification that empowers VLMs to Think in Purified Space. At its core, an Agent-Centric Tokenizer utilizes a task-guided vector quantization mechanism supervised by a frozen 3D detection head, which explicitly reallocates limited codebook capacity from pervasive static backgrounds to critical dynamic agents and effectively isolates spatial redundancy. Leveraging this purified spatial vocabulary, TPS-Drive employs a decoupled reasoning pipeline that sequentially performs scene understanding, future forecasting, and action generation. The framework is optimized via a progressive three-stage training paradigm, culminating in reward-driven refinement that surpasses pure imitation learning. Extensive experiments validate our approach: TPS-Drive achieves accurate agent spatial state forecasting and reduces collision rates in open-loop nuScenes evaluations, while establishing new safety records on the rigorous closed-loop NAVSIMv1 and NAVSIMv2 benchmarks.

</details>


### [204] [Learning to Balance Motor Thermal Safety and Quadrupedal Locomotion Performance with Residual Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27046)
*Yuhang Wan,Weixian Lin,Letian Qian,Yiqi Zou,Weiwei Wu,Shengwei Wu,Chuanlin Zhao,Xin Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合全身热模型与强化学习的两阶段训练框架，用于四足机器人电机热管理，有效平衡了热安全与运动性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电机过热限制了带负载条件下长时间运动，而现有研究常忽视电机热管理问题。

Method: 将四足机器人的全身热模型集成到强化学习框架中，采用两阶段训练：先预训练一个基础运动策略，再在其基础上训练一个根据热状态调整动作的残差策略。

Result: 仿真和真实实验表明，该方法在多种地形下实现了稳定运动；在3公斤负载下，机器人运行超过13分钟未过热，而仅用基础策略约5分钟即过热。

Conclusion: 所提出的两阶段强化学习框架能有效提升四足机器人在负载下的热管理能力与运动持续性。

Abstract: Motor thermal management is often overlooked in the context of electrically-actuated robots, particularly legged robots, but motor overheating is a key factor that limits long-duration locomotion especially under payload conditions. This paper integrates a whole-body thermal model of a quadruped robot into the reinforcement learning pipeline to update motor temperatures, and proposes a two-stage training framework for motor thermal management. In this framework, a nominal policy is first pre-trained as a locomotion baseline capable of traversing diverse terrains. A residual policy is then trained on top of the nominal policy to provide corrective actions based on the robot's thermal state, ensuring high performance under low-temperature conditions and preventing motor overheating under high-temperature conditions. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed policy achieves an effective balance between motor thermal safety and locomotion performance. Real-world experiments on a Unitree A1 quadruped robot further validate the approach: under a 3 kg payload, the robot achieves stable locomotion across multiple terrains for over 13 minutes, while the nominal policy alone leads to motor overheating in about 5 minutes.

</details>


### [205] [VR-DAgger: Immersive VR for Dexterous Data Collection and Uncertainty-Guided On-Policy Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27114)
*René Zurbrügg,Tifanny Portela,Arjun Bhardwaj,Aravind Elanjimattathil Vijayan,Maximum Wilder-Smith,Marco Hutter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在机器人操作中通过演示学习的有效性，但指出收集足够的任务特定数据仍然是一个主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决在分布偏移下性能下降以及专家时间被低价值修正占用的问题。

Method: 未明确提及具体方法，但暗示需要更高效的数据利用和关键失败案例的针对性改进。

Result: 未提供具体结果，但指出了当前方法的局限性。

Conclusion: 当前的演示学习面临数据效率和专家时间利用的挑战，需聚焦于关键失败情况以提升整体性能。

Abstract: Learning from demonstrations is effective for robotic manipulation, but collecting sufficient task-specific data remains a major bottleneck. Under distribution shift, small errors compound, performance degrades, and expert time is often spent on redundant, low-value corrections instead of the few critical failure cases.

</details>


### [206] [Towards Drone-based Mapping of Volcanic Gases using Gas Tomography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27180)
*Marius Schaab,Niklas Karbach,Antonia Rabe,Thomas Wiedemann,Patrick Hinsen,Dmitriy Shutin,Thorsten Hoffmann,Achim J. Lilienthal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种基于模型的气体层析成像方法，结合拉格朗日模型补偿风力影响，成功克服了无人机旋翼下洗气流对火山气体（CO2）检测的干扰，实现了对泥火山气体排放的精确遥感测绘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 火山释放大量CO2，影响气候与人类生活，准确测绘气体排放对预测喷发和环境评估至关重要。传统无人机搭载的原位传感器受旋翼下洗气流影响，难以准确检测气体浓度分布。

Method: 采用远程开放式路径气体传感结合气体层析成像技术，并引入拉格朗日模型来补偿风致平流效应，实现对气体分布的重建。实验在Salinelle dei Cappuccini泥火山进行，对比无人机原位传感器与远程传感结果。

Result: 无人机原位传感器因空气动力扰动未能检测到CO2排放，而开放式路径遥感结合模型重建成功生成了与人工实地测量一致的气体分布图。

Conclusion: 基于模型的气体层析成像能有效克服无人机下洗气流带来的测量局限，为火山气体排放的精准测绘提供了可靠技术手段。

Abstract: Volcanoes emit large amounts of CO2, directly influencing human lives. Mapping volcanic gas emissions helps to forecast eruptions and understand the impact of volcanoes on climate and the environment. Drone-based gas sensing significantly reduces risks in volcanic monitoring but faces technical limitations when measuring gas, as rotor downwash disperses the gas plume before detection. Gas Tomography using remote gas sensing addresses this challenge. At the Salinelle dei Cappuccini mud volcanoes, we demonstrate that while drone-mounted in-situ sensors failed to detect CO2 emissions due to aerodynamic disturbance, open-path sensing successfully enabled remote gas distribution mapping. We present a novel model-based gas tomographic reconstruction approach that incorporates a Lagrangian model to compensate for wind-induced advection. The resulting gas distribution maps align with manually collected in-situ measurements, confirming that model-based gas tomography effectively overcomes downwash limitations and enables accurate mapping of volcanic emissions.

</details>


### [207] [FineVLA: Fine-Grained Instruction Alignment for Steerable Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27284)
*Xintong Hu,Xuhong Huang,Jinyu Zhang,Yutong Yao,Yuchong Sun,Qiuyue Wang,Mingsheng Li,Sicheng Xie,Yitao Liu,Junhao Chen,Yixuan Chen,Yingming Zheng,Shuai Bai,Tao Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FineVLA是一个开源框架，旨在提供细粒度的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）监督，通过整合多个机器人数据集并引入人类验证的精细轨迹数据，提升机器人对执行方式的控制能力。实验表明，细粒度监督不仅不牺牲目标完成率，还能显著增强指令的可操控性，特别是在姿态、颜色和接近方向等维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人数据集通常仅提供粗略的目标级语言描述，缺乏执行过程中的关键细节（如操作臂、接近方向、接触区域），限制了可引导策略学习和机器人视频理解。因此需要一种能够提供细粒度动作对齐监督的框架。

Method: 提出FineVLA框架，包括：(1) 数据构建工具，整合10个开源数据集的近百万条轨迹，构建包含47,159条精细轨迹的人工验证数据集FineVLA-Data；(2) 专用基准测试集，含500个视频、10,816个原子事实和1,030个VQA问题；(3) 面向机器人的VLM标注器，支持大规模细粒度标注；(4) 可引导的VLA策略模型，使用细粒度与原始目标级指令的混合训练。

Result: 实验发现：(1) 仅使用细粒度监督（FG-only）在不同设置下比仅使用原始指令（Raw-only）成功率提高+1.4至+8.1个百分点；(2) 细粒度与原始指令互补，最佳混合比例为1:2到1:1，在RoboTwin仿真中达到86.8%/82.5%，在真实双臂操作中得分为62.7/100（Raw-only为49.9）；(3) 细粒度监督显著提升对执行方式的控制，尤其在姿态（+23）、颜色（+18）和接近方向（+18）上表现突出。

Conclusion: 细粒度语言应作为目标级指令的补充，明确‘如何执行’而不仅是‘做什么’，从而提升机器人任务执行的可控性和准确性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are increasingly expected to not only complete robot tasks, but also follow human instructions about how those tasks should be executed. However, existing robot datasets usually pair trajectories with coarse goal-level language, leaving execution-critical details such as active arm, approach direction, and contact region unspecified. This limits steerable policy learning and robotic video understanding. We introduce FineVLA, an open framework for action-aligned fine-grained VLA supervision. The framework includes: (1) a data construction tool that unifies 972,247 trajectories across 85K tasks from 10 open-source robot datasets and builds FineVLA-Data, a human-verified dataset of 47,159 fine-grained trajectories; (2) a held-out benchmark with 500 videos, 10,816 atomic facts, and 1,030 VQA questions; (3) a robotics-specialized VLM annotator for scalable fine-grained annotation; and (4) a steerable VLA policy trained with controlled mixtures of fine-grained and raw goal-level instructions. Our experiments yield three findings. First, fine-grained supervision does not sacrifice goal-level success: FG-only improves over Raw-only by +1.4 to +8.1 success-rate points across settings. Second, fine-grained and raw instructions are complementary, following a consistent inverted-U trend peaking at FG:Raw = 1:2 to 1:1. The best mixed setting reaches 86.8%/82.5% in RoboTwin simulation and 62.7/100 in real-world dual-arm manipulation (vs. 49.9 Raw-only). Third, fine-grained supervision improves steerable control: the largest real-world gains appear on pose (+23), color (+18), and approach direction (+18)--factors where goal-level instructions provide no guidance. Overall, fine-grained language should augment goal-level instructions: specifying how to execute alongside what to achieve. Project page: https://finevla.xlang.ai/

</details>


### [208] [Riding the Shifting Potential: When Reactive Control Suffices for Multi-Goal Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27314)
*Vito Mengers,Oliver Brock*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于图模型和零空间投影的反应式控制方法，通过动态调整目标优先级解决多目标冲突问题，在导航与平面推动物体任务中表现出色，无需演示或重新训练，并可直接迁移到真实机器人系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统反应式控制在处理多目标任务时因静态编码导致局部极小值问题，难以应对目标间的冲突，本文旨在通过动态反映目标交互结构来克服这一局限。

Method: 利用基于图的世界模型中的交互结构，引入零空间投影：将低优先级梯度投影到高优先级梯度的零空间中，优先级根据当前状态连续确定，从而在产生冲突的位置解决冲突。

Result: 在非凸障碍物导航和非凸物体平面推动任务中，该方法分别实现了100%的成功率（对比最速下降法0%，扩散策略约55%），且无需示例或再训练；并成功迁移至具身机器人，适应感知与运动学约束。

Conclusion: 反应式控制的局限性并非固有，而是源于静态编码；通过结合图模型与动态优先级零空间投影，可有效解决多目标冲突，实现鲁棒且可迁移的控制。

Abstract: Reactive control is often considered insufficient for multi-objective tasks because conflicting objectives give rise to local minima. We argue this limitation is not inherent but arises from static encodings that fail to reflect how objectives currently interact. We exploit the interaction structure encoded in a graph-based world model by extending it with nullspace projections: conflicts are resolved where they arise by projecting lower-priority gradients into the nullspace of higher-priority ones, with priorities determined continuously from the current state. We demonstrate this in two domains where conflicts between objectives are central: navigation around non-convex obstacles, where static potential fields fundamentally fail, and planar pushing of non-convex objects, where our method achieves $100\%$ success across one-hundred configurations versus $0\%$ for the steepest-descent baseline and ${\sim}55\%$ for diffusion policy, without demonstrations or retraining. The same formulation transfers directly to a real robot with additional perceptual and kinematic constraints, accommodating them through the same mechanism.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [209] [BrickAnything: Geometry-Conditioned Buildable Brick Generation with Structure-Aware Tokenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26182)
*Zhengyang Ni,Feng Yan,Yu Guo,Fei Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为BrickAnything的几何条件自回归框架，用于从多种3D表示中生成可构建的砖块结构。该方法利用点云作为统一的几何接口，并通过结构感知的树状分词策略建模砖块间的局部连接关系，提升了生成过程的一致性和物理可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有砖块生成方法在处理不符合预定义约束的3D形状时容易失效，或未显式建模几何结构与装配关系，导致生成结果不可建造。因此需要一种能同时满足几何保真度和结构稳定性的新方法。

Method: 提出BrickAnything框架，使用点云作为输入，采用结构感知的树状分词表示砖块结构，结合偏好对齐后训练、有效性约束解码和自适应回滚策略进行优化。

Result: 实验表明，BrickAnything能生成几何保真且物理可实现的砖块结构，相比传统排序策略显著减少了回滚和再生次数。

Conclusion: BrickAnything通过统一的几何接口和结构感知的序列建模，有效解决了复杂3D形状到可建造砖结构的转换问题，提升了生成效率与建造可行性。

Abstract: Generating physically buildable brick structures from 3D shapes requires more than geometric reconstruction: the output must also satisfy discrete part constraints and structural stability. Existing brick generation methods either rely on heuristic optimization, which can break down when the target 3D shape does not admit a feasible structure under predefined constraints, or generate brick sequences without explicitly modeling the underlying 3D geometry and assembly relations. In this work, we present BrickAnything, a geometry-conditioned autoregressive framework for generating buildable brick structures from diverse 3D representations. BrickAnything uses point clouds as a unified geometric interface and predicts brick sequences that reconstruct the target shape under assembly constraints. To model structural dependencies among bricks, we introduce a structure-aware tree tokenization, which represents brick structures through local attachment relations. This formulation makes sequence generation more consistent with the physical construction process, and reduces invalid intermediate states. We further introduce preference-based alignment post-training, validity-constrained decoding and adaptive rollback to improve buildability objectives such as stability and geometric fidelity. Extensive experiments demonstrate that BrickAnything produces geometrically faithful and physically realizable brick structures, and that the proposed tokenization effectively reduces rollback and regeneration compared with conventional ordering strategies.

</details>


### [210] [Can LLMs Introspect? A Reality Check](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26242)
*Shashwat Singh,Tal Linzen,Shauli Ravfogel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了大语言模型是否能真正内省其内部状态，指出当前的行为证据不足以支持强内省主张，并通过重新审视两种评估范式发现模型的表现更可能源于表面模式匹配而非真正的自我监控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受到人类元认知研究的启发，作者质疑现有研究对大语言模型具备内省能力的结论是否过早，强调需区分真正的内省与基于表层线索的模式匹配。

Method: 重新评估了两种近期提出的测试范式：一是检测模型能否识别自身内部状态被篡改；二是预测基于其隐藏状态生成的标签，并引入仅访问输入的分类器作为对照，同时设计语义无关的重标记控制任务。

Result: 发现模型无法可靠区分内部状态干预与输入 manipulation；外部分类器在预测隐藏状态标签上表现与模型相当；在控制任务中模型表现接近随机水平。

Conclusion: 当前证据不足以证明大语言模型具备元认知监控能力，许多看似内省的行为可由对输入的敏感性解释。

Abstract: Can large language models detect and report their own internal states? A number of studies have argued that the answer to this question is yes. We argue, based on lessons from human metacognition research, that this conclusion may be premature: to be convinced of this conclusion we need to distinguish genuine introspection from pattern matching based on surface-level cues. Furthermore, we argue that behavioral evidence alone is inherently insufficient to establish strong introspective claims.
  We re-examine two recently introduced evaluation paradigms in light of this consideration. In the first paradigm, models are expected to detect whether their internal states have been tampered with. We find that models cannot reliably distinguish such interventions on their internal states from manipulations of the input, suggesting that their success in the original studies reflects their ability to detect anomalies more generally, as opposed to interventions on their internal states in particular. In the second paradigm we examine, models are tasked with predicting labels derived from their own hidden states. Here, we find that classifiers that only have access to the input achieve equivalent performance to the model's own in-context predictions, indicating that the original results do not conclusively demonstrate that the model has privileged access to its internal representations. We further introduce a relabeled control setting, where models cannot rely on the semantics of the task to solve it, and instead must rely on the internal representation; models perform closer to chance on this better-controlled version of the task. Taken together, these results indicate that current evidence is insufficient to establish that LLMs display metacognitive monitoring.

</details>


### [211] [OmniToM: Benchmarking Theory of Mind in LLMs via Explicit Belief Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26322)
*Adam Bawatneh,Sagar Sapkota,Amrit Singh Bedi,Santu Karmaker,Mubarak Shah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OmniToM是一个新的基准，用于评估大语言模型在社会推理中对心理状态表征的构建能力，通过两阶段任务揭示当前模型在信念追踪上的瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法仅通过最终答案评估心智理论能力，无法判断模型是否真正构建了必要的心理状态表征，尤其是在涉及错误或动态信念的情境中。

Method: 提出OmniToM基准，包含信念提取和信念标注两个阶段，使用22,343个标注的信念命题对模型进行评估。

Result: 在零样本设置下，发现当前大语言模型在知识获取和表征转换方面存在困难，难以将叙事事实转化为角色的信念和共享心理状态。

Conclusion: 当前的大语言模型在显式建模他人信念方面存在局限，OmniToM为改进心智理论评估提供了更细粒度的工具。

Abstract: Theory of Mind (ToM), the ability to infer others' knowledge, intentions, and emotions, is commonly evaluated in large language models (LLMs) using end-point question answering, where performance is judged solely by the final answer to a social reasoning query. This paradigm obscures whether the model actually constructs the underlying mental-state representations required for robust reasoning, particularly in scenarios involving divergent, evolving, or mistaken beliefs. In order to address this research gap, we introduce OmniToM, a benchmark that directly evaluates these representations by requiring explicit modeling of belief structures for all relevant actors within a narrative. These structures are composed of belief propositions: minimal statements of what an actor takes to be true about the world or another actor's mental state, allowing knowledge, intentions, emotions, and false beliefs to be analyzed in a common format. Models are evaluated in two stages: Stage 1: Belief Extraction, which extracts from the story the beliefs relevant to its social dynamics, and Stage 2: Belief Labeling, which assigns each belief a seven-dimensional schema label covering recursive order, truth status, knowledge access, explicitness, content type, mental source, and context. Built from 895 stories from the existing ToMBench story corpus and augmented with 22,343 labeled belief propositions, OmniToM uses a human-calibrated LLM-assisted annotation pipeline. Across diverse models in zero-shot evaluation, OmniToM reveals an actor-specific belief-tracking bottleneck: current LLMs struggle with the knowledge-access and representational decisions required to transform narrative facts into actors' beliefs and shared mental states.

</details>


### [212] [Managing Uncertainty in LLM-Generated Procedural Knowledge for Virtual Laboratory Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26333)
*Polychronis Karpodinis,Dimitris Kalles*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于管理大型语言模型（LLM）生成的虚拟实验 procedural knowledge 中不确定性的原型框架，通过结构化领域表示和不确定性状态转移样本来提取规则并修复步骤，以支持可执行的虚拟实验流程规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于创建新的虚拟实验程序成本高昂，且LLM生成的实验步骤常存在遗漏、顺序错误或逻辑不一致问题，因此需要一种方法来管理和修正这些不确定性。

Method: 利用结构化领域知识和LLM生成的不确定状态转移样本，提取候选过程规则，将其转化为显式可检查的约束，并用于修复不确定的过程步骤。

Result: 在涉及仪器、容器、工具和物料转移操作的虚拟实验室环境中验证了该方法的有效性，能够识别并修正LLM生成的不可靠步骤。

Conclusion: 该框架有助于降低LLM生成内容的不确定性，提升虚拟实验程序的可靠性与可执行性，具有推广到其他结构化交互环境中的潜力。

Abstract: Educational virtual laboratories can make experimental training more scala-ble, adaptive, and accessible, especially when students have limited access to physical laboratory facilities. However, authoring new simulated laboratory procedures remains costly: educators must describe new equipment, define how instruments and materials interact, and specify valid procedural flows that can be executed or assessed inside the virtual environment. Large lan-guage models can assist in this authoring process by generating detailed ex-perimental procedures, but their output should not be treated as directly exe-cutable plans. They may omit necessary actions, arrange steps in the wrong order, or produce instructions that are logically incorrect or incompatible with the laboratory equipment. This paper presents a prototype framework for managing uncertainty in LLM-generated procedural knowledge for virtu-al laboratory planning. The framework aims to reduce procedural uncertainty by using structured domain representations and uncertain LLM-generated state-transition samples to extract candidate procedural rules, transform them into explicit and inspectable constraints, and use them to repair uncertain procedural steps. Although the motivating domain refers to educational vir-tual laboratories, the underlying problem is more general: managing uncer-tain procedural knowledge for action planning in structured interactive envi-ronments. We illustrate the approach in a virtual laboratory domain involving laboratory instruments, containers, tools, and material-transfer actions.

</details>


### [213] [Automatic Layer Selection for Hallucination Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26366)
*Xinpeng Wang,William Cao,Andrew Gordon Wilson,Zhe Zeng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需训练、计算开销极低的自动层选择方法FEPoID，用于增强大语言模型中的幻觉检测，通过挖掘中间层的本征维度峰值并结合截断策略，显著提升了检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏自动化选择适合幻觉检测的中间层的系统性方法，且已有准则在不同模型和任务上表现不稳定，因此需要一种普适且高效的自动层选择机制。

Method: 提出了多个关于中间层为何蕴含更强幻觉信号的假设，并评估了多种层选择标准；最终提出基于本征维度首个有效峰值（FEPoID）的新准则，并引入一种生成阶段的截断策略以增强幻觉信号。

Result: FEPoID在多种大语言模型架构、规模和任务（问答与摘要）下均能稳定识别最优或近优层，性能优于现有基线与先前准则；截断策略进一步显著提升检测效果。

Conclusion: FEPoID是一种高效、通用且无需训练的层选择方法，结合生成截断策略可有效强化幻觉检测，为实际应用提供了低开销高精度的解决方案。

Abstract: Recent studies on hallucination detection have shown that hallucination-related signals are more strongly encoded in intermediate layers than in the final layer of large language models (LLMs). Although a growing body of work has sought to exploit this property for hallucination detection, how to automate the selection of high-performing layers remains underexplored, and principled methods for this purpose are still lacking. To address this gap, we first propose several hypotheses for why such signals emerge in intermediate layers and evaluate corresponding criteria for automatic layer selection across diverse LLM architectures, scales, and tasks, covering both question answering and summarization hallucination detection benchmarks. However, we find that none of these criteria consistently delivers satisfactory performance. We therefore propose a new selection criterion, First Effective Peak of Intrinsic Dimension (FEPoID), which consistently identify optimal or near-optimal layers and outperforms both the aforementioned criteria and existing hallucination detection baselines. FEPoID is training-free and incurs negligible computational overhead. In addition, we study the generation behaviors of LLMs and introduce a simple yet effective truncation strategy, which further amplifies hallucination-related signals and substantially improves overall detection performance. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/DesoloYw/Automatic-Layer-Selection-for-Hallucination-Detection.git

</details>


### [214] [Exploiting Local Dynamics Regularity for Reusable Skills in Offline Hierarchical RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26371)
*Sarthak Dayal,Abhinav Peri,Carl Qi,Claas Voelcker,Alexander Levine,Caleb Chuck,Amy Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于对比动作表示的可重用局部控制算法CARL，通过利用局部动态的直觉来学习可迁移技能，并在复杂环境中实现了更好的分层强化学习性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的分层强化学习方法难以获得真正可重用的技能，本文旨在通过利用局部动态在不同全局上下文中的相似性来解决这一问题。

Method: 提出CARL算法，通过将不同全局上下文中所需的局部转移与对应的动作序列对齐，利用对比学习方法构建动作表示，从而实现技能的发现与重用。

Result: 在复杂的人形环境和OGBench基准测试中，CARL展现出有意义的技能聚类效果，并在与HIQL结合时提升了下游任务性能。

Conclusion: CARL通过建模局部动态与动作序列的关系，有效支持了分层强化学习中高阶策略对低阶技能的选择与复用，为提升HRL的效率和泛化能力提供了新思路。

Abstract: Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning (HRL) promises to solve long-horizon Reinforcement Learning (RL) tasks more efficiently than non-hierarchical counterparts by discovering and reusing temporally-extended skills. However, obtaining skills that are actually reusable remains an open challenge. Towards this end, we focus on abstractions that exploit the intuition of local dynamics: local transitions in different global contexts require similar kinds of action sequences. By aligning these contexts with the action sequences they require, we are able to learn which skills to reuse and where to reuse them. In principle, this information should benefit many HRL algorithms, where high-level policies have to reason about the low-level skills they use. The resulting algorithm CARL (Contrastive Action-based Representations for Reusable Local Control) shows both qualitative clustering of meaningful skills in complex humanoid environments and improved downstream performance on the OGBench benchmark when integrated with HIQL.

</details>


### [215] [Advancing Creative Physical Intelligence in Large Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26396)
*Cheng Qian,Hyeonjeong Ha,Jiayu Liu,Jeonghwan Kim,Emre Can Acikgoz,Bingxuan Li,Kunlun Zhu,Jiateng Liu,Aditi Tiwari,Zhenhailong Wang,Xiusi Chen,Mahdi Namazifar,Heng Ji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了MM-CreativityBench，一个用于评估大型多模态模型在视觉丰富、物理约束环境中基于视觉支持的创造性工具使用能力的新基准，并提出通过基于支持的对齐方法提升模型在此类任务中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的大型多模态模型在开放环境中发现视觉支持的创造性解决方案的能力尚未得到充分测试，尤其是在需要重新利用场景元素进行非显而易见但物理上可行的操作时。这种创造性的解决问题能力是人类智能的核心，但在现有基准中缺乏评估。

Method: 引入MM-CreativityBench基准，包含场景图像和候选实体及其部件的结构化视图，以细粒度地评估模型如何逐步检查场景、识别相关支持并构建解决方案；提出支持性对齐方法，采用直接偏好优化（DPO）鼓励模型基于视觉证据进行属性-支持推理，并结合支持知识库指导更广泛的实体探索与多轮规划。

Result: 实验表明当前LMMs在持续的视觉支持探索方面表现不足，常忽略相关实体或产生未在图像中体现的幻觉属性；所提方法显著提高了正确选择实体和部件的能力，并大幅减少了幻觉和与视觉支持相关的错误。

Conclusion: 支持性对齐能有效提升大型多模态模型在创造性工具使用任务中的性能，强调了将视觉证据驱动的推理和系统性探索机制融入模型训练的重要性。

Abstract: Large multimodal models (LMMs) have rapidly advanced in perception and reasoning; however, it remains unclear whether these capabilities generalize to discovering visually grounded solutions in open-ended environments, beyond pattern recognition. In such settings, intelligence requires more than answering well-posed questions: it involves identifying how elements in a scene can be repurposed in non-obvious yet physically feasible ways. This form of creative problem-solving is central to human intelligence, but remains largely untested in current benchmarks. To evaluate this ability, we introduce MM-CreativityBench, a benchmark for affordance-grounded creative tool use in visually rich, physically constrained environments. Each instance presents a scenario image with structured views of candidate entities and their parts, enabling fine-grained, interactive evaluation of how models iteratively inspect the scene, identify relevant affordances, and compose visually and physically grounded solutions. Our experiments show that current LMMs often fall short, not due to lack of generative capability, but because they do not sustain grounded exploration. Models often overlook relevant entities, under-examine critical parts, or hallucinate attributes not grounded in the image. Motivated by this failure mode, we propose affordance-grounded alignment, which casts creative tool use as a preference learning problem. Using Direct Preference Optimization, we encourage models to prefer attribute-affordance reasoning grounded in visual evidence over hallucinated alternatives. In addition, we incorporate supervision derived from an affordance knowledge base to guide broader entity exploration and multi-turn planning. Our results show consistent gains in selecting the correct entities and parts, while substantially reducing hallucination and grounding-related errors.

</details>


### [216] [MedGuideX: Internalizing Decision Logic from Executable Guidelines into Large Language Models for Clinical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26567)
*Yuhao Shen,Lang Cao,Simo Du,Yuqing Wang,Juexiao Zhou,Hao Peng,Yue Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种从临床实践指南（CPGs）中提取可执行决策逻辑的训练方法，用于生成事实与反事实的问答数据，从而提升医学大模型在临床推理任务中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常将临床指南作为自由文本使用，未能充分利用其内在的程序化决策结构。本文旨在通过挖掘CPG中的条件逻辑，提供更有效的监督信号来训练医学语言模型。

Method: 设计一个基于指南的训练流程，将CPG推荐转化为可执行的临床决策逻辑，并据此生成事实和反事实的问答对数据，用于微调医学大语言模型。

Result: 在四个临床推理基准测试中，所提出的模型MedGuideX平均准确率相对提升了10.28%；医生评估显示其推理步骤更忠实、有效、完整且清晰，且生成的理由更受偏好。

Conclusion: 从临床指南中提取可执行决策逻辑并转化为可扩展的监督信号，是构建可靠医学大模型的有效途径。

Abstract: Clinical practice guidelines (CPGs) encode evidence-based decision logic that clinicians apply by evaluating patient variables, conditional criteria, and recommendation rules. However, existing methods often use CPGs as free-text training data or retrieval sources, underutilizing their procedural decision structure. To better exploit this structure, we introduce a guideline-derived training pipeline that transforms CPG recommendations into executable clinical decision logic and uses it to generate factual and counterfactual question-answering data. Theses data teach models both guideline-supported decisions and how decisions change under different patient conditions. Post-training a medical LLM on the generated data yields MedGuideX. Across four clinical reasoning benchmarks, MedGuideX achieves a 10.28% relative improvement in average accuracy. Physician evaluation further shows that MedGuideX better recovers clinician authored reasoning steps and produces physician-preferred rationales in faithfulness, validity, completeness, and clarity. Overall, our results show that executable decision logic from CPGs can be transformed into scalable supervision for building reliable medical LLMs.

</details>


### [217] [Tail-Aware HiFloat4: W4A4 Post-Training Quantization for Wan2.2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26628)
*Zhanfeng Feng,Shuai Guo,Xin Di,Long Peng,Yang Cao,Zhengjun Zha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Tail-Aware HiFloat4，用于低比特文本到视频生成的量化挑战，基于HiFloat4格式改进ViDiT-Q流程以适配Wan2.2模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在保持生成质量的同时实现高效的低比特量化，解决罕见激活值对量化精度的影响。

Method: 采用W4A4 HiFloat4伪量化处理主要线性层，保留敏感边界模块为高精度，并引入激活尾部感知的百分位校准模块进行通道掩码构建，结合紧凑的PTQ状态恢复机制。

Result: 有效降低了校准阶段异常值的影响，同时保持运行时HiFloat4计算和采样流程不变。

Conclusion: 该方法在不改变原有推理流程的前提下提升了低比特量化的稳定性与性能表现。

Abstract: This report describes Tail-Aware HiFloat4, our submission to the low-bit text-to-video generation quantization challenge. Our method adapts the public ViDiT-Q post-training quantization pipeline to Wan2.2 under the HiFloat4 numerical format. We quantize the main linear layers in both Wan2.2 transformer modules with W4A4 HiFloat4 fake quantization, keep numerically sensitive boundary modules in high precision, and introduce an activation-tail-aware percentile calibration module for channel-mask construction. Together with compact PTQ-state restoration, this design reduces the influence of rare calibration outliers while keeping the runtime HiFloat4 arithmetic and sampling pipeline unchanged.

</details>


### [218] [UnityMAS-O: A General RL Optimization Framework for LLM-Based Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26646)
*Yiqun Chen,Wei Yang,Erhan Zhang,Shijie Wang,Qi Liu,Zechun Niu,Bin Zhang,Haitao Li,Rui Li,Lingyong Yan,Jinyuan Feng,Biqing Qi,Xiaochi Wei,Yan Gao,Yi Wu,Yao Hu,Jiaxin Mao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: UnityMAS-O 是一个针对基于大语言模型的多智能体系统的通用强化学习优化框架，将整个工作流作为优化单元，支持角色定义、结构化交互和灵活的参数共享，可有效提升复杂任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习框架主要针对单策略优化，缺乏对用户自定义多智能体工作流、结构化交互、角色信用分配和可配置参数共享的支持，限制了多智能体系统的学习与优化能力。

Method: 提出 UnityMAS-O 框架，将完整工作流作为优化单位，引入四个一等对象：逻辑智能体角色、图轨迹、用户定义奖励和智能体-模型映射，并基于 verl 和 Ray 构建星型拓扑运行时，实现集中控制与分布式更新。

Result: 在检索增强问答、迭代智能体搜索和反思性代码生成任务上验证，UnityMAS-O 在 Natural Questions、HotpotQA 和私有代码任务上均提升了原有工作流的表现，尤其对小模型和严格代码通过率指标增益显著。

Conclusion: UnityMAS-O 能够作为可复用的基础架构，将多样化的基于大语言模型的多智能体工作流转化为可训练的多智能体强化学习系统。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems decompose complex tasks into interacting roles, but most remain manually orchestrated by prompts, tools, and control rules, while agents are rarely optimized through a unified reinforcement learning interface. Existing RL post-training frameworks mainly target single-policy optimization and lack abstractions for user-defined multi-agent workflows, structured interaction, role-specific credit assignment, and configurable parameter sharing.
  We present UnityMAS-O, a general RL optimization framework for LLM-based multi-agent systems. UnityMAS-O treats the complete workflow as the optimization unit, rather than a single response or policy trajectory. It represents workflows through four first-class objects: logical agent roles, graph trajectories, user-defined rewards, and agent--model mappings. This decouples logical agents from physical model parameters, supporting full sharing, full separation, and partial sharing, with rewards assigned at role, turn, and trajectory levels.
  UnityMAS-O extends verl with a Ray-based star-topology runtime. A central controller executes workflows, invokes tools, records structured trajectories, and assembles rewards; model-local worker groups handle rollout, buffering, advantage computation, and distributed PPO-style updates. Users can define agents, workflows, model mappings, and rewards without rewriting the optimization infrastructure.
  We instantiate UnityMAS-O on retrieval-augmented QA, iterative agentic search, and reflective code generation. Across Natural Questions, HotpotQA, and held-out code tasks, multi-agent RL improves manually specified workflows after optimization, with especially large gains for smaller models and strict code all-passed metrics. These results show that UnityMAS-O can serve as a reusable substrate for converting diverse LLM-based multi-agent workflows into trainable multi-agent RL systems.

</details>


### [219] [Completion vs Optimality: Policy Gradient in Long-Horizon Cumulative-Damage Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26657)
*Wolfgang Maass,Sabine Janzen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了长时程决策问题中累积损伤导致的策略梯度方法的两种失败模式，并提出分解方法，通过两个不同领域环境验证了四个可检验预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在具有累积损伤的长时程决策问题中，局部吸引的动作可能导致全局不利结果，需识别并解决策略梯度方法在此类问题中的失败机制。

Method: 提出“完成性”与“最优性”的分解框架，在PPO算法中引入线性软惩罚，并结合动作空间限制进行实验，使用砖匠和NBA球员两种独立校准的环境进行验证。

Result: 单独提供时域访问会降低完成率；动作空间限制结合时域访问可实现完成但存在最优性差距（ΔM_final = 0.271），该差距源于在损伤起点的初期贪婪承诺；四个预测在两种环境中均得到定性复现，其中三个时域满足时域不变性预测，H=15为例外，符合H*边界范围。

Conclusion: 策略梯度方法在处理长时程累积损伤问题时面临完成性和最优性双重挑战，需通过机制设计避免早期过度承诺，所提出的分解框架有助于理解并改进此类算法性能。

Abstract: Long-horizon decision problems with cumulative damage couple locally attractive actions to globally adverse outcomes. We identify two orthogonal failure modes for policy-gradient methods on this class and propose a decomposition that separates them: \emph{completion} (reaching the terminal horizon rather than exiting via an implicit terminal constraint) and \emph{optimality} (matching the dynamic-programming reference given completion). Under PPO with a linear soft penalty, granting horizon access alone reduces the completion rate: the penalty's equilibrium drives the dominant-activity share to zero, while action-space restriction combined with horizon access achieves completion but leaves an optimality gap ($ΔM_{\text{final}} = 0.271$) that we trace to first-phase greedy commitment at the damage origin. We derive four testable predictions and evaluate them in two separately calibrated environments that share the same abstract structure but differ in domain, horizon, activity set, and calibration data: a 49-step bricklayer career and a 20-season NBA power-forward career. All four predictions replicate qualitatively. The horizon-invariance prediction is met at three of four tested horizons, with the exception at $H = 15$ consistent with the $H^*$ boundary ($H^* \in [6, 14]$ under the NBA parameters).

</details>


### [220] [MemFail: Stress-Testing Failure Modes of LLM Memory Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26667)
*Ishir Garg,Neel Kolhe,Dawn Song,Xuandong Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了MemFail，一个用于诊断现代大语言模型记忆系统的基准测试工具，通过分解记忆系统为总结、存储和检索三个基本操作，并设计针对性的数据集来识别各环节的失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基准测试仅报告整体问答准确率，将记忆系统视为黑箱，无法定位错误来源，因此需要一种能够细粒度分析记忆系统失败模式的诊断工具。

Method: 将记忆系统形式化为三个核心操作：总结、存储和检索；针对每个操作识别潜在的失败模式，并构建五个跨任务的对抗性数据集以测试特定操作的表现。

Result: 在四个最先进的记忆系统上评估了MemFail，结果展示了不同架构在各项操作中的表现差异与权衡，验证了该基准对失败模式的可诊断性。

Conclusion: MemFail能够有效揭示现代LLM记忆系统的具体失败原因，帮助理解不同设计选择带来的影响，为改进记忆系统提供了实证依据。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly rely on external memory systems to remain consistent across long-horizon interactions, but little empirical work has been done to understand the specific failure modes and design choices that these systems present. Existing benchmarks report aggregate question-answering accuracy and treat memory systems as black boxes, making it impossible to attribute an incorrect answer to a particular failure mode of the system. We introduce MemFail, a diagnostic benchmark that isolates the failure modes of modern LLM memory systems. We begin by formalizing memory systems as the composition of three canonical operations -- summarization, storage, and retrieval -- and identify the potential failure modes induced by each. Based on these hypothesized failure modes, we construct five datasets spanning four tasks, each adversarially designed to test a specific operation of a memory system. Using these datasets, we evaluate four state-of-the-art memory systems on MemFail and demonstrate how MemFail can be used to empirically understand the tradeoffs induced by differences in memory system architectures.

</details>


### [221] [A Dataset of Robot-Patient and Doctor-Patient Medical Dialogues for Spoken Language Processing Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26747)
*Heriberto Cuayahuitl,Grace Jang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了MeDial-Speech，一个用于训练和评估医疗AI的新型语音数据集，包含111+小时的真实环境对话数据，涵盖四种健康状况，并通过句子选择任务评估了三种先进大语言模型的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型在通用任务中表现出色，但在医疗咨询中的应用仍面临挑战，因此需要专门的数据集和评估方法来推动医疗AI的发展。

Method: 收集了来自机器人-患者和医生-患者对话的真实语音数据，构建了MeDial-Speech数据集，并设计了一个包含20个选项的句子选择对话基准来评估GPT-5 mini、DeepSeek-V3和Claude Sonnet 4三种大语言模型。

Result: 实验结果表明，Claude Sonnet 4在手动转录和自动转录下的准确率分别为71.1%和74.7%，表现最佳，但所有模型在其概率预测中均表现出过度自信。

Conclusion: MeDial-Speech为医疗对话AI提供了有价值的数据资源和评估基准，揭示了当前大语言模型在医疗场景中的潜力与局限性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have brought huge improvements to Artificial Intelligence (AI), which can be applied to general-purpose tasks. However, their application to textual or spoken medical consultations is still an open research problem. This paper proposes MeDial-Speech, a novel speech dataset for training and evaluating Med-AIs that can carry out consultations with patients. It was collected in realistic environments from robot-patient and doctor-patient dialogues, contains 111+ hours of speech data (without data augmentation), and covers four health conditions: Lewy body dementia, heart failure, shoulder pain, and angina. In addition, we propose a dialogue benchmark via sentence selection (with 20 options) to evaluate three state-of-the-art LLMs: GPT-5 mini, DeepSeek-V3, and Claude Sonnet 4. Experimental results reveal that Claude Sonnet 4 is the best in sentence selection, with 71.1% accuracy using manual transcriptions and 74.7% using automatic transcriptions, and that all LLMs are highly overconfident in their probabilistic predictions, regardless of selecting correct or incorrect sentences in medical dialogues. This dataset is free of charge for non-commercial purposes at: https://huggingface.co/datasets/hcuayahu/MeDial-Speech

</details>


### [222] [Beyond a Single Direction: Chain-of-Thought Disrupts Simple Steering of Refusal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26772)
*Kia-Jüng Yang,Dominik Meier,Jiachen Zhao,Terry Ruas,Bela Gipp*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了大型推理模型（LRM）中拒绝行为的控制机制，发现其不仅依赖于残差流激活，还与链式思维（CoT）密切相关。CoT在维持和重建合规性信号方面起关键作用，使模型对激活层干预更具鲁棒性，但也可能成为新的攻击面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解大型推理模型中拒绝机制的内部动态，尤其是链式思维（CoT）在其中的作用，以提升对模型行为的可控性。

Method: 通过激活引导干预和两阶段CoT重生成实验，分析DeepSeek-R1-Distill-LLaMA-8B模型在不同条件下拒绝行为的变化。

Result: 固定CoT时激活引导仅逆转39%的拒绝行为，移除CoT后提升至70%；两阶段干预下逆转率达94%，且新生成的CoT本身能保留48%的效果。

Conclusion: LRM中的拒绝行为由残差流激活和CoT共同编码，CoT不仅能强化拒绝，还能独立携带和重建合规性信号，这增强了模型鲁棒性但也引入了潜在的表面攻击风险。

Abstract: Large reasoning models (LRMs) generate chain-of-thought (CoT) traces before producing final outputs, introducing a dynamic internal state that may complicate control mechanisms such as refusal. Unlike instruction-tuned LLMs, where refusal is mediated by a single directional subspace, refusal in large reasoning models (LRMs) additionally depends on the CoT. In DeepSeek-R1-Distill-LLaMA-8B, activation steering reverses refusal in only 39% of cases when the CoT is kept fixed, but removing the CoT entirely increases this to 70%, indicating that the CoT actively reinforces refusal. In a two-stage intervention where the model regenerates its CoT under activation steering, refusal is reversed in 94% of cases, while the resulting CoT alone retains 48% of this effect even after steering is removed. This suggests that the CoT can carry and reconstruct the compliance signal independently. These findings indicate that refusal in LRMs is jointly encoded in residual stream activations and CoT. This joint activation makes LRM more robust against activation-level interventions alone, but exposes CoT to a possible alternative surface attack.

</details>


### [223] [The Attribution Blind Spot: Detecting When Language Models Rely on Memory Rather Than Retrieved Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26778)
*Zhe Yu,Wenpeng Xing,Yunzhao Wei,Bo Yang,Chen Ye,Gaolei Li,Meng Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为计算现实监控（CRM）的新方法，用于检测语言模型在生成文本时是否真正依赖于检索到的上下文，而非仅依靠其参数化记忆。该方法揭示了现有归因机制中的“归因盲点”，并通过内部表征差异提供诊断信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于检索增强生成中无法可靠验证模型是否真正基于检索到的上下文生成内容，存在模型仅依赖训练数据记忆而产生看似准确输出的风险，因此需要一种能识别此问题的方法。

Method: 借鉴认知科学中的现实监控框架，提出计算现实监控（CRM），通过比较有无上下文时的语言模型内部表示差异，检测由预训练暴露引起的可测量轨迹特征。

Result: 在跨越三个家族的九个模型变体上验证了该方法的有效性，发现差异集中在特定架构层中，并通过噪声干预实验得到支持，且在不同任务和数据集上具有泛化能力。

Conclusion: 归因盲点是可测量且部分可解决的，模型内部表征包含输出层面无法观察到的诊断信号，为构建能够自我感知证据来源的可靠系统奠定了基础。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation promises to ground language model outputs in external evidence, yet the field has no reliable way to verify whether retrieved context actually governs generation -- a prerequisite for any high-stakes deployment. The standard assumption, that context-consistent output implies context-governed output, breaks when the retrieved document overlaps with the model's pretraining data: the model can produce faithful-looking text entirely from parametric memory, and both pathways yield indistinguishable output. We name this failure the attribution blind spot and introduce Computational Reality Monitoring (CRM) to address it. CRM operationalizes a principle adapted from cognitive science's reality monitoring framework: comparing internal representations with and without context reveals membership-conditioned representational divergence that output-level monitors systematically miss. CRM does not certify which source an individual generation used; it detects whether pretraining exposure leaves a measurable internal trajectory signature, establishing a necessary substrate for source attribution. Across nine model variants spanning three families, this divergence concentrates in architecture-specific layer patterns, receives converging support from block-level noise intervention, and generalizes across tasks and datasets while collapsing on domain-confounded benchmarks. The attribution blind spot is measurable and partially addressable: internal representations carry a diagnostic signal invisible at the output level, establishing a foundation for systems whose internal awareness of evidence provenance governs their external behavior.

</details>


### [224] [LiveK12Bench: Have Large Multimodal Models Truly Conquered High School-level Examinations?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26781)
*Xiaohan Wang,Mingze Yin,Yilin Zhao,Gang Liu,Dian Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LiveK12Bench是一个动态、多学科的基准测试，用于评估大型多模态模型在真实考试环境中的推理能力，揭示了先进模型在实际教育场景中的性能下降和关键缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试多为静态且易受数据污染，难以反映真实考试的复杂性，限制了对大型多模态模型在实际教育应用中推理能力的准确评估。

Method: 构建了一个包含2000多个来自最新真实考试题目的多学科数据集，并设计了自动化流水线以持续更新题目，同时提出“模拟考试”评估机制，从准确性、推理路径的严谨性和效率三方面综合评估模型表现。

Result: 在12个大型多模态模型上的实验表明，当同时考虑推理过程的严谨性和效率时，先进模型性能显著下降，例如GPT-5得分从79降至53；模型对复杂的视觉布局敏感，暴露出与真实教育需求之间的差距。

Conclusion: 当前大型多模态模型在真实考试环境下仍存在明显不足，LiveK12Bench为未来教育导向的模型发展提供了更真实、全面的评估标准。

Abstract: Advanced Large Multimodal Models (LMMs) have demonstrated impressive performance in K-12 reasoning tasks, exhibiting great promise as intelligent tutors. Realizing this potential requires models to navigate real-world examinations effectively, yet most existing benchmarks fail to capture the complexity of authentic testing environments. Specifically, most datasets are static, prone to data contamination, and are often confined to restricted modalities, disciplines, and evaluation criteria. To address these issues, we introduce LiveK12Bench, a dynamic, holistic, multi-disciplinary benchmark designed to evaluate the reasoning abilities of LMMs in realistic examination scenarios. LiveK12Bench comprises 2K+ verified questions spanning Mathematics, Physics, Chemistry, and Biology, sourced from the latest real-world exam papers and designed to grow over time. Our framework features several core innovations: 1) featuring an automated pipeline that continuously ingests and parses the latest examination papers to mitigate data leakage; and 2) proposing a novel `Mock Exam' evaluation scheme, which assesses the ability to complete end-to-end exams autonomously with accurate and efficient reasoning paths. Extensive experiments on 12 LMMs reveal that advanced models suffer substantial performance degradation under exam-realistic constraints: GPT-5's score drops from 79 to 53 (out of 100) when process rigor and efficiency are jointly evaluated. Our findings expose critical vulnerabilities, such as sensitivity to complex visual layouts, highlighting the gap between idealized reasoning capabilities and true educational readiness. Both code and dataset are publicly available.

</details>


### [225] [Composition Collapse: Stable Factual Knowledge Does Not Imply Compositional Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26789)
*Zhe Yu,Wenpeng Xing,Yunzhao Wei,Jie Chen,Hongzhi Wang,Xuyang Teng,Meng Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了在评估多跳推理能力时，仅依赖总体基准分数可能导致误解，提出了一种双门控协议来更准确地衡量组合性失败，并发现训练后目标会以总体指标所掩盖的方式影响组合能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的评估方法将多跳推理视为单一能力，忽略了模型在组合已知事实时可能出现的系统性失败，因此需要更精细的评估手段。

Method: 提出了双门控协议，将估计目标从总体组合性差距转变为条件于稳定原子访问的残差组合失败，分解出原子稳定性、残差组合和关键深度三个独立通道，并在时间事实链基准上进行验证。

Result: 实验显示不同训练方法在原子知识上表现相近，但在组合行为上差异超过40个百分点；双门控协议揭示了训练目标对组合能力的影响被总体指标掩盖，且部分组合失败源于生成时的计算限制而非永久无法组合。

Conclusion: 应使用控制原子门的组合度量来补充总体指标，以更准确评估多跳推理改进，且需关注生成时计算约束对组合性能的影响。

Abstract: Post-training is routinely evaluated through aggregate benchmark scores that treat multi-hop reasoning as a single capability -- as if a model that answers more questions correctly must be better at assembling facts. We show that this assumption can be misleading: recipes with statistically indistinguishable atomic knowledge produce composition behaviour separated by over 40 percentage points, a phenomenon we call composition collapse: the systematic failure to assemble stably-known facts into chains, invisible to aggregate metrics. We introduce a double-gate protocol that changes the estimand from an aggregate compositionality gap to residual composition failure conditioned on stable atomic access, decomposing post-training gains into three independent channels: atomic stability, residual composition, and critical depth. On a benchmark of temporal factual chains spanning depths 2--11 across four post-training recipes, this decomposition reveals that post-training objectives shift composition capability in directions that aggregate metrics mask, and suggests that claims about multi-hop reasoning improvement should be accompanied by atomic-gate-controlled composition metrics. Diagnostic probes further show that a substantial share of measured composition failure reflects generation-time computation constraints rather than permanent inability to compose.

</details>


### [226] [What Makes Chain-of-Thought Work at Probe Time? Local Co-occurrence Rather Than Global Derivation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26795)
*Xiang Wang,Wei Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了链式思维（CoT）提示中促使语言模型性能提升的关键因素，发现即使打乱句子顺序，仅保留局部词元共现也能显著提升效果，表明性能增益主要来自词汇激活和短距离词元共现，而非句子级别的逻辑推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管链式思维提示能有效提升语言模型准确率，但其背后起作用的文本特性尚不明确。本文旨在探究在固定上下文推理文本的前提下，哪些文本特征真正影响了答案生成。

Method: 通过在探针时间（probe-time）分析不同扰动版本的推理文本（如全局词序打乱、保留不同长度的连续词元窗口等），比较其对模型输出的影响，并控制复制答案声明、语法完整性等因素。

Result: 发现全局词序打乱后的推理文本仍显著优于无推理文本基线；保留仅2到3个词元的连续片段即可恢复大部分完整CoT的增益；句子级逻辑顺序并非主要驱动因素。

Conclusion: 链式思维提示的效果主要源于局部词元共现激活（LCA），即词汇激活和短距离词元共现是性能提升的核心机制，而非传统认为的句子级逻辑推导过程。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) prompting reliably improves language-model accuracy, but which properties of a rationale text drive the improvement is poorly understood. Prior work has largely studied generation-time behavior. We instead ask a probe-time question: given a fixed rationale in context, what in that text changes the answer? We identify two complementary sources of the gain. First, even a globally word-shuffled rationale substantially outperforms the no-rationale baseline, indicating a strong lexical activation effect. More importantly, the additional gain from structured text appears to arise less from sentence-level logical ordering and more from short-range token adjacency. Preserving contiguous windows of just $n^\star{=}2$--$3$ tokens recovers most of the remaining gain toward full CoT performance. Supporting experiments rule out copying of explicit answer declarations or answer values, as well as full grammatical realization, as primary drivers. Further generalization experiments show that the qualitative pattern remains stable across multiple model families, parameter scales, and datasets. These results support a local co-occurrence activation (LCA) account of probe-time CoT, in which the observed gains appear to arise primarily from lexical activation and short-range token co-occurrence rather than sentence-level logical derivation.

</details>


### [227] [Multi-Stakeholder LLM Alignment: Decomposing Estimation from Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26878)
*Lulu Zheng,Wenjin Yang,Xiangwen Zhang,Rong Yin,Yulan Hu,Zheng Pan,Xin Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出DecompR方法，通过在评分前根据查询结构固定反事实校准的权重，并独立估计每个角色的效用，以减少多利益相关方任务中的权重漂移和估计噪声。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的整体LLM判断在处理多利益相关方任务时，由于效用估计与效用聚合混淆，导致隐式权重不稳定，特别是在利益相关方满意度分散或数量增加时产生较大的评分波动。

Method: 提出DecompR方法，将权重从候选评分中分离，在评分前基于查询结构设定固定的反事实校准权重，并独立估计各角色的效用，从而消除候选依赖的权重漂移。

Result: 实验证明，DecompR能有效减少因权重噪声引起的评分偏移，尤其在利益相关方数量增多时表现更优。

Conclusion: DecompR通过解耦权重设定与效用估计，提升了多利益相关方任务中LLM判断的稳定性与准确性。

Abstract: Multi-stakeholder tasks require one output to satisfy users with conflicting preferences. Holistic LLM judges conflate utility estimation and utility aggregation, yielding unstable implicit weights. We show empirically and theoretically that this aggregation-specific \emph{weighting noise} can create large score shifts when stakeholder satisfaction is dispersed; in our experiments, these weight-induced shifts also increase with stakeholder count. We propose \textsc{DecompR}: counterfactual-calibrated weights are fixed from query structure before candidate scoring, while per-role utilities are estimated independently, removing candidate-dependent weight drift and reducing estimation noise.

</details>


### [228] [On the Detection of Commutative Factors in Factor Graphs: Necessary and Sufficient Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26908)
*Malte Luttermann,Ralf Möller,Marcel Gehrke*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了识别因子图中可交换因子的现有算法的理论基础，指出现有方法中的一个关键定理仅满足必要条件而非充分条件，可能导致错误结果。作者修正了该定理并提出了改进且正确的算法版本，同时引入了一个具有更优最坏情况界的新算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的检测可交换因子的算法依赖于一个被误认为是充分条件的定理，实际上仅为必要条件，这可能导致不正确的结果，因此需要修正其理论基础并提出正确且高效的算法。

Method: 通过形式化分析现有定理的局限性，提出一个修正版本的定理作为识别可交换因子的必要条件，并在此基础上设计两个算法：一个是保持效率的修正版原算法，另一个是具有更紧最坏情况界的新算法。

Result: 证明了原有定理的不足，提出了修正后的正确版本定理；给出了两个新算法，其中一个修正了现有算法的错误，另一个在理论上具有更好的性能保证。

Conclusion: 本文纠正了当前可交换因子检测算法中的理论缺陷，提供了正确且高效的替代方案，增强了基于提升的概览推理方法在大规模域上的可靠性与适用性。

Abstract: Exploiting the indistinguishability of objects in a probabilistic graphical model such as a factor graph is key to lifted probabilistic inference algorithms and allows for tractable probabilistic inference problems with respect to domain sizes. A central building block for the exploitation of indistinguishable objects in factor graphs is the identification of commutative factors, i.e., factors whose output values are invariant under permutations of input values assigned to a subset of their arguments. In this paper, we revisit the theoretical foundations underlying the state-of-the-art algorithm to detect commutative factors. Specifically, we show that in its current form, the state-of-the-art algorithm relies on a central theorem that is mistakenly regarded as a sufficient condition to identify commutative factors, while it actually only implies necessary condition. Consequently, the state of the art might, as we show in this paper, deliver incorrect results. To fix the flaws currently present in the state of the art, we prove a slightly modified version of the aforementioned theorem, which serves as a necessary condition to identify commutative factors. Moreover, we present a corrected version of the state-of-the-art algorithm, which keeps its efficiency while ensuring correctness and introduce a complementary algorithm with tighter worst-case bounds.

</details>


### [229] [Neuro-Symbolic Verification of LLM Outputs for Data-Sensitive Domains (extended preprint)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26942)
*Paul Sigloch,Christoph Benzmüller*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合形式化符号方法与神经语义分析的混合验证架构，用于提升高风险领域中大语言模型（LLM）生成内容的可靠性，有效检测幻觉并减少报告生成时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高风险应用场景中，LLM存在幻觉、不一致性和隐私漏洞等问题，可能带来法律、财务或安全后果，因此需要更可靠的验证机制。

Method: 采用逻辑推理进行输入验证，利用完备性提供可判定保证；通过基于嵌入的语义相似性检测输出中的上下文幻觉；整体架构为并行的基于行为者（actor-based）流水线。

Result: 在HAIMEDA医疗设备损伤评估系统中验证该架构，对结构化实体的幻觉检测率达83%以上，语义虚构检测率达72%，报告生成时间减少30%。

Conclusion: 神经-符号混合架构能为数据敏感领域的LLM部署提供有原则的安全保障，克服纯提示式自验证方法的局限性。

Abstract: LLMs deployed in high-stakes domains face fundamental reliability challenges: hallucinations, inconsistencies, and privacy vulnerabilities introduce unacceptable risks where errors carry legal, financial, or safety consequences.
